| 13 | thanksgiving | 感謝；感恩節 | 感恩節；感恩；感謝 | 最常見的意思是感恩節；原例句也未使用原形小寫詞。 |
| 1 | great | 大的；很棒的；偉大的；重大的 | 很棒的；大的；偉大的；重大的 | great 最常見的意思是「很棒的」，「大的」應列為補充義。 |
| 1 | much | 許多；非常 | 許多；非常；太多 | much 的主要意思是「許多」，「太多」並非最常見義；例句譯文也應對應。 |
| 1 | something | 某物；某事 | 某事物；某種東西 | 補充義「某事」與主要義重複，且例句譯文漏掉 something「某種東西」的意思。 |
| 4 | solid | 穩固的；固體的；實心的；可靠的 | 固體；固體的；穩固的；實心的 | 詞性含名詞，主要義應為「固體」；穩固的屬形容詞義，應移至補充義。 |
| 4 | rush | 催促；急忙；衝；高峰期 | 匆忙；催促；衝；高峰期 | 最常見義為「匆忙」；「催促」屬次要及物義，應移至補充義。 |
| 4 | bread | 麵包；生計 | 麵包 | 「生計」為罕見用法，且 vt 義（裹麵包粉）不符，應刪除。 |
| 3 | victory | 獲勝；克服 | 勝利；成功；戰勝 | 「獲勝」是動詞性說法，名詞應為「勝利」；「克服」並非 victory 的常見意思。 |
| 3 | session | 活動；會議；一段時間；學期 | 一段時間；會議；學期；開庭 | session 主要指從事某活動的一段時間，「活動」不是最常見的意思。 |
| 3 | essential | 必要的；要素；本質的 | 必要的；要素；必需品 | 「本質的」與 essential 的常用義不同；譯文「泡奶粉」不自然且漏譯 essential。 |
| 3 | copy | 抄下；複製；副本；一本 | 複製；抄寫；影印；副本 | 動詞 copy 最常見意思是「複製」，「抄下」應放在補充義；「一本」不是常見釋義。 |
| 3 | gather | 採集；聚集；推測 | 聚集；採集；推測 | gather 最常見的意思是「聚集」，「採集」應放在補充義。 |
| 3 | jump | 跳；暴漲 | 跳；驟升 | 「暴漲」偏口語且只限價格，「驟升」較通用。 |
| 3 | writing | 文字；寫作；筆跡；作品 | 寫作；文字；筆跡；作品 | writing 最常見的意思是「寫作」；例句中應譯為「作品」。 |
| 8 | initiate | 開始；發起；入門者 | 發起；開始；使入門；新加入者 | 主要詞義應為「發起」；「入門者」不夠準確；例句譯文「開始」不自然。 |
| 8 | basket | 籃子；一籃 | 籃子；一籃；籃框 | 補充詞義「一籃」非獨立詞義，宜改為較常見詞義；譯文「一只」不自然。 |
| 8 | sue | 提告；請求 | 控告；提起訴訟 | 「請求」為罕見詞義，應移除；主要詞義用「控告」較標準。 |
| 8 | accounting | 會計 | 會計；帳目；會計學 | 例句語意不自然：查帳無法發現箱內硬幣，與詞義不符。 |
| 5 | revolution | 旋轉一圈；革命；重大變革；循環 | 革命；旋轉一圈；重大變革；循環 | 最常用的意思是「革命」，「旋轉一圈」應放在補充意思。 |
| 5 | philosophy | 人生觀；哲學；基本理念 | 哲學；人生觀；基本理念 | 最常見的意思是「哲學」，不是「人生觀」。 |
| 5 | tower | 塔樓；高樓；高聳 | 塔；塔樓；高聳 | 主要意思應為「塔」，補充意思「高塔」與主義重複。 |
| 5 | preparation | 準備；預習 | 準備；預備工作；籌備 | 「預習」不是 preparation 的常見意思，應刪除。 |
| 5 | sand | 沙；沙灘；撒沙 | 沙；沙灘；用砂紙磨 | 「撒沙」不是 sand 動詞的常見意思，應為用砂紙磨；譯文漏掉 our。 |
| 5 | jacket | 外套；護套 | 外套；夾克；書套 | 「護套」不自然，常見補充意思為夾克與書套。 |
| 5 | gentle | 輕柔的；溫和的 | 溫和的；輕柔的；溫柔的 | 最常見詞義為「溫和的」，「輕柔的」應為補充；原譯文逗號亦不自然。 |
| 5 | plain | 無調味的；簡單的；清楚的；平原 | 簡單的；無調味的；清楚的；平原 | 主要詞義應為「簡單的」，「無調味的」並非最常見詞義。 |
| 5 | bath | 洗澡；浴缸 | 洗澡；浴缸；浴室 | 例句 warm bath 為泡澡，補充詞義「浴室」較常見於 bathroom，建議調整。 |
| 10 | conquer | 克服；征服；戰勝 | 征服；克服；戰勝 | conquer 最常見的意思是「征服」，「克服」應列為補充義，例句也需配合主要詞義。 |
| 10 | definitive | 決定性的；明確的；最終的 | 最終的；決定性的；明確的 | definitive 最常見義為「最終的」；詞性標示 n 也不恰當，但主要義需修正。 |
| 10 | wholesale | 以批發方式；批發的；大規模的 | 批發的；以批發方式；大規模的 | 主要詞義應為形容詞或名詞的「批發的」，副詞義應放在補充義。 |
| 10 | mediate | 調解；居中 | 調解；居中調停；斡旋 | 補充義「居中」不完整，不是自然的詞義，應改為完整詞語。 |
| 6 | excess | 過量的；過度；超額 | 過量；過度；超額 | 詞性含名詞，主要詞義應為名詞「過量」，不應用形容詞「過量的」。 |
| 6 | renew | 換發；更新；續約；恢復 | 更新；續約；恢復；換發 | renew 最常見詞義是「更新」，「換發」過於特定，應移到補充義。 |
| 6 | amateur | 業餘的；業餘愛好者；外行 | 業餘愛好者；外行人；業餘的 | 詞性為名詞，meaningZh 不應是形容詞「業餘的」，應為「業餘愛好者」。 |
| 6 | exile | 流放；流亡者 | 流亡；流放；被放逐者 | 例句中 exile 為「流亡」，且「流亡者」並非最常見的補充義，已調整詞義。 |
| 10 | wicked | 壞的；邪惡的；淘氣的；很棒的 | 邪惡的；淘氣的；很棒的 | wicked 最常見的意思是邪惡的，「壞的」過於籠統，故修正主要詞義。 |
| 10 | infinite | 無窮無盡的；極大的 | 無限的；極大的；無數的 | 例句未使用 infinite，且用法不自然；已改為包含該字的句子。 |
| 10 | regiment | 團；大量；嚴格管制 | 團；大量 | 名詞 regiment 的補充義「嚴格管制」是動詞義，不符詞性，應刪除。 |
| 11 | lag | 延遲；落後 | 落後；延遲 | 動詞 lag 最常見義為落後，延遲為補充；譯文漏譯 behind 的意思。 |
| 11 | contingent | 隊伍；代表團；視情況而定的 | 視情況而定的；代表團；分遣隊 | 詞性為形容詞與名詞，主要義應為視情況而定的，且例句需與詞義相符。 |
| 11 | savage | 殘酷的；兇猛攻擊 | 殘酷的；兇猛攻擊；野蠻的 | 補充義「兇猛攻擊」不自然應改為動詞義，譯文漏冠詞語氣不自然。 |
| 11 | pilgrim | 朝聖者；旅人 | 朝聖者 | 「旅人」為罕見補充義，應刪除。 |
| 1 | follow | 沿著走；跟隨；遵循；接著 | 跟隨；遵循；沿著走；接著 | 最常見的意思是「跟隨」，「沿著走」應放在補充意思，例句也配合主要意思。 |
| 1 | power | 電力；力量；權力；動力 | 力量；權力；電力；動力 | 最常見的意思是「力量」而非「電力」，例句也配合主要意思。 |
| 1 | study | 研究；學習；讀書；書房 | 學習；研究；讀書；書房 | 對高中生而言最常見的意思是「學習」，「研究」較不常見，應放在補充意思。 |
| 11 | sovereign | 有主權的；主權國家；君主 | 有主權的；君主；元首；獨立自主的 | 補充義「主權國家」與例句用法重複，改為君主、元首等常見意義。 |
| 6 | organized | 整齊的；有組織的；安排好的 | 有組織的；井然有序的；安排好的 | 主要詞義應為「有組織的」，「整齊的」並非最常見義項。 |
| 6 | racing | 賽車；賽馬；競速 | 賽車；賽馬；競賽 | 「競速」用詞不自然，改為「競賽」；其餘無誤。 |
| 6 | prime | 絕佳的；主要的；全盛期 | 首要的；絕佳的；全盛期；質數 | 主要詞義應為「首要的」，「絕佳的」宜列為補充義。 |
| 6 | provision | 條款；規定；供應；準備 | 供應；條款；準備；糧食 | 最常見義為「供應」，「條款」較少見；例句也配合修正。 |
| 12 | outdoors | 在戶外；戶外活動；野外 | 在戶外；戶外；野外 | 詞性含名詞，補充義「戶外活動」不精確；例句中譯「味道更好」不自然。 |
| 12 | beak | 鳥嘴；喙 | 鳥嘴 | 補充義「喙」與主要義重複，且例句中譯漏掉「那隻鳥的」。 |

## Examples

### Core 7 · attraction

- Before: The night market's main attraction is a century-old tofu stall. 那家百年豆腐攤，是夜市最吸引人的攤位。
- After: The night market's main attraction is a century-old tofu stall. 夜市最吸引人的景點是一家百年豆腐攤。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「吸引力」，「賣點」不當；例句中譯也不自然。

### Core 7 · reservation

- Before: Our reservation at the hot spring inn includes breakfast. 我們的溫泉旅館訂房包含早餐。
- After: Our reservation at the hot spring inn includes breakfast. 我們在溫泉旅館的預訂包含早餐。
- Why: 補充詞義「訂房」與主要詞義重複，已移除並調整中譯。

### Core 7 · dirt

- Before: Fine dirt from the vegetable plot covered his sandals. 菜園細土沾滿他的涼鞋。
- After: Fine dirt from the vegetable plot covered his sandals. 菜園的細泥土沾滿了他的涼鞋。
- Why: 「污垢」用字應為台灣常用的「髒污」，中譯也較不自然。

### Core 11 · cavalry

- Before: The cavalry crossed the plain before dust hid them. 騎兵穿越平原後被塵土遮住。
- After: The cavalry crossed the plain before the dust hid them from view. 騎兵部隊穿越平原後，身影便被塵土遮住了。
- Why: 例句與翻譯語意不順，原譯文未清楚表達塵土遮住騎兵的先後關係。

### Core 11 · dismay

- Before: Dismay crossed his face when the violin string snapped. 小提琴弦斷時，他臉上露出沮喪。
- After: Dismay crossed his face when the violin string snapped. 小提琴弦突然斷掉時，他臉上閃過驚愕。
- Why: dismay 主要意思為驚愕，沮喪較次要，主要詞義與例句翻譯不精確。

### Core 11 · flap

- Before: The torn awning began to flap above the fruit stand. 水果攤上方的破遮雨棚開始啪啪作響。
- After: The bird began to flap its wings above the fruit stand. 那隻鳥開始在水果攤上方拍動翅膀。
- Why: 例句中的 flap 詞義是啪啪作響，與主要詞義拍動不一致，需改用拍動義。

### Core 13 · deed

- Before: The lawyer found the deed folded inside a biscuit tin. 律師在餅乾鐵盒裡找到折好的契約。
- After: Helping the lost child find her mother was a kind deed. 幫助迷路的小孩找到媽媽是一件善行。
- Why: deed 最常見的意思是行為，契約較少見，應調整主要詞義與例句。

### Core 13 · consistency

- Before: The soup reached the perfect consistency after ten more minutes. 再煮十分鐘後，湯達到剛好的濃稠度。
- After: Consistency is important if you want to improve your English. 想提升英文的話，持之以恆很重要。
- Why: consistency 最常見的意思是一致性，濃稠度較次要，應對調並更換例句。

### Core 12 · stuck

- Before: The stuck drawer finally opened after Mira pulled the handle hard. 米拉用力拉把手後，卡住的抽屜終於打開。
- After: The stuck drawer finally opened after Mira pulled the handle hard. 米拉用力拉把手後，卡住的抽屜終於打開了。
- Why: 詞性標示 word 不正確，應為形容詞 a；其餘內容無誤

### Core 12 · tricky

- Before: The tricky salesman hid the extra fee until signing. 那個狡猾業務把額外費用藏到簽約時才說。
- After: The tricky math problem took Mira almost an hour to solve. 這道棘手的數學題花了米拉將近一小時才解出來。
- Why: tricky 最常見意思是棘手的，狡猾的較次要，例句也應配合主要詞義

### Core 12 · unwanted

- Before: Several unwanted weeds grew between the tiles after spring rain. 春雨過後，幾株多餘雜草從磁磚縫長出。
- After: Several unwanted weeds grew between the tiles after spring rain. 春雨過後，幾株不想要的雜草從磁磚縫長出來。
- Why: unwanted 最常見意思是不想要的，多餘的應為補充義

### Core 12 · striker

- Before: The striker carried a sign outside the factory gate. 那名罷工者在工廠門外舉著標語。
- After: The striker scored twice before the school team won the match. 那名前鋒進了兩球，學校球隊贏得了比賽。
- Why: striker 最常見意思是足球前鋒，罷工者較次要，例句也需配合

### Core 12 · binding

- Before: The notebook's red binding cracked after years of travel. 那本筆記本紅色裝訂經多年旅行後裂開。
- After: The contract is binding, so both sides must follow every rule. 這份合約具有約束力，所以雙方都必須遵守每項規定。
- Why: 詞性為名詞但主要義與詞性不符，且例句詞義需與主要義一致

### Core 10 · thoughtful

- Before: Her thoughtful note arrived on the anniversary of his loss. 她體貼的紙條在他喪親周年那天送達。
- After: Her thoughtful note arrived on the anniversary of his loss. 她體貼的紙條在他喪親週年那天送達。
- Why: 「周年」宜寫作「週年」，台灣用字較標準。

### Core 10 · underestimate

- Before: Do not underestimate the climb behind the waterfall. 別低估瀑布後方那段攀爬。
- After: Do not underestimate the climb behind the waterfall. 別低估瀑布後方那段攀爬路程。
- Why: 詞性應以動詞為主，譯文「攀爬」不自然，需補足。

### Core 10 · hike

- Before: We took a hike through bamboo groves before breakfast. 早餐前我們穿過竹林健行。
- After: We took a hike through bamboo groves before breakfast. 早餐前我們去竹林健行了一趟。
- Why: 原譯文語意不夠自然，應表達健行一趟。

### Core 12 · advisory

- Before: An advisory notice warned swimmers about strong currents near the pier. 一則警示公告提醒泳客碼頭旁有強勁海流。
- After: She joined the advisory board to help the school choose new clubs. 她加入顧問委員會，協助學校挑選新社團。
- Why: advisory 作形容詞最常見為顧問或諮詢的，警示的並非主要意義。

### Core 12 · philosophical

- Before: Nora sounded philosophical after the storm ruined her picnic. 暴風雨毀了野餐後，諾拉聽起來很豁達。
- After: The class had a philosophical discussion about what makes people happy. 全班針對什麼讓人快樂進行了一場哲學討論。
- Why: philosophical 最常見意義為哲學的，豁達的屬次要意義。

### Core 12 · pro

- Before: Students argued the new phone rule pro and con before voting. 投票前，學生從正反兩面辯論新的手機規定。
- After: The pro at the golf club gave Leo some useful tips. 高爾夫球場的專業球員給了里奧一些實用的建議。
- Why: 詞性標示 adv 不當，pro 最常見為名詞，且原例句不自然。

### Core 12 · bastard

- Before: In the old play, the king's bastard son dreamed of taking the throne. 在那齣古老的戲劇裡，國王的私生子一心想奪取王位。
- After: The angry driver called the other man a bastard. 那位憤怒的駕駛罵另一個男人是混蛋。
- Why: bastard 主要為名詞，原釋義「該死的」並非常見意義，例句也不自然。

### Core 12 · huh

- Before: Huh? I thought the math test was tomorrow, not today. 蛤？我還以為數學考試是明天，不是今天。
- After: Huh, I thought the test was tomorrow, not today. 蛤，我還以為考試是明天，不是今天。
- Why: huh 最常見表示疑惑的「蛤」，原釋義「哼」不當，例句也不符。

### Core 3 · prospect

- Before: The prospect of working abroad excites many young engineers in Taiwan. 出國工作的前景讓台灣許多年輕工程師感到興奮。
- After: The prospect of studying abroad made her both excited and nervous. 出國留學的前景讓她既興奮又緊張。
- Why: 最常見的詞義是「前景」，「景色」較少用，應移到補充意義。

### Core 3 · secure

- Before: Please secure the windows before the storm arrives. 暴風雨來前請固定窗戶。
- After: Please make sure the windows are secure before the storm arrives. 暴風雨來臨前，請確認窗戶都已關緊。
- Why: 詞性首位為形容詞，最常見詞義應為「安全的」，「固定」為動詞用法。

### Core 3 · sharp

- Before: A sharp knife sliced the papaya cleanly into strips. 鋒利的刀子俐落地把木瓜切成條。
- After: A sharp knife sliced the papaya cleanly into thin strips. 鋒利的刀子俐落地把木瓜切成細條。
- Why: 此項無明顯錯誤，但例句翻譯略有疏漏。

### Core 3 · weak

- Before: The weak bridge shook under the loaded truck. 脆弱的橋在滿載卡車下搖晃。
- After: The weak bridge shook under the loaded truck. 那座脆弱的橋在滿載的卡車下搖晃。
- Why: weak 最常見詞義為「虛弱的」，「脆弱的」應列為補充。

### Core 3 · decline

- Before: Tourist numbers decline sharply when ferry prices rise in summer. 夏天渡輪票價上漲時遊客數會大幅減少。
- After: Tourist numbers often decline when ferry prices rise in summer. 夏天渡輪票價上漲時，遊客人數常會減少。
- Why: 原例句翻譯「大幅」與 sharply 對應不當，已修正並保持自然。

### Core 12 · equalize

- Before: The late goal helped Spain equalize before extra time. 那記晚進球幫西班牙在延長賽前追平。
- After: The late goal helped Spain equalize before extra time. 那記晚進球幫西班牙在延長賽前追平比分。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「使相等」，「追平」為體育情境的次要用法，故調換。

### Core 2 · outside

- Before: No one waited outside after the temple bell stopped ringing. 廟鐘停響後外面沒有人等著。
- After: No one waited outside after the temple bell stopped ringing. 廟鐘停響後，外面沒有人在等待。
- Why: meaningZh 與詞性不符，名詞應為「外面」，且補充義重複。

### Core 2 · attack

- Before: Geese may attack people who walk too close to their nests. 鵝可能會攻擊太靠近巢的人。
- After: Mosquitoes attack bare ankles near the pond after sunset. 日落後，池塘邊的蚊子會叮咬裸露的腳踝。
- Why: 最常見意思是「攻擊」而非「叮咬」；原中文翻譯也不夠自然。

### Core 2 · visit

- Before: We plan to visit our grandparents in Hualien this weekend. 我們打算這個週末去花蓮拜訪爺爺奶奶。
- After: We plan to visit the lantern market before it gets crowded. 我們打算在人潮變多前去參觀燈籠市集。
- Why: 「去逛」不是最常見的主要意思，應為「拜訪」。

### Core 2 · sister

- Before: My sister fixes bicycles behind the family tea shop. 我姊姊在家裡茶行後面修腳踏車。
- After: My sister fixes bicycles behind the family tea shop. 我姐姐在家裡茶行後面修腳踏車。
- Why: sister 不分長幼，主要意思應為「姊妹」；且不應有 vt 詞性意義。

### Core 5 · unknown

- Before: An unknown number kept calling during the family dinner. 晚餐時陌生號碼一直打來。
- After: An unknown number kept calling during the family dinner. 全家吃晚餐時，一通陌生號碼一直打來。
- Why: 最常見的意思是「未知的」，「陌生的」應放在補充意思；譯文漏掉「全家」。

### Core 4 · grab

- Before: Grab your raincoat before the clouds reach us. 烏雲到達前先抓起你的雨衣。
- After: Grab your raincoat before the clouds reach us. 趁烏雲飄到我們這裡之前，先拿起你的雨衣。
- Why: exampleZh 語意不順，「烏雲到達前」不自然；meaningZh 改為較常用的「抓住」。

### Core 4 · tank

- Before: The water tank cracked during the strong earthquake. 水塔在強震中裂開。
- After: The water tank cracked during the strong earthquake. 水箱在強震中裂開了。
- Why: meaningZh「儲槽」不是最常見的意思；exampleZh 把 water tank 誤譯為水塔。

### Core 4 · angry

- Before: The angry customer returned with the cracked phone case. 生氣的顧客拿著裂開手機殼回來。
- After: The angry customer returned with the cracked phone case. 生氣的顧客拿著裂開的手機殼回來了。
- Why: exampleZh 缺少「的」，讀起來不自然。

### Core 4 · sick

- Before: The sick child slept through the temple fireworks. 生病的孩子在廟會煙火中睡著。
- After: The sick child slept through the temple fireworks. 生病的孩子在廟會煙火聲中睡著了。
- Why: 詞性標示 vt 有誤；exampleZh 稍欠自然。

### Core 4 · pupil

- Before: The pupil widened when the doctor shone a light. 醫師照光時，瞳孔放大了。
- After: The pupil raised her hand to answer the teacher's question. 那位學生舉手回答老師的問題。
- Why: pupil 最常見的意思是「學生」，「瞳孔」應放在補充義，例句也需配合主要意義。

### Core 4 · intelligence

- Before: Border officers used intelligence to stop the smuggling boat. 邊境人員靠情報攔下走私船。
- After: Her intelligence helped her solve the difficult math problem quickly. 她的智力讓她很快解開了那道困難的數學題。
- Why: intelligence 最常見的意思是「智力」，「情報」較不常見，應調整順序與例句。

### Core 4 · fairly

- Before: The judge divided the prize money fairly among the dancers. 評審把獎金公平分給舞者。
- After: The test was fairly easy, so most students finished early. 這次考試相當簡單，所以大多數學生都提早完成。
- Why: fairly 在高中最常用的意思是「相當地」，「公平地」為次要義，例句也需對應。

### Core 12 · superpower

- Before: The superpower sent two carriers into the disputed sea. 那個超級大國派兩艘航母進入爭議海域。
- After: The superpower sent two carriers into the disputed sea. 那個超級大國派兩艘航空母艦進入爭議海域。
- Why: 「航母」為中國大陸用語，台灣應說「航空母艦」。

### Core 12 · disturbed

- Before: Mia felt disturbed after watching the news about the earthquake. 看完地震的新聞後，米亞心裡很不安。
- After: The disturbed child could not sleep after the loud storm. 那個不安的孩子在巨大的暴風雨後無法入睡。
- Why: disturbed 最常見意思是不安的；原例句語意為混亂，與常用義不符。

### Core 12 · sub

- Before: A sub replaced the injured goalkeeper after halftime. 一名替補在中場後取代受傷守門員。
- After: A sub replaced the injured goalkeeper after halftime. 一名替補球員在中場休息後替換了受傷的守門員。
- Why: 訂戶不是 sub 常見義；替補應說明為球員，譯文也應完整。

### Core 10 · prestigious

- Before: She rejected a prestigious award to protest the sponsor. 她拒領那個聲望崇高的獎項，以抗議贊助商。
- After: She rejected a prestigious award to protest the sponsor. 她拒絕了一個享有盛名的獎項，以抗議贊助商。
- Why: 中文翻譯未忠實對應 rejected，且「拒領」與原句語意略有出入。

### Core 2 · language

- Before: Hakka is the language Grandma uses when she sings to the baby. 客家話是奶奶唱歌給寶寶聽時用的語言。
- After: Their language changes when relatives gather for New Year. 親戚過年團聚時，他們說的語言會改變。
- Why: meaningZh 應為最常見的「語言」，「用語」不是主要意義，例句翻譯也需配合。

### Core 2 · guy

- Before: That guy sells roasted chestnuts outside the cinema. 那個人在電影院外賣烤栗子。
- After: That guy sells roasted chestnuts outside the cinema. 那個傢伙在電影院外賣烤栗子。
- Why: guy 最常見的意思是「傢伙」，且例句翻譯漏掉 guy 的對應詞「男人」。

### Core 6 · yen

- Before: The ticket cost five thousand yen at the counter. 櫃台那張票要五千日圓。
- After: The ticket cost five thousand yen at the counter. 那張票在櫃檯要價五千日圓。
- Why: 「櫃台」應為台灣常用寫法「櫃檯」，且原譯文語序不自然。

### Core 6 · victimize

- Before: Scammers often victimize older adults with official-looking tax letters. 詐騙者常用看似官方的稅務信件，誘使年長者受騙。
- After: Scammers often victimize older adults with official-looking tax letters. 詐騙者常用看似官方的稅務信件，欺騙並傷害年長者。
- Why: 譯文「誘使受騙」未完整表達 victimize 使人受害之意，宜修正。

### Core 6 · associated

- Before: Headaches are often associated with too little sleep and too much screen time. 頭痛常與睡眠不足和長時間盯著螢幕相關。
- After: Three associated clinics share one emergency hotline in the valley. 三間聯合診所在山谷共用一支緊急專線。
- Why: associated 最常見意思為「相關的」，且「熱線」為大陸用語，應改為「專線」。

### Core 11 · blossom

- Before: Plum trees blossom beside the temple every February. 每年二月，寺旁李樹都會開花。
- After: Plum trees blossom beside the temple every February. 每年二月，寺旁的李樹都會開花。
- Why: 補充義「花」過於簡略，改為「花朵」，譯文也稍作順暢化。

### Core 14 · defective

- Before: A defective brake light made the scooter unsafe. 這輛機車的煞車燈故障，騎上路很危險。
- After: A defective brake light made the scooter unsafe. 有瑕疵的煞車燈讓這輛機車騎起來不安全。
- Why: 譯文把 defective 譯為故障且添加「騎上路」，與原句不完全對應。

### Core 1 · across

- Before: The bank is across the street from the clinic. 銀行就在診所的街道對面。
- After: We walked across the bridge to reach the night market. 我們走過那座橋前往夜市。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「橫越」，原譯「在對面」較不常用，例句中文也不自然。

### Core 1 · sense

- Before: This answer makes sense after the diagram appears. 圖示出現後這個答案說得通。
- After: She has a good sense of direction in the city. 她在城市裡的方向感很好。
- Why: 最常見詞義是「感覺」而非「意義」，故調整主要詞義並改寫例句。

### Core 1 · die

- Before: Many herbs die if the soil stays wet. 土壤一直潮濕時，許多香草會枯死。
- After: Many herbs die if the soil stays wet. 土壤一直潮濕時，許多香草會死亡。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「死亡」，「枯死」只是特定用法。

### Core 6 · roach

- Before: A roach ran across the kitchen floor when I turned on the light. 我一開燈，就有一隻蟑螂從廚房地板上跑過去。
- After: A roach ran across the kitchen floor when I turned on the light. 我一開燈，一隻蟑螂就從廚房地板上跑過去。
- Why: roach 最常見的意思是蟑螂，斜齒鯿很罕見，主要與補充義顛倒。

### Core 6 · done

- Before: The dumplings are done when they float to the top. 水餃浮上水面時就煮好了。
- After: My homework is done, so I can watch TV now. 我的功課寫完了，所以現在可以看電視。
- Why: done 作形容詞最常見的意思是完成的，熟的應作補充義。

### Core 6 · last

- Before: These batteries should last through the lantern parade. 這些電池應該能撐完整場燈籠遊行。
- After: This is the last bus to the station tonight. 這是今晚開往車站的最後一班公車。
- Why: last 最常見的意思是最後的，夠用不是最常見義，且補充義有重複。

### Core 6 · case

- Before: This case holds six bottles of winter plum wine. 這個箱子裝著六瓶冬梅酒。
- After: In that case, we should take the earlier train. 那樣的話，我們應該搭早一點的火車。
- Why: case 最常見的意思是情況，箱子不是最常見義。

### Core 6 · place

- Before: Please place the lanterns beside the north gate. 請把燈籠放在北門旁。
- After: This is a quiet place to study after school. 這是個放學後適合念書的安靜地方。
- Why: place 最常見的意思是名詞的地方，放置是動詞義。

### Core 6 · long

- Before: I long for the quiet harbor of my childhood. 我渴望童年那座安靜港口。
- After: The line for bubble tea was very long today. 今天買珍珠奶茶的隊伍非常長。
- Why: long 最常見的意思是長的，渴望不是最常見義。

### Core 6 · present

- Before: Every volunteer was present when the trucks arrived. 卡車抵達時，每位志工都在場。
- After: She gave me a present for my birthday yesterday. 她昨天送了我一份生日禮物。
- Why: present 最常見的意思是禮物，在場的不是首要義。

### Core 5 · rough

- Before: The rough blanket scratched Nora's chin during the cold night. 寒夜裡粗毯子刮著諾拉下巴。
- After: The rough blanket scratched Nora's chin during the cold night. 寒冷的夜裡，粗糙的毯子刮得諾拉的下巴很不舒服。
- Why: exampleZh 語句不自然，「粗毯子刮著下巴」過於簡略，漏譯 scratched 的語氣。

### Core 14 · eradicate

- Before: Inspectors hope to eradicate mosquitoes from the storage yard. 稽查員希望徹底根除倉庫院子裡的蚊子。
- After: Inspectors hope to eradicate mosquitoes from the storage yard. 稽查員希望根除倉庫院子裡的蚊子。
- Why: exampleZh 將 eradicate 譯成「清除」，與 meaningZh「根除」不一致。

### Core 12 · spelling

- Before: The sign's spelling changed after the painter returned. 油漆工回來後，修正了招牌上的拼字。
- After: The sign's spelling changed after the painter returned. 油漆工回來後，招牌上的拼字變了。
- Why: 例句說拼字改變，中文卻譯成「修正」，與原句意思不符。

### Core 12 · nephew

- Before: My nephew hid warm dumplings in his lunchbox. 我的外甥把熱水餃藏在午餐盒裡。
- After: My nephew hid warm dumplings in his lunchbox. 我的姪子把熱水餃藏在午餐盒裡。
- Why: nephew 最常見的意思是姪子，外甥應放在補充意思。

### Core 12 · motorcycle

- Before: A motorcycle splashed mud onto the shop window. 一輛摩托車把泥水濺到店窗上。
- After: A motorcycle splashed mud onto the shop window. 一輛機車把泥水濺到店窗上。
- Why: 台灣最常用「機車」，「摩托車」較屬大陸用語，應調換。

### Core 8 · scatter

- Before: Wind will scatter the receipts if you open that drawer. 你打開抽屜的話，風會吹散那些收據。
- After: The wind will scatter the receipts if you open that window. 如果你打開那扇窗，風會把收據吹散。
- Why: 原例句抽屜裡的收據不會被風吹散，情境不合理，已改寫。

### Core 8 · equip

- Before: The rescue team will equip each seat on the boat with a flashlight. 救援隊會為船上每個座位配備手電筒。
- After: The school will equip every classroom with a new projector. 學校會為每間教室配備一台新的投影機。
- Why: 原例句主語救援船配備座位手電筒不自然，已改寫。

### Core 8 · handsome

- Before: A handsome reward was offered for the missing necklace. 為了找回遺失項鍊，有人提供可觀賞金。
- After: The handsome actor smiled at the fans outside the theater. 那位英俊的演員對劇院外的粉絲微笑。
- Why: 最常見的意思是英俊的，不是可觀的，已調整主要詞義與例句。

### Core 8 · imperial

- Before: Old imperial seals decorated the museum's crimson gate. 古老帝王印記裝飾著博物館的深紅大門。
- After: The imperial palace attracts thousands of visitors every year. 這座帝國皇宮每年吸引數千名遊客。
- Why: imperial 最常見意思是帝國的，已調整詞義順序並簡化例句。

### Core 12 · lawmaker

- Before: A lawmaker proposed stricter rules for basement rentals. 一名立法者提議加嚴地下室出租規定。
- After: A lawmaker proposed stricter rules for basement rentals. 一名立法委員提議加嚴地下室出租規定。
- Why: lawmaker 最常見意思為立法委員或議員，「立法者」較少用，譯文不自然。

### Core 12 · scattered

- Before: The scattered sandals lay outside the crowded temple gate after prayers. 祈禱結束後，涼鞋散落在擁擠的廟門外。
- After: The scattered sandals lay outside the crowded temple gate after prayers. 祈禱結束後，散落的涼鞋躺在擁擠的廟門外。
- Why: exampleZh 漏譯 scattered 的修飾語氣，且「涼鞋散落」稍不完整，應譯出「散落的涼鞋」。

### Core 2 · movement

- Before: Sudden movement in the bushes startled the hikers. 樹叢裡突然有東西移動，嚇到了登山客。
- After: Sudden movement in the bushes startled the hikers. 樹叢裡突然的動靜嚇到了登山客。
- Why: meaningZh 「動靜」不是最常見的意思，應為「動作」，已調整。

### Core 2 · private

- Before: The clinic keeps private records in locked cabinets. 診所把私人病歷鎖在櫃子裡。
- After: The clinic keeps private records in locked cabinets. 診所把私人紀錄鎖在櫃子裡。
- Why: 主要意思應為「私人的」，原「個人的」不是最常見的詞義。

### Core 6 · assembly

- Before: The assembly of the wooden puppet took three evenings. 組裝那尊木偶花了三個晚上。
- After: The whole school gathered in the hall for the Monday morning assembly. 全校師生星期一早上聚集在禮堂參加集會。
- Why: assembly 最常見的意思是集會，組裝應列為補充，例句也需配合主要詞義。

### Core 6 · solicitor

- Before: The solicitor explained the inheritance papers beside the stove. 律師在爐邊解釋繼承文件。
- After: The solicitor explained the inheritance papers beside the stove. 事務律師在爐邊解釋繼承文件。
- Why: solicitor 指英國的事務律師，並非泛稱律師；補充義也需調整。

### Core 6 · bare

- Before: Long bare branches scratched the roof during the typhoon. 光禿樹枝在颱風中刮著屋頂。
- After: Long bare branches scratched the roof during the typhoon. 光禿的長樹枝在颱風中刮著屋頂。
- Why: bare 最常見義為赤裸的；例句翻譯漏掉 Long 且不自然。

### Core 6 · jet

- Before: A private jet crossed the moon above the runway. 一架私人噴射機掠過跑道上空，從月亮前方飛過。
- After: A private jet crossed the moon above the runway. 一架私人噴射機從跑道上空的月亮前方飛過。
- Why: 例句 crossed the moon 不自然，且中文翻譯也不通順。

### Core 9 · betray

- Before: She would never betray her teammates to win the game. 她絕不會為了贏得比賽而背叛隊友。
- After: His shaking voice seemed to betray his fear. 他顫抖的聲音似乎流露出他的恐懼。
- Why: betray最常見的意思是「背叛」，「暴露」應放到補充意義並改為「流露」。

### Core 9 · boxing

- Before: His boxing taught him patience as well as strength. 拳擊不只讓他變強壯，也教會他耐心。
- After: Boxing taught him patience as well as strength. 拳擊不只讓他變強壯，也教會他耐心。
- Why: boxing作「拳擊」時為不可數名詞，前面加his不自然。

### Core 9 · homeland

- Before: Songs from her homeland made the room quiet. 故鄉的歌曲一響起，房間裡頓時安靜下來。
- After: Songs from her homeland made the room quiet. 來自她祖國的歌曲讓房間安靜下來。
- Why: 譯文把homeland譯成「故鄉」與詞義不符，且與例句不完全對應。

### Core 14 · obstruction

- Before: A fallen sign caused an obstruction on the bike lane. 倒下的招牌造成自行車道障礙。
- After: A fallen sign caused an obstruction on the bike lane. 倒下的招牌在自行車道上造成阻礙。
- Why: 例句中譯「造成自行車道障礙」不自然，且主要詞義宜為阻礙物。

### Core 14 · capsule

- Before: The capsule brought rock samples back from the asteroid. 太空艙從小行星帶回岩石樣本。
- After: The doctor told me to take one capsule after dinner. 醫生要我晚餐後服用一顆膠囊。
- Why: 最常見詞義是膠囊而非太空艙，應調整主要詞義與例句。

### Core 2 · dog

- Before: The dog waited outside the noodle shop patiently. 那隻狗耐心等在麵店外。
- After: The dog waited patiently outside the noodle shop for its owner. 那隻狗耐心地在麵店外等主人。
- Why: 例句缺少完整性且英文語序不夠自然，中文漏譯主人；已修正。

### Core 2 · wonder

- Before: I wonder why the crab crossed the road. 我想知道螃蟹為何過馬路。
- After: I wonder why the crab crossed the road. 我想知道螃蟹為什麼要過馬路。
- Why: 中文翻譯略去「為什麼要」的語氣，不夠自然；已修正。

### Core 3 · telephone

- Before: Please telephone Aunt Lin before visiting the farm tomorrow. 明天去農場前請先打電話給林阿姨。
- After: The telephone rang while we were eating dinner last night. 昨晚我們吃晚餐時，電話響了。
- Why: 最常見的意思是名詞「電話」，應作為主要詞義；例句也須配合。

### Core 10 · bark

- Before: The old tree’s bark peeled away in long strips. 老樹樹皮一長條一長條剝落。
- After: The dog began to bark whenever a stranger walked past our house. 每當陌生人走過我們家，那隻狗就會開始吠叫。
- Why: bark 最常見的意思是吠叫，樹皮應放在補充義，例句也需配合主要詞義。

### Core 11 · feeder

- Before: We hung a bird feeder outside the classroom window last week. 我們上週在教室窗外掛了一個餵鳥器。
- After: The orphaned lamb drank milk from a rubber feeder. 失親小羊用橡膠奶瓶喝奶。
- Why: feeder 最常見義為餵食器，奶瓶非主要義；「供給者」亦不自然。

### Core 14 · replicate

- Before: No kitchen could replicate the taste of her aunt's noodles. 沒有一家廚房能重現她阿姨做的麵條味道。
- After: No kitchen could replicate the taste of her aunt's noodles. 沒有一間廚房能重現她阿姨做的麵條味道。
- Why: 例句用「重現」，與主要詞義「複製」不完全對應，但此處僅需將量詞「家廚房」改為「間」較自然。

### Core 14 · admittedly

- Before: Admittedly, the attic was dusty, but the view from its window was beautiful. 不可否認，閣樓滿是灰塵，但從窗戶看出去的景色很美。
- After: Admittedly, the attic smelled better after we opened the windows. 誠然，我們打開窗戶後，閣樓的氣味好多了。
- Why: admittedly 主要意思是「誠然、無可否認」，「確實」不精確，且例句用法不符。

### Core 14 · sensibility

- Before: The film appealed deeply to her romantic sensibility. 這部電影深深打動了她浪漫的感性。
- After: The film appealed deeply to her romantic sensibility. 這部電影深深打動了她浪漫的情感特質。
- Why: 「感性」易與 sensitivity 混淆，主要義應為敏感度或感受力，譯文也需調整。

### Core 8 · liability

- Before: The store accepted liability after a customer slipped on its wet floor. 一名顧客在濕滑的地板上滑倒後，店家承擔了責任。
- After: The company accepted liability after several visitors slipped on the cracked sidewalk. 幾名訪客在裂開的人行道上滑倒後，公司承擔了責任。
- Why: 原例句中 liability 的用法與「責任」不符，中文也不自然。

### Core 11 · daring

- Before: The daring rescue began from a broken balcony. 大膽救援從一座破陽台開始。
- After: The daring rescue began from a broken balcony. 大膽的救援行動從一座破損的陽台開始。
- Why: 「冒險精神」不是形容詞義，且譯文漏掉 broken；已修正。

### Core 2 · green

- Before: She painted her bedroom door green to match her plants. 她把臥室的門漆成綠色，好搭配她的植物。
- After: A green mango tastes sharper than a ripe yellow one. 青芒果比熟黃芒果吃起來更酸。
- Why: 最常見的詞義是「綠色的」，「青的」應移到補充義。

### Core 12 · sooner

- Before: A sooner grabbed the corner lot before others arrived. 一名搶先者在其他人到前佔下角落土地。
- After: The sooner you start studying, the better you will do. 你愈早開始讀書，成績就會愈好。
- Why: sooner 最常見為副詞，「搶先者」是極罕見名詞義。

### Core 11 · guild

- Before: The weavers' guild repaired the ancient loom together. 織布工協會一起修復古老織機。
- After: The weavers' guild repaired the ancient loom together. 織布工行會一起修復了古老的織布機。
- Why: exampleZh 漏譯 together 的語氣且不自然；補充義「協會」過於籠統。

### Core 11 · victor

- Before: The victor raised the shiny trophy while the crowd cheered loudly. 勝利者在群眾熱烈歡呼聲中舉起閃亮的獎盃。
- After: The victor raised her paddle after the final bid. 勝利者在最後一次出價後舉起號牌。
- Why: 例句為拍賣情境，victor 用法不自然，與勝利者語意不符。

### Core 11 · favourable

- Before: Clear weather was favourable for drying tea leaves outside. 晴朗天氣很適合在戶外曬茶葉。
- After: Clear weather was favourable for drying tea leaves outside. 晴朗的天氣有利於在戶外曬茶葉。
- Why: favourable 最常見意思為有利的或贊成的，主要釋義不當。

### Core 11 · martial

- Before: Children learn martial discipline at the mountain kung fu school. 孩子們在山上武術館學習武術紀律。
- After: The martial arts club meets every Friday after school. 武術社每週五放學後聚會。
- Why: martial 作形容詞主要義為軍事的；原例句不自然且與釋義不符。

### Core 13 · tenure

- Before: The lease set the tenant's tenure at three years. 租約把租戶的保有期定為三年。
- After: The professor's tenure as department head ended after four years. 那位教授擔任系主任的任期四年後結束。
- Why: 最常見的意思是「任期」，主要義不當，例句也須配合此義。

### Core 13 · layout

- Before: The kitchen layout lets cooks reach every pan quickly. 這種廚房格局，讓廚師能迅速拿到每一只鍋子。
- After: The kitchen layout lets cooks reach every pan quickly. 這種廚房佈局，讓廚師能迅速拿到每一個鍋子。
- Why: 例句譯文「格局」與「只」用法不自然，應與主要義一致並改用「個」。

### Core 12 · min

- Before: The next bus comes in 5 min, so we can wait here. 下一班公車五分鐘後就來，我們在這裡等就好。
- After: The bus leaves in ten min, so please hurry up. 公車十分鐘後就開了，請你快一點。
- Why: min 最常見的意思是分鐘；原例句用 Min. 與字形不符且不自然。

### Core 12 · op

- Before: My grandmother's knee op went well, and she is walking again. 奶奶的膝蓋手術很順利，她又能走路了。
- After: The doctor said the op would take about two hours. 醫師說這項手術大約需要兩小時。
- Why: op 最常見意思是手術（operation 縮寫），觀測所過於罕見。

### Core 6 · islam

- Before: The course on Islam filled quickly at the community center. 社區中心的伊斯蘭教課程很快額滿。
- After: The course on islam filled quickly at the community center. 社區中心的伊斯蘭教課程很快額滿。
- Why: 例句中未包含與詞條完全相同的 islam（原為大寫 Islam）。

### Core 11 · sceptical

- Before: The mechanic sounded sceptical about the cheap replacement part. 技師聽起來對那個便宜的替代零件抱持懷疑。
- After: The mechanic sounded sceptical about the cheap replacement part. 技師對那個便宜的替代零件抱持懷疑。
- Why: 「很不放心」與 sceptical（懷疑）語意不準確，應譯為抱持懷疑。

### Core 11 · manoeuvre

- Before: The driver used a clever manoeuvre to avoid the sinkhole. 駕駛用巧妙的操作避開地洞。
- After: The driver used a clever manoeuvre to avoid the sinkhole. 駕駛用巧妙的操控動作避開了地洞。
- Why: 「操作」不是名詞最常見義，主要義應為策略或機動動作，並調整譯文。

### Core 11 · rehabilitate

- Before: Therapists helped rehabilitate his injured wrist after surgery. 治療師在手術後協助他受傷的手腕復健。
- After: Therapists helped rehabilitate his injured wrist after surgery. 治療師在手術後協助他受傷的手腕復原。
- Why: 「復健」與主要詞義「使康復」用詞不一致，改為「復原」較貼切。

### Core 2 · death

- Before: His death left the whole fishing village unusually quiet. 他的去世讓整個漁村異常安靜。
- After: His death left the whole fishing village unusually quiet. 他的死讓整個漁村異常安靜。
- Why: death 主要意思為「死亡」，「去世」是動詞性說法，不宜作主要釋義。

### Core 11 · parish

- Before: The parish collected blankets for families after the typhoon. 堂區在颱風後為家庭募集毛毯。
- After: The parish collected blankets for families after the typhoon. 教區在颱風後為家庭募集毛毯。
- Why: parish 最常見義為「教區」，應作主要詞義，「堂區」為次要說法。

### Core 14 · ginger

- Before: Fresh ginger warmed the soup on cold nights. 寒夜裡，新鮮薑讓湯更暖身。
- After: Fresh ginger warmed the soup on cold nights. 寒夜裡，新鮮的薑讓湯更暖身。
- Why: 「活力」並非 ginger 常見的補充詞義，應刪除。

### Core 14 · overtime

- Before: Chen worked overtime to finish the wedding dress. 陳加班完成那件婚紗。
- After: Chen worked overtime to finish the wedding dress. 陳小姐加班完成了那件婚紗。
- Why: 補充詞義「延長時間」不夠常見，應改為加班費與延長賽。

### Core 14 · bracelet

- Before: A jade bracelet clicked against her porcelain teacup. 一只玉手鐲碰到她的瓷茶杯發出輕響。
- After: A jade bracelet clicked against her porcelain teacup. 一只玉手鐲碰到她的瓷茶杯，發出輕響。
- Why: bracelet 最常見的意思是手環，手鐲應列為補充詞義。

### Core 13 · dietary

- Before: Dietary changes helped Mr. Chen control his blood sugar. 飲食改變幫陳先生控制血糖。
- After: Dietary changes helped Mr. Chen control his blood sugar. 飲食改變幫助陳先生控制血糖。
- Why: dietary 的補充義「規定飲食」罕見，且與詞性不符。

### Core 1 · able

- Before: She is able to repair radios with simple tools. 她能用簡單工具修收音機。
- After: She is able to repair radios with simple tools. 她能用簡單的工具修理收音機。
- Why: meaningZh「能」不是完整形容詞詞義，應為「有能力的」，補充義重複。

### Core 13 · inheritance

- Before: Her inheritance included a watch, two letters, and farmland. 她的遺產包括一只錶、兩封信和農地。
- After: Her inheritance included a watch, two letters, and farmland. 她繼承的財產包括一只錶、兩封信和農地。
- Why: inheritance 指繼承所得的財產，「遺產」偏指死者所留，且譯句宜自然通順。

### Core 13 · amusement

- Before: Grandpa watched the magician with quiet amusement from the sofa. 爺爺坐在沙發上，帶著淡淡興味看魔術師。
- After: Grandpa watched the magician with quiet amusement from the sofa. 爺爺坐在沙發上，面帶淡淡的笑意看魔術師表演。
- Why: 「興味」不是最常見意思；amusement 常指娛樂或愉悅，譯句也不自然。

### Core 13 · vegetation

- Before: Dense vegetation hid the abandoned railway from the road. 茂密植被遮住路旁的廢棄鐵道。
- After: Dense vegetation hid the abandoned railway from the road. 茂密的植物遮住了路旁的廢棄鐵道。
- Why: 「植被」較專業，主要意思應為植物；譯句也漏了助詞顯得生硬。

### Core 2 · involve

- Before: The ceremony will involve lanterns, drums, and a river blessing. 儀式會包括燈籠、鼓聲和河祭祝福。
- After: The ceremony will involve lanterns, drums, and a river blessing. 儀式將包括燈籠、鼓聲和河祭祝福。
- Why: involve 最常見義為「涉及」；中文翻譯缺少標點，讀來不自然。

### Core 2 · college

- Before: Her college overlooks the sea on Taiwan's east coast. 她的大學位於台灣東海岸，可以俯瞰大海。
- After: Her college overlooks the sea on Taiwan's east coast. 她的學院位於台灣東海岸，可以俯瞰大海。
- Why: college 主要指「學院」，「大學」為補充義，例句翻譯也需配合。

### Core 13 · remnant

- Before: A remnant of blue silk lined the drawer. 一塊藍色絲綢碎布襯在抽屜裡。
- After: A remnant of blue silk lined the drawer. 一塊藍色的絲綢碎料襯在抽屜裡。
- Why: exampleZh 漏譯 silk（絲綢），譯文不完整。

### Core 13 · turning

- Before: Take the first turning on the left after the temple. 過了廟之後，在第一個轉彎處左轉。
- After: The turning on the mountain road was so sharp that the bus slowed down. 山路上的轉彎非常急，公車因此放慢了速度。
- Why: turning 最常見的意思是轉彎處，車削是較冷僻的專門用法，例句也應配合常見意義。

### Core 13 · sweeping

- Before: Sweeping changes turned the quiet pier into a market. 大刀闊斧的改造，把安靜的碼頭變成了市集。
- After: The school announced sweeping changes to the lunch menu this semester. 學校宣布本學期將全面更動午餐菜單。
- Why: 補充義「掃除」不適合形容詞用法，應改為較常見的意思。

### Core 2 · dark

- Before: The hallway was so dark that I could not find the light switch. 走廊太暗了，我找不到電燈開關。
- After: Dark clouds gathered behind the mountain temple before evening prayers. 晚禱前烏雲聚集在山寺後方。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「黑暗的」而非「烏黑的」，補充詞義也調整為常見義。

### Core 4 · connection

- Before: The ferry connection to Green Island stopped during the storm. 通往綠島的渡輪接駁在暴風中停駛。
- After: The internet connection at the hotel was too slow for video calls. 飯店的網路連線太慢，無法視訊通話。
- Why: 「接駁」不是最常見的詞義，應以「連接」為主，例句也需配合。

### Core 4 · lesson

- Before: The storm taught everyone a lesson about mountain weather. 那場暴風給大家上了山區天氣的一課。
- After: Our English lesson starts at nine on Monday morning. 我們星期一早上九點上英文課。
- Why: lesson 最常見的意思是「課」，「教訓」應放在補充，例句也需配合。

### Core 5 · vegetable

- Before: Nina added one vegetable to the noodles for color and crunch. 妮娜在麵裡加一樣蔬菜添色和口感。
- After: Nina added one vegetable to the noodles for color and crunch. 妮娜在麵裡加了一樣蔬菜，增添色彩和口感。
- Why: exampleZh 漏譯「for color and crunch」的語氣且過於簡略，已改為更完整自然的翻譯。

### Core 5 · perfectly

- Before: The new key turned perfectly after Aaron cleaned the rusty lock. 亞倫清完鏽鎖後新鑰匙轉得很順。
- After: The new key turned perfectly after Aaron cleaned the rusty lock. 亞倫清完生鏽的鎖之後，新鑰匙轉得很順暢。
- Why: meaningZh 不是最常見義，應為「完美地」；「很順利地」移至補充義。

### Core 5 · lock

- Before: Jun forgot to lock the balcony door before the typhoon arrived. 颱風來前阿俊忘了鎖陽台門。
- After: Jun forgot to lock the balcony door before the typhoon arrived. 颱風來臨前，阿俊忘了把陽台門鎖上。
- Why: exampleZh 過於簡略，已改為更自然完整的翻譯。

### Core 5 · boot

- Before: Mud soaked one boot when Paul crossed the flooded farm path. 保羅走過淹水農路時一隻靴子濕透。
- After: Mud soaked one boot when Paul crossed the flooded farm path. 保羅走過淹水的農路時，有一隻靴子被泥水浸濕了。
- Why: 「解雇」為罕見義，exampleZh 亦略嫌不自然，已修正。

### Core 13 · usher

- Before: An usher guided late guests to the balcony seats. 引座員帶遲到賓客到樓上座位。
- After: The usher guided late guests to the balcony seats. 引座員帶遲到的賓客到樓上座位。
- Why: 英文 An usher 冠詞錯誤，應為 The usher（usher 以子音發音開頭，不能用 An）。

### Core 9 · puzzle

- Before: The missing key remained a puzzle all week. 那把鑰匙究竟去了哪裡，整整一週都沒人解得開。
- After: The missing key remained a puzzle all week. 那把鑰匙不見的原因整整一週都是個謎。
- Why: puzzle 名詞最常見義為「難題」；原中文翻譯過於自由且不自然。

### Core 5 · mere

- Before: A mere apology could not replace the broken heirloom. 光一句道歉不能補回破碎傳家物。
- After: A mere apology could not replace the broken heirloom. 光是一句道歉，無法彌補那件破碎的傳家寶。
- Why: mere 是形容詞，意為「僅僅的」，「光是」詞性不符；譯文也不通順。

### Core 5 · dear

- Before: My dear friend saved a seat beside the window. 我親愛的朋友在窗邊幫我留座。
- After: My dear friend saved a seat beside the window. 我親愛的朋友在窗邊幫我留了座位。
- Why: 「可愛的人」不是 dear 的常見義，應為「親愛的人」。

### Core 5 · advanced

- Before: An advanced camera caught stars above the fishing port. 高階相機拍下漁港夜空中的繁星。
- After: An advanced camera caught stars above the fishing port. 一台先進的相機拍下了漁港上空的繁星。
- Why: advanced 最常見義為「先進的」，「高階的」宜列為補充。

### Core 5 · sink

- Before: The heavy stone began to sink in the muddy pond. 沉重石頭開始沉入泥池。
- After: The heavy stone began to sink in the muddy pond. 沉重的石頭開始沉入泥濘的池塘。
- Why: 譯文省略「泥濘」且不自然；主要釋義宜為「下沉」。

### Core 5 · stupid

- Before: Leaving medicine in the sun was a stupid mistake. 把藥放在太陽下，是個很不明智的錯誤。
- After: Leaving medicine in the sun was a stupid mistake. 把藥放在太陽下是個愚蠢的錯誤。
- Why: exampleZh 把 stupid 譯成「不明智」，與 meaningZh 愚蠢的不一致。

### Core 5 · entertainment

- Before: Street entertainment kept the children awake after dinner. 晚餐後的街頭表演，讓孩子們興奮得不肯睡覺。
- After: Street entertainment kept the children awake after dinner. 晚餐後的街頭娛樂讓孩子們都不想睡。
- Why: exampleZh 添加原文沒有的「興奮」，且將 entertainment 譯為表演，與 meaningZh 不一致。

### Core 5 · fortune

- Before: Good fortune saved us from the falling roof tile. 幸好運氣不錯，我們躲過了從屋頂掉下來的瓦片。
- After: Good fortune saved us from the falling roof tile. 好運讓我們躲過了掉落的屋瓦。
- Why: fortune 最常見的意思是財富，好運應列為補充；且譯文過度意譯。

### Core 5 · confront

- Before: Firefighters must confront danger calmly when a building is burning. 建築物失火時，消防員必須冷靜面對危險。
- After: Maya had to confront the neighbor about midnight drumming. 瑪雅得為了半夜打鼓的事當面質問鄰居。
- Why: confront 最常見的意思是面對，「找……談」不是主要字義。

### Core 10 · implicit

- Before: Her silence carried an implicit warning that nobody missed. 她的沉默隱含著警告，每個人都察覺到了。
- After: Her silence carried an implicit warning that nobody missed. 她的沉默帶著沒有人忽略的含蓄警告。
- Why: 「無條件的」不精確，implicit 此義應為「絕對的」。

### Core 3 · proper

- Before: Wear proper shoes for the slippery stone steps. 濕滑石階要穿適當鞋子。
- After: Wear proper shoes for the slippery stone steps. 濕滑石階要穿適當的鞋子。
- Why: 「真正的」並非 proper 常見義；exampleZh 少了「的」較不自然。

### Core 3 · monday

- Before: Monday rain delayed garbage collection in our lane. 星期一的雨，耽誤了我們巷子的垃圾清運。
- After: Monday is the day I return my library books. 星期一是我還圖書館書籍的日子。
- Why: 例句 Monday 為專有名詞，原句 Monday rain 用法不自然，且 word 為小寫與例句不完全一致

### Core 3 · wednesday

- Before: Wednesday markets sell the freshest mountain vegetables after dawn. 星期三市場天亮後賣最新鮮山菜。
- After: We have a short English quiz every Wednesday morning. 我們每個星期三早上都有一個簡短的英文小考。
- Why: 原例句不自然且譯文生硬，並與 word 小寫不符

### Core 3 · thursday

- Before: Thursday flights to Kinmen often leave before dawn. 星期四飛金門的班機常在黎明前起飛。
- After: Our class has a swimming lesson every Thursday afternoon. 我們班每個星期四下午有游泳課。
- Why: 例句 Thursday 與 word 小寫不符

### Core 3 · tuesday

- Before: Tuesday is best for visiting the quiet museum. 星期二最適合參觀安靜的博物館。
- After: The museum is quiet on Tuesday mornings, so we often go then. 博物館星期二早上很安靜，所以我們常在那時去。
- Why: 例句 Tuesday 與 word 小寫不符，原句也不自然

### Core 3 · latest

- Before: The latest phone update improved battery life overnight. 手機安裝最新更新後，電池續航力隔天就改善了。
- After: The latest phone update improved battery life overnight. 手機最新的更新在一夜之間改善了電池續航力。
- Why: 譯文多出「安裝」且語意不順，與原句不符

### Core 3 · armed

- Before: Armed officers guarded the courthouse after the threat. 接獲威脅後武裝警員守著法院。
- After: Armed officers guarded the courthouse after the threat. 接獲威脅後，武裝警員守衛著法院。
- Why: 補充義「配備的」不明確且不常見

### Core 7 · logic

- Before: The logic behind his shortcut failed at the flooded underpass. 他抄捷徑的邏輯，在淹水的地下道前完全行不通。
- After: The logic behind his shortcut failed at the flooded underpass. 他走捷徑的邏輯，在淹水的地下道前徹底行不通。
- Why: meaningZh「理由」不是 logic 最常見的意思，應為「邏輯」。

### Core 7 · sheep

- Before: A lost sheep wandered near the tea farm fence. 一隻迷路的羊在茶園圍籬旁徘徊。
- After: A lost sheep wandered near the tea farm fence. 一隻迷路的綿羊在茶園圍籬旁徘徊。
- Why: 主要意思應為「綿羊」，「羊」過於籠統，且補充意思與主要意思重複。

### Core 4 · substantial

- Before: A substantial stone wall protected the house during the storm. 一道結實的石牆在暴風雨中保護了房子。
- After: A substantial number of students joined the beach cleanup last weekend. 上週末有相當多的學生參加了淨灘活動。
- Why: substantial 最常見的意思是大量的，結實的較少用，例句也應配合主要詞義。

### Core 4 · contemporary

- Before: Contemporary writers described the flood in very different ways. 同時代的作家用截然不同的方式描述那場洪水。
- After: Contemporary art museums attract many students on weekends. 當代藝術館在週末吸引許多學生。
- Why: contemporary 最常見的意思是當代的，同時代的為次要意思。

### Core 4 · recognition

- Before: The rescue worker received recognition from the mayor. 那名救難員得到市長表揚。
- After: Facial recognition lets my phone unlock when I look at it. 臉部辨識讓我的手機一看就能解鎖。
- Why: recognition 最常見的意思是認出或辨識，表揚並非主要詞義。

### Core 14 · wan

- Before: A wan light shone through the hospital curtain. 一道蒼白光線透過醫院窗簾照進來。
- After: A wan light shone through the hospital curtain. 一道蒼白的光線透過醫院窗簾照進來。
- Why: 譯文漏掉「的」讀來不自然，已修正；補充義也補足。

### Core 10 · tread

- Before: Please tread carefully on the wet temple steps. 請小心走在潮濕廟階上。
- After: Please tread carefully on the wet temple steps. 請小心走在潮濕的廟階上。
- Why: meaningZh「踩著走」不自然，改為「踩；行走」；exampleZh 語句略不通順，補上「的」。

### Core 10 · redundancy

- Before: The editor removed redundancy from the final safety instructions. 編輯刪掉最後安全指示中的冗詞。
- After: The editor removed redundancy from the final safety instructions. 編輯刪掉了最後安全指示中的冗詞。
- Why: 「冗餘」才是最常見意義，「冗詞」應放補充；原中文翻譯略不自然。

### Core 10 · prose

- Before: Her prose made the fishing village feel alive. 她筆下的散文，讓那座漁村彷彿活了起來。
- After: Her prose made the fishing village feel alive. 她的散文讓那座漁村彷彿活了起來。
- Why: prose 的詞性不含形容詞，「散文的」不當；補充義改為較合理的義項。

### Core 7 · depressing

- Before: The empty amusement park looked depressing in the rain. 雨中空無一人的遊樂園看起來令人沮喪。
- After: The empty amusement park looked depressing in the rain. 雨中空蕩蕩的遊樂園看起來令人沮喪。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「令人沮喪的」，「沉悶的」不是最常見義，例句翻譯也需配合。

### Core 7 · kindly

- Before: The old barber spoke kindly to nervous children. 老理髮師親切安撫緊張孩子。
- After: The old barber spoke kindly to nervous children. 老理髮師親切地安撫緊張的孩子。
- Why: 詞性標示 a / adv，主要義應與形容詞對應；「請」為罕用義，且例句中譯不完整。

### Core 7 · riding

- Before: Night riding through the tunnel made her ears pop. 夜裡騎車穿過隧道時，她的耳朵因氣壓變化而悶住。
- After: Riding a bike through the tunnel made her ears pop. 騎腳踏車穿過隧道時，她的耳朵因氣壓變化而悶住。
- Why: 例句 Night riding 不自然，且中譯「夜裡」與原句不符；改為更自然的例句。

### Core 7 · covering

- Before: A bamboo covering shaded the vegetable stall all afternoon. 竹製遮蔽物整下午替菜攤遮陰。
- After: A bamboo covering shaded the vegetable stall all afternoon. 竹製覆蓋物整個下午替菜攤遮陰。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「覆蓋物」，補充義重複，例句中譯漏掉「整個」語意不夠自然。

### Core 7 · finding

- Before: The study's main finding surprised every student in our science club. 這項研究的主要發現讓科學社每位同學都很驚訝。
- After: The scientist's finding surprised everyone in the class. 科學家的發現讓全班同學都很驚訝。
- Why: 最常見義為「發現」，「裁決」是法律專門用法，不宜作主要義。

### Core 7 · setting

- Before: The mountain setting made the wedding feel private. 婚禮辦在山間，感覺格外私密。
- After: The mountain setting made the wedding feel private. 山間的環境讓這場婚禮感覺格外私密。
- Why: 例句 setting 指場所環境，中譯漏譯 setting 且與主要義不符。

### Core 8 · evolution

- Before: The evolution of the harbor began with one wooden pier. 這座港口的發展從一座木碼頭開始。
- After: The evolution of the harbor began with one wooden pier. 這座港口的演變從一座木碼頭開始。
- Why: evolution 最常見的意思是「進化」，「發展」應放在補充意思，例句則宜用「演變」。

### Core 8 · input

- Before: The device records input from the keypad every few seconds. 這台裝置每隔幾秒記錄鍵盤輸入。
- After: The device records input from the keypad every few seconds. 這台裝置每隔幾秒就會記錄鍵盤的輸入內容。
- Why: 原翻譯「記錄鍵盤輸入」略顯生硬，補充「輸入內容」較自然。

### Core 2 · store

- Before: Store dried mushrooms in a sealed glass jar. 把乾香菇存放在密封玻璃罐裡。
- After: I bought a notebook at the store near my school. 我在學校附近的商店買了一本筆記本。
- Why: store 最常見的意思是名詞「商店」，應作為主要詞義，動詞義放入補充。

### Core 2 · memory

- Before: That melody brought back a childhood memory during dinner. 晚餐時那段旋律喚回童年記憶。
- After: That melody brought back a childhood memory during dinner. 晚餐時那段旋律喚回了一段童年回憶。
- Why: 最常見詞義為「記憶」，「回憶」應放補充；譯文也漏掉 a 的意思。

### Core 2 · investment

- Before: Her investment in good boots saved her ankles. 她投資一雙好靴子保護了腳踝。
- After: Her investment in good boots saved her ankles. 她買好靴子這項投資保護了她的腳踝。
- Why: 補充義「投入」過於籠統，譯文把名詞 investment 誤譯成動詞。

### Core 2 · institution

- Before: The temple became an institution for local charity. 那座廟成了地方慈善中心。
- After: The temple became an institution for local charity. 那座廟成了地方慈善的重要機構。
- Why: 譯文把 institution 譯成「中心」，與詞義「機構」不一致。

### Core 3 · highly

- Before: The new bubble tea shop near our school is highly popular with students. 學校附近新開的手搖飲店非常受學生歡迎。
- After: The guide spoke highly of this quiet mountain inn. 導遊對這間安靜的山莊讚不絕口。
- Why: 例句 spoke highly of 是「高度讚許」，與主要詞義「非常」不符，譯文也不自然。

### Core 14 · plank

- Before: A loose plank creaked beneath the fisherman's rubber boots. 一塊鬆動木板在漁夫的膠靴下嘎吱作響。
- After: A loose plank creaked beneath the fisherman's rubber boots. 一塊鬆動的木板在漁夫的膠靴下嘎吱作響。
- Why: 「政綱條目」為罕見用法，不宜列為補充；例句譯文漏「的」略不自然。

### Core 14 · doom

- Before: Loose wiring spelled doom for the carnival ride. 電線鬆脫，這座遊樂設施注定要停擺。
- After: Loose wiring spelled doom for the carnival ride. 電線鬆脫，預示這座遊樂設施將面臨厄運。
- Why: 譯文未忠實對應 spelled doom；補充義「注定失敗」不精確。

### Core 6 · mill

- Before: The old rice mill still smells of warm grain. 老碾米廠仍有溫熱穀物的味道。
- After: The old rice mill still smells of warm grain. 那間老碾米廠仍有溫熱穀物的味道。
- Why: 例句 mill 指碾米廠，與磨坊略有出入；翻譯語句也不夠完整自然。

### Core 6 · prominent

- Before: A prominent scar crossed the chef's left hand. 主廚左手上有一道明顯疤痕。
- After: A prominent scar crossed the chef's left hand. 主廚左手上有一道明顯的疤痕。
- Why: prominent 最常見義為顯著或著名，中文翻譯語句不夠通順。

### Core 6 · ownership

- Before: Legal ownership of the orchard passed to the youngest daughter. 果園所有權轉到小女兒名下。
- After: Legal ownership of the orchard passed to the youngest daughter. 果園的所有權轉到了小女兒名下。
- Why: 中文翻譯語句不夠自然，缺少「的」。

### Core 3 · flower

- Before: A single flower grew through the cracked pavement overnight. 一朵花一夜間從裂開的人行道長出。
- After: A single flower grew through the cracked pavement overnight. 一朵花一夜之間從裂開的人行道長了出來。
- Why: 補充義「精華」過於罕見，應刪除；譯文也略顯生硬。

### Core 2 · manage

- Before: Ken can manage the shop while his aunt rests. 阿姨休息時阿肯能幫忙看店。
- After: Ken can manage the shop while his aunt rests. 阿姨休息時，阿肯能負責管理這家店。
- Why: manage 最常見義為設法做到，管理應列為補充；例句譯文「看店」與「阿肯」稱呼不自然。

### Core 2 · sea

- Before: The sea turned silver under the full moon. 滿月下海面變成銀色。
- After: The sea turned silver under the full moon. 滿月下的海面變成了銀色。
- Why: 補充義「大量」過於罕見，應移除並保留常見義。

### Core 2 · career

- Before: A long career in nursing taught her patience. 長年護理生涯教會她耐心。
- After: A long career in nursing taught her patience. 長年的護理生涯教會了她耐心。
- Why: 譯文「護理生涯」略不自然，已調整為通順的說法。

### Core 2 · leg

- Before: His left leg cramped during the river crossing. 過河時他的左腿抽筋。
- After: His left leg cramped during the river crossing. 過河時他的左腿抽筋了。
- Why: 詞性 vi 並不常見，補充義可保留，但譯文需更完整自然。

### Core 2 · lay

- Before: Lay the fish on ice before the drive home. 開車回家前把魚放在冰上。
- After: Lay the fish on ice before the drive home. 開車回家前，先把魚放在冰上。
- Why: 譯文略顯生硬，已調整為自然的台灣華語。

### Core 2 · television

- Before: Public television showed the typhoon path all night. 公共電視整夜播報颱風路徑。
- After: Public television showed the typhoon path all night. 公共電視整夜播放颱風路徑。
- Why: 「顯示」用於電視節目不自然，應改為播放。

### Core 2 · remove

- Before: Remove the wet shoes before stepping onto the mat. 踏上墊子前先脫掉濕鞋。
- After: Remove the wet shoes before stepping onto the mat. 踏上墊子前，先脫掉濕鞋。
- Why: remove 最常見義為移除，脫掉應列為補充義。

### Core 13 · imagery

- Before: The speech used storm imagery to describe the crisis. 演說用風暴比喻描述那場危機。
- After: The poet used vivid imagery of storms to describe the crisis. 詩人用生動的風暴意象來描寫那場危機。
- Why: 補充語意「比喻」不正確；例句譯文漏掉 imagery 的意象意義，且與原句語意不夠貼合。

### Core 13 · exclaim

- Before: Children exclaim with delight when the fireworks begin. 煙火開始時，孩子們開心地大叫。
- After: Children exclaim with delight when the fireworks begin. 煙火開始時，孩子們高興地驚呼。
- Why: exclaim 最常見的意思是驚呼，「大叫」不精準。

### Core 8 · attribute

- Before: Many residents attribute the cracks to last winter's earthquake. 許多居民把裂縫歸因於去年冬天的地震。
- After: Honesty is the attribute I admire most in my best friend. 誠實是我最欣賞好朋友的特質。
- Why: 名詞用法為主，主要意思應為「屬性」，「歸因於」是動詞用法；例句需與詞義相符。

### Core 8 · heritage

- Before: The festival preserves the fishing heritage of the old village. 這場祭典保存老村莊的漁業傳承。
- After: The festival preserves the fishing heritage of the old village. 這場祭典保存了老村莊的漁業文化遺產。
- Why: heritage 最常見意思為「遺產」，「傳承」並非主要詞義，例句翻譯也不自然。

### Core 8 · resemble

- Before: The newborn's eyes resemble her father's in old photos. 新生兒的眼睛像舊照片裡的父親。
- After: The newborn's eyes resemble her father's in old photos. 在舊照片裡，新生兒的眼睛長得像她父親的眼睛。
- Why: 原中文譯文把眼睛與父親比較，語意錯誤，應是與父親的眼睛相像。

### Core 8 · regardless

- Before: The ferry leaves at seven sharp regardless of the rain. 不管有沒有下雨，渡輪都會在七點整出發。
- After: The ferry leaves regardless of rain at seven sharp. 不管下雨與否，渡輪七點整出發。
- Why: regardless 詞性應為副詞，原標示為形容詞且詞義不精確。

### Core 8 · warrior

- Before: A painted warrior stood beside the temple doorway. 一位彩繪武士立在寺廟門口旁。
- After: A painted warrior stood beside the temple doorway. 一位臉上彩繪的戰士站在寺廟門口旁。
- Why: warrior 最常見的意思是戰士，武士偏向特定文化，應列為補充義。

### Core 8 · feedback

- Before: Customer feedback persuaded the bakery to open earlier on weekends. 顧客意見促使麵包店在週末提早開門。
- After: Customer feedback persuaded the bakery to open earlier on weekends. 顧客的回饋促使麵包店週末提早開門。
- Why: feedback 最常見的意思是回饋，意見應列為補充義。

### Core 8 · exclusively

- Before: The upstairs room is used exclusively for storing tea. 樓上房間專門用來存放茶葉。
- After: The upstairs room is used exclusively for storing tea. 樓上的房間專門用來存放茶葉。
- Why: 副詞的中文釋義應為「專門地」，並補上更自然的譯文。

### Core 9 · follower

- Before: As a loyal follower of the band, she never misses a concert. 身為這個樂團的忠實追隨者，她從不錯過任何一場演唱會。
- After: The singer thanked every follower for the kind messages. 那位歌手感謝每位追隨者傳來的暖心訊息。
- Why: 主要意思應為「追隨者」，「隨從」較少用，例句也應配合。

### Core 9 · worm

- Before: A worm wriggled from the soil after rain. 雨後一條蟲從土裡扭動爬出。
- After: A worm wriggled from the soil after rain. 雨後一條蠕蟲從土裡扭動爬出。
- Why: 「蟲」過於籠統，應為「蠕蟲」，補充義重複，應修正。

### Core 9 · inn

- Before: The mountain inn served tea beside a wood stove. 山中旅館在柴爐旁為旅客奉茶。
- After: The mountain inn served tea beside a wood stove. 山中小旅館在柴爐旁為旅客奉茶。
- Why: inn 為小旅館，「旅館」太籠統；詞性 vi 亦不適當，補充義需修正。

### Core 9 · pavement

- Before: Fresh mango juice spilled across the hot pavement. 新鮮芒果汁灑在滾燙路面上。
- After: Fresh mango juice spilled across the hot pavement. 新鮮芒果汁灑在滾燙的路面上。
- Why: 英式主要義為人行道，應為主要義；例句譯文也稍顯生硬。

### Core 12 · diagram

- Before: The diagram showed how rainwater reached the basement. 圖表顯示雨水如何流到地下室。
- After: The diagram showed how rainwater reached the basement. 示意圖顯示雨水如何流到地下室。
- Why: meaningZh 與例句翻譯不一致，譯文應與詞義對應為示意圖

### Core 8 · emission

- Before: The lamp's emission of blue light attracted insects after dusk. 黃昏後，燈發出的藍光吸引昆蟲。
- After: The factory's emission of smoke worried the people living near the river. 工廠排放的煙霧讓住在河邊的居民很擔心。
- Why: meaningZh 最常見義應為「排放」；原例句藍光與「排放」義不符且「放射」較少用

### Core 8 · architecture

- Before: Spanish architecture still shapes the church facing the central plaza. 西班牙建築風格仍塑造著廣場前的教堂。
- After: She wants to study architecture at university because she loves designing buildings. 她想在大學讀建築學，因為她喜歡設計建築物。
- Why: 最常見義為「建築學」，「建築風格」應列為補充義，例句也需配合

### Core 7 · carrot

- Before: A crooked carrot poked from the market basket. 一根彎胡蘿蔔從市場籃裡探出。
- After: A crooked carrot poked out of the market basket. 一根彎曲的胡蘿蔔從市場籃子裡探出來。
- Why: 中文「彎胡蘿蔔」不自然，改為「彎曲的胡蘿蔔」。

### Core 7 · ending

- Before: The film's quiet ending left the theater silent. 電影在寂靜中結束，整間戲院也跟著沉默下來。
- After: The film's quiet ending left the theater silent. 電影安靜的結局讓整間戲院沉默下來。
- Why: ending 最常見意思為結局；原譯文也增添了原句沒有的內容。

### Core 7 · inability

- Before: His inability to sleep worsened after the quake. 地震後他無法入睡的情況惡化。
- After: His inability to sleep worsened after the quake. 地震後，他無法入睡的情況更嚴重了。
- Why: 「缺乏能力」不是最常見意思，補充意義也重複。

### Core 12 · gilt

- Before: Thin gilt edges made the wedding invitations shine under candlelight. 薄鍍金邊讓婚禮請柬在燭光下發亮。
- After: Thin gilt edges made the wedding invitations shine under candlelight. 薄薄的鍍金邊讓婚禮請柬在燭光下閃閃發亮。
- Why: 詞性含名詞，meaningZh 應為名詞「鍍金」而非形容詞；補充義更合適。

### Core 12 · topical

- Before: The pharmacist gave Mei a topical cream for the rash. 藥師給美一條治皮疹的外用乳膏。
- After: The teacher chose a topical issue for today's class discussion. 老師為今天的課堂討論挑了一個時事議題。
- Why: 最常見意思是「時事的」，外用是較專業的義項；例句也需配合主要義。

### Core 12 · modernize

- Before: The museum will modernize its ticket gates next spring. 博物館明年春天將把售票閘門現代化。
- After: The museum will modernize its ticket gates next spring. 博物館明年春天將使售票閘門現代化。
- Why: 補充義「現代化」與主要義重複；中文例句缺少「使」，語句不通順。

### Core 12 · soundtrack

- Before: The film's soundtrack carried scratches from the old projector. 電影聲帶留有舊放映機造成的刮痕。
- After: The film's soundtrack made the final scene feel very emotional. 這部電影的配樂讓最後一幕非常感人。
- Why: 最常見意思是配樂或原聲帶，而非「電影聲帶」；例句語意也不自然。

### Core 12 · insecure

- Before: An insecure railing made the balcony unsafe for children. 欄杆不夠牢固，孩子待在陽台上很危險。
- After: She felt insecure about speaking English in front of the class. 她在全班面前說英語時覺得沒有安全感。
- Why: 最常見意思是缺乏安全感或沒自信，「不牢靠」不是主要義。

### Core 12 · gore

- Before: The horror film used too much gore for my taste. 那部恐怖片的血腥畫面對我來說太多。
- After: The horror film used too much gore for my taste. 那部恐怖片的血腥畫面對我來說太多了。
- Why: 補充義「凝血」「刺傷」不自然，應改為「凝結的血」「用角刺傷」。

### Core 12 · consuming

- Before: The consuming public rejected the costly imported fruit. 消費大眾拒買昂貴進口水果。
- After: She has a consuming passion for painting after school every day. 她對放學後每天畫畫有著強烈的熱情。
- Why: consuming 作形容詞最常用義為「強烈的、耗盡心力的」，原例句也不符此詞性常用義。

### Core 12 · vatican

- Before: The Vatican announced new rules for archive access. 梵蒂岡宣布檔案查閱的新規定。
- After: The Vatican announced new rules for archive access. 梵蒂岡宣佈檔案查閱的新規定。
- Why: 例句字詞須與給定單字完全一致，且「宣布」台灣常用「宣布」，此處無誤；但原詞為小寫，需確認。

### Core 12 · brit

- Before: Our new English teacher is a Brit who loves bubble tea. 我們的新英文老師是個愛喝珍珠奶茶的英國人。
- After: My teacher is a Brit who loves Taiwanese bubble tea. 我的老師是個愛喝台灣珍珠奶茶的英國人。
- Why: brit 最常見義為「英國人」，小鯡魚群很罕見；例句也未含原字 brit。

### Core 12 · poised

- Before: The dancer stood poised before the curtain rose. 布幕升起前，舞者沉著地站著。
- After: The team is poised to win the school basketball final. 這支隊伍準備好贏得學校籃球決賽了。
- Why: poised 最常見義為「準備好的」，沉著義次之。

### Core 8 · assurance

- Before: Her calm voice gave passengers assurance during the delay. 延誤期間，她平穩的聲音讓乘客安心。
- After: The manager gave us his assurance that the repairs would finish by Friday. 經理向我們保證維修會在週五前完成。
- Why: assurance 最常見的意思是「保證」，「安心感」不是最常用的主要字義。

### Core 13 · nucleus

- Before: The old library became the nucleus of the new campus. 舊圖書館成了新校園的核心。
- After: The nucleus controls the activities of the cell. 細胞核控制細胞的各種活動。
- Why: 最常見的意思是細胞核，核心較次要；例句也應配合主要詞義。

### Core 13 · tub

- Before: The toddler filled the tub with plastic boats. 幼兒把浴盆裝滿塑膠船。
- After: The toddler filled the tub with plastic boats. 幼兒把浴缸裝滿塑膠船。
- Why: 台灣常用「浴缸」；詞性標示的動詞義罕見，補充義也需調整。

### Core 2 · glass

- Before: Be careful because the kitchen floor is covered with broken glass. 小心，廚房地板上都是碎玻璃。
- After: A glass of barley tea cooled beside the stove. 一杯麥茶在爐邊放涼。
- Why: meaningZh 應為最常見的「玻璃」，「一杯」是量詞用法，應移到補充義。

### Core 2 · ready

- Before: The soup is ready when the clams open. 蛤蜊打開時湯就好了。
- After: The soup is ready when the clams open. 蛤蜊打開時，湯就準備好了。
- Why: 形容詞 ready 的主要意義是「準備好的」，「好了」不夠精確；詞性 n 也不妥。

### Core 2 · realize

- Before: Only after moving out did I realize how hard my mom worked. 搬出去住以後，我才明白媽媽有多辛苦。
- After: At dawn, I realize how quiet the harbor feels. 清晨我才領悟港口有多安靜。
- Why: 例句語意與時態不自然，譯文「才」與現在式不符，需調整。

### Core 11 · hardy

- Before: Hardy sailors crossed the bay during the winter storm. 吃苦耐勞的水手在冬季暴風中橫越海灣。
- After: Hardy sailors crossed the bay during the winter storm. 強健的水手在冬季暴風中穿越海灣。
- Why: exampleZh 的「勇敢」與 hardy 原義不符，應為強健或吃苦耐勞。

### Core 11 · unification

- Before: The unification of Germany took place in 1990 after the Berlin Wall fell. 柏林圍牆倒塌後，德國在1990年完成統一。
- After: Unification of the two clinics reduced paperwork for patients. 兩家診所合併後，病患要填的文件變少了。
- Why: unification 最常見意思是統一，合併為次要義，主要與補充義應對調。

### Core 11 · teen

- Before: The teen waited at the bus stop with her heavy school bag. 那名青少年揹著沉重的書包在公車站等車。
- After: The teen waited at the bus stop with her school bag. 那位青少年揹著書包在公車站等車。
- Why: meaningZh 誤用罕見古義「悲憤」，常用義應為「青少年」，例句也不符常見用法。

### Core 11 · legislative

- Before: A legislative aide carried the bill into the chamber. 一名立法助理把法案帶進議場。
- After: The legislative committee will discuss the new bill tomorrow morning. 立法委員會明天早上將討論這項新法案。
- Why: 詞性為形容詞，「立法機構」屬罕見名詞義；例句改為形容詞用法更貼切。

### Core 11 · irritation

- Before: Skin irritation appeared after he touched the cleaning powder. 他碰到清潔粉後，皮膚出現紅癢反應。
- After: She could not hide her irritation when the bus was late again. 公車又誤點，她掩不住心中的惱怒。
- Why: 最常見的意思是「惱怒」，原主要詞義「刺激反應」不是最常用義。

### Core 8 · universal

- Before: A smile is not universal, but it often softens awkward moments. 微笑並非普遍通用，卻常能緩和尷尬時刻。
- After: A smile is universal, and it often softens awkward moments between strangers. 微笑是普遍的，常能緩和陌生人之間的尷尬時刻。
- Why: 原例句語意不自然，且 universal 作形容詞時以「普遍的」為主，故修正。

### Core 8 · scope

- Before: The scope of our science project became much larger than we planned. 我們自然科專題的範圍比原先計畫的大得多。
- After: The scope of the school project was too large for one weekend. 這個學校專題的範圍太大，一個週末做不完。
- Why: scope 最常見的意思是「範圍」，不是「眼界」。

### Core 8 · parking

- Before: The hotel offers parking behind the terminal on weekdays. 這家旅館平日在碼頭後方提供停車。
- After: The hotel offers free parking behind the building on weekdays. 這家旅館平日在大樓後方提供免費停車。
- Why: 原例句 terminal 與碼頭翻譯不一致，補充義也需修正。

### Core 8 · conservation

- Before: Water conservation saved the village during three rainless weeks. 三週無雨期間，節水讓整個村子撐了過來。
- After: Water conservation saved the village during three rainless weeks. 三週無雨期間，節約用水讓整個村子撐了過來。
- Why: conservation 最常見義為「保育」，「節約」應列為補充義。

### Core 10 · daylight

- Before: By daylight, the broken pier looked more dangerous than romantic. 天亮後破碼頭看來危險多於浪漫。
- After: By daylight, the broken pier looked more dangerous than romantic. 天亮後，那座破碼頭看起來危險多於浪漫。
- Why: daylight 最常見義為日光，「白天」較不精確；例句中譯「破碼頭」也不自然。

### Core 10 · tutor

- Before: Her tutor noticed the fever before the piano lesson began. 她的家教在鋼琴課前發現她發燒。
- After: Her tutor noticed the fever before the piano lesson began. 她的家教老師在鋼琴課開始前發現她發燒了。
- Why: 「導師」易與班導師混淆，宜改為助教；例句中譯補足語氣使其自然。

### Core 8 · testify

- Before: Two neighbors will testify about the noise before sunrise. 兩名鄰居將作證說明日出前的噪音。
- After: Two neighbors will testify about the noise before sunrise. 兩名鄰居將就日出前的噪音作證。
- Why: exampleZh 語序不自然，「作證說明噪音」誤譯。

### Core 8 · horrible

- Before: A horrible smell rose from the drain after the flood. 水災過後，排水孔冒出一股惡臭。
- After: A horrible smell rose from the drain after the flood. 水災過後，排水孔冒出一股可怕的臭味。
- Why: 譯文漏掉 horrible 的意思，應譯出「可怕的」。

### Core 8 · telescope

- Before: The telescope revealed craters along the moon's bright edge. 望遠鏡顯示月亮亮邊上的坑洞。
- After: The telescope revealed craters along the moon's bright edge. 望遠鏡讓人看見月亮明亮邊緣的坑洞。
- Why: 譯文「亮邊上」不自然，revealed 語意也未完整傳達。

### Core 9 · conceal

- Before: Heavy curtains conceal the tiny kitchen from street view. 厚窗簾遮住從街上可見的小廚房。
- After: Heavy curtains conceal the tiny kitchen from street view. 厚重的窗簾把小廚房遮住，從街上看不到。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「隱藏」，「遮住」較次要；例句中文也不夠自然。

### Core 9 · outlet

- Before: Running became her outlet after long shifts at the hospital. 醫院長班後跑步成了她的紓壓出口。
- After: Running became her outlet after long shifts at the hospital. 在醫院輪完長班後，跑步成了她紓壓的出口。
- Why: 最常見詞義應為「出口」，發洩管道為延伸義；例句中文稍不自然。

### Core 5 · violent

- Before: A violent wave knocked the camera from the ferry rail. 猛烈海浪把相機從渡輪欄杆打落。
- After: A violent wave knocked the camera from the ferry rail. 猛烈的海浪把相機從渡輪欄杆上打落了。
- Why: violent 最常見的意思是「暴力的」，應作為主要詞義；「猛烈的」改列補充。

### Core 5 · slide

- Before: Children waited for the blue slide after the rain stopped. 雨停後孩子排隊等藍色溜滑梯。
- After: Children waited for the blue slide after the rain stopped. 雨停後，孩子們排隊等著玩藍色的溜滑梯。
- Why: slide 為 n/vt/vi，主要詞義應為「滑動」，「溜滑梯」僅是名詞用法，改列補充。

### Core 11 · superficial

- Before: His apology felt superficial because he never named the damage. 他的道歉很表面，因為從未說出造成的傷害。
- After: His apology felt superficial because he never named the damage. 他的道歉很膚淺，因為他從未說出造成的傷害。
- Why: 例句語意為「膚淺」，主要詞義應為膚淺的；「表面的」列為補充。

### Core 8 · bold

- Before: Her bold red scarf stood out in the gray crowd. 她那條大膽的紅圍巾在灰色調的人群中格外顯眼。
- After: Her bold red scarf stood out in the gray crowd. 她那條醒目的紅圍巾在灰暗的人群中很突出。
- Why: 例句中 bold 指醒目而非大膽，中文翻譯與主要詞義不符且「人群灰色中」不自然。

### Core 4 · consequence

- Before: One consequence of the drought was smaller dragon fruit. 乾旱的一個結果是火龍果變小。
- After: One consequence of the drought was smaller dragon fruit. 乾旱的一個後果是火龍果變小。
- Why: consequence 常指負面的後果，補充義宜加入「後果」，譯文亦宜用「後果」。

### Core 13 · polar

- Before: The twins held polar opinions about selling the orchard. 雙胞胎對出售果園持完全相反的意見。
- After: The twins held polar opinions about selling the orchard. 雙胞胎對出售果園持截然相反的意見。
- Why: meaningZh 非最常見詞義，「極地的」才是 polar 作形容詞的首要意思，已調整。

### Core 13 · devastate

- Before: One careless spark could devastate the entire pine forest. 一點火星就可能毀壞整片松林。
- After: One careless spark could devastate the entire pine forest. 一點火星就可能毀掉整片松林。
- Why: 補充詞義「使震驚」不夠精確，應為「使悲痛」；譯文也略作修正。

### Core 9 · texture

- Before: The soup had a silky texture from blended tofu. 這湯因打成泥的豆腐而口感滑順。
- After: The soup had a silky texture from blended tofu. 這湯因為加了打成泥的豆腐，質地滑順。
- Why: texture 最常見意思是「質地」，「口感」不應作為主要詞義。

### Core 3 · sight

- Before: The first sight of snow made Grandpa silent today. 第一次看見雪，讓爺爺今天一時說不出話來。
- After: The first sight of snow made Grandpa silent today. 爺爺今天第一次看見雪，感動得一時說不出話。
- Why: sight 作名詞主要義為視力或景象，「看見」不是最常見名詞義；例句譯文也與原句不符。

### Core 3 · wave

- Before: A sudden wave soaked our shoes on the pier. 一道突如其來的浪打濕了我們在碼頭上的鞋子。
- After: A sudden wave soaked our shoes on the pier. 突來的一個浪打濕了我們在碼頭上的鞋子。
- Why: wave 最常見義為波浪，「浪花」較不精確，譯文也不自然。

### Core 3 · thinking

- Before: Clear thinking helped her escape the room filled with smoke. 清晰的思考幫助她逃出充滿濃煙的房間。
- After: Clear thinking helped her escape the room filled with smoke. 冷靜的思考幫助她逃出充滿濃煙的房間。
- Why: 補充義「有理性的」不常見且不準確，改為「思維方式」；例句翻譯漏掉「充滿」並不自然。

### Core 3 · leading

- Before: The leading dancer stood at the front of the line. 領頭舞者站在隊伍最前方。
- After: The leading dancer stood at the front of the line. 領頭的舞者站在隊伍最前方。
- Why: 補充義「領導」詞性不符，應為形容詞「領先的」。

### Core 3 · narrow

- Before: A narrow lane led to the hidden teahouse. 一條狹窄巷子通往隱密的茶館。
- After: A narrow lane led to the hidden teahouse. 一條狹窄的巷子通往隱密的茶館。
- Why: 補充義「變窄」與「有限的」不夠準確，改為常見義；譯文補上「的」較自然。

### Core 3 · succeed

- Before: She hopes to succeed her father as temple keeper. 她希望接替父親成為廟宇管理人。
- After: She worked hard to succeed in her English exam. 她努力讀書，希望英文考試能成功。
- Why: succeed最常見義為「成功」，應作主要義，例句也應配合。

### Core 3 · impose

- Before: Officials may impose fines for dumping trash here. 官員可能對在此亂倒垃圾開罰。
- After: Officials may impose fines for dumping trash here. 官員可能對在此亂倒垃圾的人處以罰款。
- Why: impose最常見義為「強加」；例句翻譯也漏掉「罰款」的完整語意。

### Core 12 · barrack

- Before: Soldiers returned to the barrack before heavy rain. 士兵在大雨前回到兵舍。
- After: The soldiers returned to the barrack before the heavy rain started. 士兵們在大雨開始前回到了兵營。
- Why: 「兵舍」非台灣慣用詞，應為「兵營」；例句過短不符字數，補充義也宜修正。

### Core 12 · made

- Before: This school bag is made from recycled plastic bottles. 這個書包是用回收塑膠瓶製成的。
- After: This table is made of recycled wood from old school desks. 這張桌子是用舊課桌的回收木材製成的。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「製成的」，「鋪好的」並非最常見義，例句用法也不自然。

### Core 12 · tempted

- Before: Nina was tempted to steal buns when hunger grew. 飢餓加劇時，妮娜受到誘惑想偷包子。
- After: Nina was tempted to buy another snack when she passed the night market. 妮娜經過夜市時，很想再買一份點心。
- Why: 原例句「偷包子」情境不適合；詞性標示 v 亦不合，應為形容詞。

### Core 12 · home

- Before: The rescue boat brought every fisherman home before dark. 救援船在天黑前把每位漁夫送回家。
- After: Mia walked home after cram school because the last bus had already left. 最後一班公車已開走，所以咪雅補習後走路回家。
- Why: 最常見的主要詞義應為名詞「家」，且例句用法與主要詞義不一致。

### Core 12 · minute

- Before: A minute crack appeared near the porcelain handle. 瓷把手旁出現一道細小裂痕。
- After: Please wait a minute while I find my student ID card. 請等一分鐘，我找一下我的學生證。
- Why: 主要詞義應為最常見的「分鐘」，「細小的」是較少見的形容詞義。

### Core 12 · bank

- Before: Children slid down the grassy bank after school. 放學後，孩子們從長草的河岸滑下。
- After: My mother goes to the bank every Friday to deposit money. 我媽媽每週五都會去銀行存錢。
- Why: 最常見的主要詞義應為「銀行」，「河岸」應列為補充義。

### Core 12 · condom

- Before: The nurse placed a condom in each travel health kit. 護理師在每份旅行保健包放入一個保險套。
- After: The health teacher explained how a condom helps prevent disease. 健康教育老師說明保險套如何幫助預防疾病。
- Why: 例句情境不自然，旅行保健包放保險套不貼近青少年日常。

### Core 1 · half

- Before: She ate half of the sandwich before swimming. 游泳前她吃了半個三明治。
- After: She ate half of the sandwich before going swimming with us. 游泳前她吃了半個三明治。
- Why: 補充義「半場」與「部分地」不夠常見且不準確，應改為「半個；一半地」。

### Core 14 · helper

- Before: A quiet helper guided elders across the flooded street. 一名沉默寡言的助手，帶著長者穿過淹水的街道。
- After: A quiet helper guided elders across the flooded street. 一名安靜的幫手帶著長者穿過淹水的街道。
- Why: 例句譯文把 helper 譯成「助手」，與主要詞義「幫手」不一致；「沉默寡言」也不自然。

### Core 14 · orderly

- Before: Patients waited in an orderly line outside the clinic. 病人在診所外排成整齊隊伍等候。
- After: Patients waited in an orderly line outside the clinic. 病人在診所外排成整齊的隊伍等候。
- Why: 詞性標示含 adv 不妥，補充詞義缺少常見的「有秩序的」，譯文漏「的」略不順。

### Core 6 · cutting

- Before: The cutting of the ribbon took place before noon. 剪綵在中午前舉行。
- After: She kept a cutting from the newspaper about the school festival. 她留著一份關於校慶的報紙剪報。
- Why: 例句 cutting of the ribbon 不自然，且與詞義「切割」不符；改用剪報例句。

### Core 6 · whip

- Before: The baker used a whisk to whip the cream. 麵包師用打蛋器攪打鮮奶油。
- After: The rider held a whip while the horse trotted around the field. 馬在場上小跑時，騎士手裡拿著一根鞭子。
- Why: whip 最常見的意思是鞭子或鞭打，「攪打」較少見，不宜為主要詞義。

### Core 6 · slim

- Before: A slim chance remained after the ferry departed. 渡輪離開後，只剩微小機會。
- After: She stayed slim by walking to school every day. 她每天走路上學，所以保持苗條。
- Why: slim 最常見的意思是「苗條的」，「微小的」應列為補充。

### Core 7 · absent

- Before: Her brother was absent from the family dinner again because of work. 她哥哥又因為工作缺席了家庭聚餐。
- After: Her brother was absent from the family portrait again. 她哥哥又沒有出現在全家合照中。
- Why: 例句 absent 用法不自然，改為更自然的 absent from 用法並修正翻譯。

### Core 7 · nonsense

- Before: His excuse about invisible ink was pure nonsense. 他說隱形墨水的藉口完全荒唐。
- After: His excuse about invisible ink was pure nonsense. 他說隱形墨水的藉口純屬胡說八道。
- Why: nonsense 最常見義為胡說八道，原主要義「荒唐」偏形容詞。

### Core 7 · devoted

- Before: A devoted nurse visited the old fisherman every morning. 一位盡心護理師每天早上探望老漁夫。
- After: A devoted nurse visited the old fisherman every morning. 一位盡心盡力的護理師每天早上探望老漁夫。
- Why: exampleZh 語句不順，缺少「盡力」的語氣。

### Core 14 · curtail

- Before: New rules may curtail late-night karaoke near hospitals. 新規定可能限制醫院附近深夜卡拉OK。
- After: New rules may curtail late-night karaoke near hospitals. 新規定可能縮減醫院附近的深夜卡拉OK營業。
- Why: curtail 主要義為縮減；「剝奪」不常見；譯文漏了營業且不自然。

### Core 14 · thwart

- Before: Locked gates could not thwart the determined hikers. 上鎖大門無法阻撓下定決心的健行者。
- After: Locked gates could not thwart the determined hikers. 上鎖的大門無法阻撓下定決心的健行者。
- Why: 補充義遺漏常見義項；譯文不夠自然，缺少「的」。

### Core 14 · monumental

- Before: A monumental arch marked the entrance to the old cemetery. 一座紀念碑式拱門標示老墓園入口。
- After: Her monumental achievement changed science education across the country. 她劃時代的成就改變了全國的科學教育。
- Why: monumental 最常用義為不朽、巨大；原例句語意偏冷僻。

### Core 14 · luxurious

- Before: The luxurious train cabin had silk curtains and orchids. 豪華列車包廂有絲窗簾和蘭花。
- After: The luxurious train cabin had silk curtains and orchids. 豪華列車包廂裡有絲質窗簾和蘭花。
- Why: 譯文「絲窗簾」不自然，應為「絲質窗簾」。

### Core 14 · buffer

- Before: A row of bamboo served as a buffer against traffic noise. 一排竹子成了阻隔車聲的緩衝帶。
- After: A row of bamboo served as a buffer against traffic noise. 一排竹子成了阻隔車聲的緩衝物。
- Why: 主要詞義應為通用的「緩衝物」，「緩衝帶」過於狹隘；補充加入動詞義。

### Core 14 · deposition

- Before: Her deposition described the crash in calm detail. 她的證詞冷靜詳細地描述那場撞車。
- After: Heavy rain increased the deposition of mud at the river mouth. 大雨使河口的泥沙沉積增加了。
- Why: deposition 最常見意義應為沉積；證詞較專門，故調整主要義並改寫例句

### Core 14 · crater

- Before: Rainwater filled the crater left by the explosion. 雨水填滿爆炸留下的彈坑。
- After: Hikers stood at the edge of the crater and looked down. 登山客站在火山口邊緣往下看。
- Why: 最常見意義是火山口，彈坑較少見，不宜作主要義

### Core 14 · foe

- Before: The boxer greeted his former foe with a quick hug. 拳擊手用短暫擁抱問候昔日對手。
- After: The boxer greeted his former foe with a quick hug. 拳擊手用短暫擁抱問候昔日的對手。
- Why: foe 最常見意義為敵人，對手應為補充義

### Core 14 · boiler

- Before: A leaking boiler forced the hotel to close early. 漏水鍋爐迫使旅館提早關門。
- After: A leaking boiler forced the hotel to close early. 漏水的鍋爐迫使旅館提早關門。
- Why: 中文翻譯不夠自然，缺少「的」

### Core 4 · democratic

- Before: The island council chose a democratic process for sharing fishing rights. 島上議會用民主程序分配捕魚權。
- After: The island council chose a democratic process for sharing fishing rights. 島上議會選擇以民主程序來分配捕魚權。
- Why: 中文漏譯 chose（選擇採用），且「程序」宜用台灣用語。

### Core 4 · involved

- Before: The repair was too involved for me to finish before dinner. 這項維修太複雜，我晚餐前做不完。
- After: Several students were involved in organizing the school charity sale last week. 上週有幾位學生參與籌辦學校的慈善義賣。
- Why: 最常見意義是牽涉其中的，複雜的應列為補充義。

### Core 4 · democrat

- Before: A democrat from Kaohsiung promised cheaper buses for elderly residents. 一位來自高雄的民主派人士承諾，會降低長者的公車票價。
- After: A democrat from Kaohsiung promised cheaper buses for elderly residents. 一位來自高雄的民主主義者承諾，會降低長者的公車票價。
- Why: 主要意義應為民主主義者，例句譯文與詞義也需一致。

### Core 7 · sour

- Before: The milk smelled sour after hours in the sun. 牛奶曬了幾小時後聞起來酸臭。
- After: The milk smelled sour after hours in the sun. 牛奶曬了幾小時後聞起來酸酸的。
- Why: sour 最常見意思是「酸的」，「酸臭的」不是最主要的意思，應調整順序。

### Core 7 · lazy

- Before: A lazy afternoon settled over the fishing port. 慵懶午後籠罩漁港。
- After: A lazy afternoon settled over the fishing port. 慵懶的午後籠罩著漁港。
- Why: lazy 最常見意思是「懶惰的」，「慵懶的」應放在補充意思。

### Core 7 · backward

- Before: The old truck rolled backward toward the ditch. 老卡車向後滑向水溝。
- After: The old truck rolled backward toward the ditch. 那輛老卡車向後滑向水溝。
- Why: adv 的 backward 應為「向後地」，並補充常見的倒過來意思。

### Core 10 · intercourse

- Before: Trade created regular intercourse between the two port cities. 貿易往來，讓兩座港口城市定期交流。
- After: Trade created regular intercourse between the two port cities. 貿易使這兩座港口城市之間有了定期的往來。
- Why: 此字最常見意義為往來；原例句中文漏譯並與主要詞義不符，且此字不適合高中生。

### Core 10 · stark

- Before: The empty shelves offered a stark warning before the storm. 空貨架在風暴前成了鮮明警訊。
- After: The empty shelves offered a stark warning before the storm. 風暴來臨前，空貨架發出了鮮明的警訊。
- Why: stark 作形容詞最常見為荒涼、嚴酷；鮮明為次要詞義，且 全然的 不如 完全的 自然。

### Core 10 · plague

- Before: Mosquitoes plague the riverside apartments every humid summer evening. 每個潮濕夏夜蚊子都困擾河邊公寓住戶。
- After: The plague spread quickly through the crowded medieval city. 瘟疫在擁擠的中世紀城市中迅速蔓延。
- Why: plague 最常見詞義為名詞的瘟疫，主要詞義應與詞性 n 對應。

### Core 13 · auditor

- Before: An auditor found the missing receipt under the cash drawer. 審計員在收銀抽屜下找到遺失收據。
- After: An auditor found the missing receipt under the cash drawer. 審計員在收銀抽屜下找到遺失的收據。
- Why: 此項無明顯錯誤，但依指示不應列入

### Core 5 · tight

- Before: The lid was tight after sitting in the refrigerator overnight. 蓋子在冰箱裡放了一夜後，變得很難打開。
- After: The lid was tight after sitting in the refrigerator overnight. 蓋子在冰箱裡放了一夜後變得很緊。
- Why: meaningZh「很緊」不是詞典式釋義，且與補充義重複；例句翻譯也加入了原文沒有的「難打開」。

### Core 2 · executive

- Before: The executive committee approved repairs for the old market. 執行委員會核准了老市場的修繕計畫。
- After: The executive of the company spoke to the students. 公司的主管對學生們發表了談話。
- Why: 詞性以名詞開頭，主要義應為「主管」，不是「執行的」。

### Core 2 · village

- Before: My grandparents live in a small village near the mountains. 我的祖父母住在山邊的一個小村莊。
- After: The village bakery opens before the first bus. 村裡麵包店在首班公車前開門。
- Why: village 主要為名詞「村莊」，「村裡的」不是常見主要義。

### Core 2 · focus

- Before: Turn off your phone so you can focus on your homework. 把手機關掉，你才能專心寫作業。
- After: Focus the camera on the bride's hands during the vows. 誓詞時把相機焦點對準新娘的手。
- Why: 主要義應為常見的「焦點」，「對焦」較次要。

### Core 2 · structure

- Before: The bamboo structure survived three typhoons without damage. 這座竹造棚架挺過三次颱風沒受損。
- After: The bamboo structure survived three typhoons without damage. 這座竹造結構物挺過三次颱風，沒有受損。
- Why: meaningZh「結構物」不是最常見的名詞義，應為「結構」；補充義重複，例句中譯也應對應。

### Core 2 · theory

- Before: Our science teacher explained the theory with a simple experiment. 自然老師用一個簡單的實驗說明這個理論。
- After: His theory about the missing boat sounded possible. 他對那艘失蹤船隻的推測，聽起來不無可能。
- Why: meaningZh「推測」不是 theory 最常見的意思，應為「理論」。

### Core 2 · federal

- Before: Federal rules protect migratory birds across state borders. 聯邦規則保護跨州遷徙鳥類。
- After: Federal rules protect migratory birds across state borders. 聯邦法規保護跨州遷徙的鳥類。
- Why: 補充義「聯合的」不恰當；中譯「規則」稍不自然且漏掉「的」。

### Core 10 · fitting

- Before: A simple wooden frame was fitting for the old photograph. 簡單木框很適合那張老照片。
- After: A simple wooden frame is fitting for the old photograph. 簡單的木框很適合那張老照片。
- Why: fitting 作形容詞時，例句應用現在式 is；「試穿」與「配件」為名詞義，且原例句時態不自然。

### Core 10 · imaginative

- Before: Her imaginative drawings turned our classroom wall into an underwater world. 她充滿想像力的畫作，把我們教室的牆變成了海底世界。
- After: The teacher asked for an imaginative scene, not a real memory. 老師要的是想像出來的情境，而不是真實的回憶。
- Why: 主要意思應為「有想像力的」，「想像中的」不是最常見義；「虛構的」過於罕見。

### Core 10 · groan

- Before: The old stairs groan whenever anyone carries luggage up. 有人提行李上樓時老樓梯就呻吟般作響。
- After: The old stairs groan whenever anyone carries luggage up. 每當有人提行李上樓，老樓梯就會嘎吱作響。
- Why: 主要義應為「呻吟」，「呻吟般作響」不是常見詞義，譯文也不自然。

### Core 9 · supervision

- Before: Children may swim here only under adult supervision. 孩子只能在成人監督下在此游泳。
- After: Children may swim here only under adult supervision. 孩子只能在大人的監督下在此游泳。
- Why: 「成人監督下」生硬，應改為較自然的「大人的監督下」。

### Core 9 · outfit

- Before: She bought a new outfit for her cousin's wedding. 她為了表姊的婚禮買了一套新衣服。
- After: She wore a bright outfit to the school dance. 她穿著一套亮麗的服裝去參加學校舞會。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「服裝」而非「機構」，例句詞義也需配合。

### Core 9 · notebook

- Before: His notebook smelled faintly of ink and rain. 他的筆記本有淡淡墨水和雨水味。
- After: His notebook smelled faintly of ink and rain. 他的筆記本有淡淡的墨水和雨水味。
- Why: 「筆電」過於口語，補充義改為「筆記型電腦」；中文補上「的」較自然。

### Core 13 · brow

- Before: Sweat gathered on Omar's brow as the oven door opened. 烤箱門打開時，汗水聚在奧瑪額頭上。
- After: Sweat gathered on Omar's brow as the oven door opened. 烤箱門打開時，汗水聚在奧瑪的額頭上。
- Why: brow 主要指眉毛，額頭為次要義；主要義與補充義應調整。

### Core 7 · jam

- Before: A traffic jam trapped the ambulance near the bridge. 交通堵塞讓救護車困在橋邊。
- After: She spread strawberry jam on her toast before going to school. 她上學前在吐司上抹了草莓果醬。
- Why: jam 名詞最常見的意思是果醬，不是堵塞；例句也需配合主要詞義。

### Core 7 · alcoholic

- Before: All alcoholic drinks are locked away during the school fair. 校慶期間所有酒精飲料都被鎖起來。
- After: All alcoholic drinks are locked away during the school fair. 校慶期間所有含酒精的飲料都被鎖起來。
- Why: 「酒精的」不自然，應為「含酒精的」；例句譯文也需配合修正。

### Core 7 · approximate

- Before: The approximate cost includes paint, tiles, and delivery. 估算費用包含油漆磁磚和運費。
- After: The approximate cost includes paint, tiles, and delivery. 大約的費用包含油漆、磁磚和運費。
- Why: 「粗估」詞性不符；譯文漏譯 approximate 且標點不自然。

### Core 7 · irritate

- Before: Dry dust can irritate the eyes during harvest. 收割時乾塵會刺激眼睛。
- After: Loud chewing can irritate me during quiet exams. 安靜的考試中，大聲咀嚼會讓我很煩。
- Why: irritate 最常見的意思是惹惱，刺激非首要義；例句也需配合。

### Core 9 · vacuum

- Before: A tiny vacuum cleaned crumbs from the taxi seat. 小型吸塵器清掉計程車座位上的碎屑。
- After: Astronauts float in a vacuum because there is no air around them. 太空人漂浮在真空中，因為周圍沒有空氣。
- Why: vacuum 最常見詞義為「真空」，吸塵器應為補充義，例句也需配合主要詞義。

### Core 9 · remainder

- Before: She packed the remainder of dinner for lunch. 她把晚餐剩下的部分打包當午餐。
- After: She packed the remainder of the dinner for lunch the next day. 她把晚餐剩下的部分打包留作隔天的午餐。
- Why: 詞性標示含 v 與 a 不恰當，且例句缺少 the，英文不夠自然；中文亦需補足句意。

### Core 9 · provincial

- Before: The provincial museum displayed tools from sugar farms. 省立博物館展示糖業農場工具。
- After: The provincial museum displayed old tools from local sugar farms. 省立博物館展示了來自當地糖業農場的舊工具。
- Why: 「省級的」偏大陸用語，且例句翻譯漏掉 local 與 old。

### Core 9 · furious

- Before: She was furious when the ferry left early. 渡船提早開走時她氣炸了。
- After: She was furious when the ferry left without her. 渡船沒等她就開走，她氣得暴跳如雷。
- Why: 原例句 left early 與翻譯「氣炸了」不夠準確對應，且語氣偏口語，改為更清楚的句子。

### Core 10 · virgin

- Before: The chef used virgin olive oil on the tomato salad. 主廚在番茄沙拉上用初榨橄欖油。
- After: Hikers found a virgin forest far from any road. 登山客在遠離道路的地方發現一片原始森林。
- Why: 主要詞義「初榨的」並非最常見的意思，且範例與主要詞義不符，已修正。

### Core 5 · leather

- Before: A leather bag cracked after years in humid weather. 皮包多年受潮後裂開。
- After: A leather bag cracked after years in humid weather. 皮包在潮濕天氣放了多年後裂開了。
- Why: 名詞主要意義應為「皮革」而非形容詞；例句翻譯漏掉 humid 與 years 的語意。

### Core 6 · counsel

- Before: Her counsel advised silence until the warrant arrived. 她的法律顧問建議在搜索令到前保持沉默。
- After: She followed her teacher's counsel and chose the safer route. 她聽從老師的建議，選了比較安全的路線。
- Why: counsel 最常見的意思是「建議」，「法律顧問」較少用，應調整主要詞義與例句。

### Core 11 · skate

- Before: Children skate around the pond when the winter ice thickens. 冬天池面的冰變厚時，孩子們就繞著池塘滑冰。
- After: Children skate around the pond when the winter ice thickens. 冬天冰層變厚時，孩子們繞著池塘滑冰。
- Why: 詞性n/vi，名詞義應為冰鞋，主要義項順序不當；例句中文「冬冰」不自然。

### Core 11 · booking

- Before: Our booking at the seafood restaurant is for seven o'clock on Saturday. 我們在海鮮餐廳的訂位是星期六晚上七點。
- After: Her booking at the hotel includes two nights in Taipei. 她在飯店的預訂包含在台北住兩晚。
- Why: booking最常見義為預訂，演出預約不是主要義。

### Core 11 · clearing

- Before: They found a sunny clearing beyond the last pine trees. 他們在最後一排松樹後找到一片陽光空地。
- After: They found a sunny clearing beyond the last pine trees. 他們在最後一排松樹後面找到一片陽光普照的空地。
- Why: 「清算」不適合作補充義，宜改為「結算」。

### Core 13 · secular

- Before: A secular charity rented the church hall for winter meals. 一個世俗慈善團體租下教堂大廳供應冬餐。
- After: A secular charity rented the church hall for winter meals. 一個非宗教的慈善團體租下教堂大廳供應冬季餐點。
- Why: 例句翻譯將 secular 譯為「世俗」不貼切，應與主要詞義「非宗教的」一致。

### Core 8 · jack

- Before: A rusty jack lifted the van enough to change tires. 生鏽千斤頂把廂型車頂高到足以換胎。
- After: A rusty jack lifted the van enough to change tires. 生鏽的千斤頂把廂型車頂高到足以換輪胎。
- Why: 詞性標示不符且例句中文漏掉「的」讀來不自然；補充意義可更完整。

### Core 8 · terror

- Before: The sudden blackout filled the tunnel with terror. 隧道突然停電，讓在場的人十分恐慌。
- After: The sudden blackout filled the tunnel with terror. 隧道突然停電，讓隧道裡充滿了恐懼。
- Why: 例句翻譯「在場的人十分恐慌」未忠實對應 filled the tunnel with terror。

### Core 8 · companion

- Before: His dog became a quiet companion during months of recovery. 復原數月期間，他的狗成了安靜伴侶。
- After: His dog became a quiet companion during months of recovery. 在休養的幾個月裡，他的狗成了安靜的同伴。
- Why: companion 最常見意思是同伴，伴侶較少用；例句中文也略不自然。

### Core 7 · engaged

- Before: The phone line stayed engaged through the storm. 暴風雨中電話線一直占線。
- After: She was engaged to her classmate before they finished university. 她在大學畢業前就和同班同學訂婚了。
- Why: 最常見詞義是「訂婚的」，「佔線」為次要義，故調整主要與補充詞義及例句。

### Core 7 · bin

- Before: Place the broken glass in the metal bin. 把碎玻璃放進金屬桶裡。
- After: Please throw the empty bottles into the recycling bin. 請把空瓶丟進資源回收桶。
- Why: bin 最常見意思為垃圾桶，原主要詞義過於籠統，補充詞義重複。

### Core 7 · bent

- Before: A bent spoon lay under the noodle cart. 彎掉的湯匙躺在麵攤下。
- After: A bent spoon lay under the noodle cart. 一支彎曲的湯匙躺在麵攤下。
- Why: 「彎掉的」用語不自然，應為「彎曲的」；補充詞義「興趣」不精確。

### Core 7 · customs

- Before: Airport customs opened her suitcase after the long flight. 長途飛行後機場海關打開她的手提箱。
- After: The customs officer opened her suitcase after the long flight. 長途飛行後，海關人員打開了她的行李箱。
- Why: 原例句「手提箱」與原文 suitcase 不精確，且 customs 搭配不自然，補充常見義「風俗」。

### Core 7 · typically

- Before: The design is typically Taiwanese, with tiles and shaded arcades. 這設計很有台灣特色，有磁磚和騎樓。
- After: Students typically arrive at school before eight in the morning. 學生通常在早上八點前到校。
- Why: 例句 typically 的意思是「典型地」，與最常見義「通常」不符，已調整。

### Core 7 · closed

- Before: The closed bakery still smelled of butter after midnight. 午夜過後，關了門的麵包店仍飄著奶油香。
- After: The closed bakery still smelled of butter after midnight. 午夜過後，打烊的麵包店仍飄著奶油香。
- Why: 原中文「關門麵包店」不自然，詞義與譯文需調整。

### Core 2 · significant

- Before: A significant crack appeared in the old sea wall. 舊海堤出現一道明顯裂縫。
- After: A significant crack appeared in the old sea wall. 舊海堤出現一道明顯的裂縫。
- Why: meaningZh 最常見的意思應為「重要的」；「明顯」改列為補充義「顯著的」。

### Core 2 · whatever

- Before: Take whatever fruit is ripe from the basket. 籃中熟了的水果你都可以拿。
- After: Take whatever fruit is ripe from the basket. 籃子裡任何熟的水果你都可以拿。
- Why: 主要詞義含「……」不自然，且 whatever 在例句中作限定詞；中文已調整。

### Core 9 · satisfactory

- Before: The repair was satisfactory, though the radio still buzzed. 維修算令人滿意但收音機仍有雜音。
- After: The repair was satisfactory, though the radio still buzzed. 維修結果還算令人滿意，不過收音機仍有雜音。
- Why: 譯文漏掉語氣與連接，讀來不自然，已補全。

### Core 9 · scramble

- Before: Tourists scramble up the rocks to watch sunrise. 遊客爬上岩石去看日出。
- After: Fans scramble for tickets as soon as the concert goes on sale. 演唱會一開賣，歌迷就爭搶門票。
- Why: 名詞與動詞最常見義為爭搶，攀爬不是主要意思，例句也需配合。

### Core 9 · drill

- Before: The dentist's drill whined behind the blue curtain. 牙醫的鑽子在藍簾後嗡嗡響。
- After: The dentist's drill whined behind the blue curtain. 牙醫的鑽孔機在藍簾後嗡嗡作響。
- Why: 「鑽子」不是台灣常用說法，應為鑽孔機。

### Core 13 · preclude

- Before: Heavy fog may preclude helicopter rescue before noon. 濃霧可能導致中午前無法出動直升機救援。
- After: Heavy fog may preclude helicopter rescue before noon. 濃霧可能使中午前無法出動直升機救援。
- Why: preclude 最常見意思為排除或使不可能，原例句譯文「導致」不精確，已修正。

### Core 13 · fellowship

- Before: The fellowship paid for Ana's research in rural clinics. 研究獎助金支付安娜在鄉間診所的研究費用。
- After: The club's fellowship made every new student feel welcome at school. 社團的情誼讓每位新生在學校都感到受歡迎。
- Why: 主要意思應為友誼或夥伴情誼，獎助金較少用，且例句需配合主要意思。

### Core 4 · sky

- Before: The sky turned orange above the fishing boats. 漁船上空染成了一片橘紅。
- After: The sky turned orange above the fishing boats. 漁船上空的天空染成了一片橘紅。
- Why: 例句譯文漏譯 sky（天空），讀起來也不夠完整。

### Core 4 · contribute

- Before: Local cooks contribute soup whenever floods close the road. 淹水封路時，當地廚師都會捐湯幫忙。
- After: Local cooks contribute soup whenever floods close the road. 淹水封路時，當地廚師都會捐出湯品幫忙。
- Why: 譯文「捐湯」不自然，應改為「捐出湯品」。

### Core 1 · return

- Before: His return brought comfort to the worried family. 他回來後，焦急的家人終於放下心來。
- After: I will return to school on Monday after my short trip. 短途旅行結束後，我星期一會回學校。
- Why: 例句用名詞，與詞性順序及動詞意義不符，已改為動詞用法；補充釋義修正。

### Core 10 · splash

- Before: A splash of seawater hit the camera lens. 一陣海水濺到相機鏡頭。
