- After: A splash of seawater hit the camera lens. 一陣海水濺到相機鏡頭上。
- Why: 主要意義不夠常見，補充的「醒目色塊」太罕見，且例句翻譯不自然。

### Core 10 · nutrient

- Before: Iron is a nutrient many toddlers lack during rapid growth. 鐵是許多幼兒快速成長時缺乏的營養素。
- After: Iron is a nutrient many toddlers lack during rapid growth. 鐵是許多幼兒在快速成長期間缺乏的營養素。
- Why: nutrient 詞性有誤導，補充義「滋養的」罕見；譯文宜完整。

### Core 2 · despite

- Before: Despite the heat, Grandpa walked to the shrine. 儘管天氣炎熱，阿公仍走到小廟去。
- After: Despite the heat, Grandpa walked to the shrine. 儘管天氣炎熱，阿公仍走到神龕。
- Why: despite 詞性應為 prep，不是 n / prep。

### Core 2 · anyway

- Before: It was raining hard, but we went anyway. 雨下得很大但我們還是去了。
- After: It was raining hard, but we went anyway. 雨下得很大，但我們還是去了。
- Why: anyway 最常見的意思是「無論如何」，「還是」不宜作主要詞義。

### Core 13 · heading

- Before: The bold heading warned hikers about sudden rockfall. 粗體標題提醒健行者小心突發落石。
- After: The bold heading at the top of the page told students where to begin. 頁面頂端的粗體標題告訴學生從哪裡開始。
- Why: 例句原本的落石警告與標題義不夠自然，已改為日常情境；補充義「方向」改為更精確的「前進方向」。

### Core 13 · submission

- Before: Online submission closes at midnight, so upload the form now. 線上提交午夜截止，所以現在就上傳表格。
- After: Online submission closes at midnight, so upload the form now. 線上提交將在午夜截止，所以現在就上傳表格吧。
- Why: 「意見」並非 submission 常見義，應為投稿作品；例句翻譯也應更完整自然。

### Core 1 · against

- Before: The ladder rested against the mango tree during harvest. 採芒果時梯子靠在芒果樹上。
- After: Many students were against the plan to shorten the lunch break. 許多學生反對縮短午休時間的計畫。
- Why: against 最常見的詞義是「反對」而非「靠著」，例句也一併改為此義。

### Core 4 · definition

- Before: Everyone has a different definition of success in life. 每個人對人生成功的定義都不一樣。
- After: The dictionary gives a clear definition of every difficult word in this lesson. 字典為這一課每個困難的單字都提供了清楚的定義。
- Why: 最常見的意思是「定義」，「解析度」是次要意思，主要與補充意思顛倒。

### Core 4 · discipline

- Before: Daily discipline helped him practice calligraphy before breakfast. 他每天嚴格自律，早餐前都會練習書法。
- After: Daily discipline helped him practice calligraphy before breakfast. 每天的自律讓他在早餐前練習書法。
- Why: 最常見的意思是「紀律」，「自律」較次要；例句與譯文也需配合。

### Core 4 · acknowledge

- Before: Please acknowledge the volunteer who found your lost wallet. 請向找到你錢包的志工致謝。
- After: Please acknowledge the message so we know you received it. 請回覆這則訊息，讓我們知道你已收到。
- Why: 最常見的意思是「承認」，「致謝」較少見；例句詞義也不符。

### Core 4 · busy

- Before: The busy market smelled of basil, fish, and diesel. 熱鬧的市場聞得到九層塔、魚和柴油味。
- After: The busy market smelled of basil, fish, and diesel. 忙碌熱鬧的市場聞得到九層塔、魚和柴油味。
- Why: 最常見的意思是「忙碌的」，「熱鬧的」應為補充意思。

### Core 14 · navigation

- Before: Clouds ruined navigation for the sailors near the reef. 礁石附近雲層密布，水手很難辨認方向。
- After: Clouds ruined navigation for the sailors near the reef. 礁石附近雲層密布，導航對水手來說變得很困難。
- Why: 例句譯文未忠實翻譯 navigation，與「導航」義不符。

### Core 5 · tourist

- Before: A lost tourist asked for directions near the lantern shop. 迷路旅客在燈籠店旁問路。
- After: A lost tourist asked for directions near the lantern shop. 一位迷路的觀光客在燈籠店旁問路。
- Why: exampleZh漏譯「一位」且語氣不自然；meaningZh改用台灣常用的「觀光客」。

### Core 5 · conventional

- Before: Nora prefers conventional medicine when her asthma gets worse. 諾拉氣喘加重時偏好正規醫療。
- After: Nora prefers conventional medicine when her asthma gets worse. 諾拉氣喘加重時，偏好傳統醫療。
- Why: conventional最常見的意思是「傳統的」，「正規的」並非最常用義。

### Core 5 · wet

- Before: My socks stayed wet after the scooter ride through rain. 雨中騎車後我的襪子一直濕著。
- After: My socks stayed wet after the scooter ride through rain. 騎機車穿過雨中後，我的襪子一直是濕的。
- Why: exampleZh漏譯scooter（機車）。

### Core 5 · whenever

- Before: Vendors lift baskets whenever the train slows beside their stalls. 火車在攤位旁放慢時攤販就舉起籃子。
- After: Vendors lift baskets whenever the train slows beside their stalls. 每當火車在攤位旁放慢速度，攤販就會舉起籃子。
- Why: exampleZh語氣生硬，未譯出「每當」的語意。

### Core 5 · string

- Before: A red string held the garlic beside the kitchen window. 紅繩把大蒜掛在廚房窗邊。
- After: A red string held the garlic beside the kitchen window. 一條紅繩把大蒜掛在廚房窗邊。
- Why: 「串起」為動詞義與詞性不合主要義項，改為較常見的「弦」；exampleZh補譯「一條」。

### Core 5 · pose

- Before: The bride changed her pose beside the banyan tree. 新娘在榕樹旁換了姿勢。
- After: The bride changed her pose beside the banyan tree. 新娘在榕樹旁換了個姿勢。
- Why: supplementalMeaningZh缺少常見的動詞義「擺姿勢」；exampleZh略作修飾。

### Core 5 · cake

- Before: A mango cake waited in the fridge for Auntie's birthday. 芒果蛋糕在冰箱裡等阿姨生日。
- After: A mango cake waited in the fridge for Auntie's birthday. 一個芒果蛋糕在冰箱裡，等著給阿姨慶生。
- Why: exampleZh「等阿姨生日」不自然。

### Core 5 · naturally

- Before: The puppy naturally followed anyone carrying a bowl of rice. 小狗自然跟著端飯碗的人走。
- After: The puppy naturally followed anyone carrying a bowl of rice. 小狗自然而然地跟著任何端著飯碗的人走。
- Why: exampleZh漏譯anyone（任何人）。

### Core 7 · punish

- Before: The judge refused to punish the boy for protecting himself. 法官拒絕懲罰那名為了自保的男孩。
- After: The teacher decided not to punish the boy for protecting himself. 老師決定不因那名男孩保護自己而懲罰他。
- Why: 補充詞義「嚴厲對待」不是常見詞義；例句中的法官情境也不貼近一般學生生活。

### Core 7 · shallow

- Before: Children played safely in the shallow end of the pool. 孩子們在泳池淺水區安全玩耍。
- After: Children played safely in the shallow end of the pool. 孩子們在泳池的淺水區安全地玩耍。
- Why: 詞性標示不符，且「淺水處」並非形容詞 shallow 的常見詞義；中文例句略不自然。

### Core 7 · sweat

- Before: Cold sweat soaked his shirt during the temple procession. 廟會隊伍中冷汗浸透他的襯衫。
- After: Cold sweat soaked his shirt during the temple procession. 廟會遊行時，冷汗浸濕了他的襯衫。
- Why: 「焦慮」不是 sweat 的常見詞義；例句中文「廟會隊伍中」不自然。

### Core 6 · surplus

- Before: Government surplus rice was stored in the school gym. 政府餘糧被存放在學校體育館。
- After: Government surplus rice was stored in the school gym. 政府的剩餘稻米被存放在學校體育館。
- Why: 詞性n排首位，主要意義應為名詞「盈餘」，「剩餘的」為形容詞應移到補充義。

### Core 6 · pact

- Before: The pact ended fishing disputes around the reef. 這項協議平息了礁岩周邊的捕魚爭端。
- After: The pact ended fishing disputes around the reef. 這項協定平息了礁岩周邊的捕魚爭端。
- Why: 例句翻譯用「協議」與詞義「協定」不一致，應統一用詞。

### Core 4 · gentleman

- Before: An elderly gentleman gave his seat to the nurse. 一位老先生把座位讓給護理師。
- After: An elderly gentleman gave his seat to the nurse. 一位年長的紳士把座位讓給護理師。
- Why: gentleman 最常見的意思是「紳士」，「先生」較不精確，故調整主要與補充詞義。

### Core 4 · initiative

- Before: Her initiative brought solar lights to the fishing village. 她的倡議為漁村帶來太陽能燈。
- After: She showed initiative by organizing the beach cleanup herself. 她主動自己籌辦淨灘活動，展現了積極的主動性。
- Why: initiative 最常見的意思是「主動性」，「倡議」較少見；例句也改配主要詞義。

### Core 4 · surely

- Before: This bridge will surely flood if the rain continues. 如果雨不停，這座橋肯定會淹水。
- After: This bridge will surely flood if the rain continues. 如果雨不停，這座橋一定會淹水。
- Why: surely 為副詞，「肯定」偏形容詞或動詞用法，改為「必定」較自然。

### Core 4 · resolution

- Before: The old tablet has poor resolution when displaying mountain photos. 那台舊平板顯示山景照片時解析度很差。
- After: The club passed a resolution to clean the school garden every Friday afternoon. 社團通過決議，每週五下午打掃學校花園。
- Why: 最常見的意思是「決議」，「解析度」應列為補充義。

### Core 3 · cabinet

- Before: Medicine belongs in the locked bathroom cabinet upstairs. 藥品應該放在樓上浴室上鎖的櫥櫃裡。
- After: Medicine belongs in the locked bathroom cabinet upstairs. 藥品應該放在樓上那個上鎖的浴室櫥櫃裡。
- Why: exampleZh 讀來不自然，缺少「那個上鎖的」等連接，語意不順。

### Core 3 · dance

- Before: Villagers dance around the fire after harvest each year. 村民每年收成後圍著火跳舞。
- After: Villagers dance around the fire after harvest each year. 村民每年收成後都會圍著火堆跳舞。
- Why: exampleZh 漏譯 each year 的「都會」語氣，且「火」不夠自然，宜改為「火堆」。

### Core 3 · mass

- Before: A mass of clouds hid the moon completely. 一大片雲完全遮住月亮。
- After: A mass of clouds hid the moon completely. 一大片雲完全遮住了月亮。
- Why: meaningZh「一大片」不是最常見義，宜為「大量」；補充義「質量」為中國用語，台灣用「質量」可但宜明確。

### Core 3 · organize

- Before: Volunteers organize beach cleanups after every storm season. 志工每個風暴季後舉辦淨灘。
- After: Volunteers organize beach cleanups after every storm season. 志工每個風暴季後都會舉辦淨灘活動。
- Why: meaningZh 應為最常見的「組織」，「舉辦」列為補充；exampleZh 也略有漏譯。

### Core 9 · haunt

- Before: The accident continued to haunt him for years. 那場事故多年來仍困擾著他。
- After: The accident continued to haunt him for years. 那場事故多年來仍縈繞在他心頭。
- Why: haunt 最常見的意思是鬼魂出沒或常去，「困擾」作主要義不夠常見，已調整。

### Core 9 · stunning

- Before: Her stunning mural covered the station wall overnight. 她驚人的壁畫一夜間覆蓋車站牆面。
- After: Her stunning mural covered the station wall overnight. 她那幅極漂亮的壁畫一夜之間就覆蓋了車站牆面。
- Why: stunning 最常見義為極漂亮，原義項不當，例句中譯也不自然。

### Core 9 · embark

- Before: They embark before sunrise to avoid rough seas. 他們日出前上船以避開惡劣海象。
- After: They will embark on a bike trip around Taiwan this summer. 他們今年夏天要展開環島自行車之旅。
- Why: embark 常用義為開始著手，原例句也不符合主要義。

### Core 9 · outlook

- Before: The job outlook for nurses in Taiwan is very good. 台灣護理師的就業前景非常好。
- After: The economic outlook for next year looks quite positive. 明年的經濟前景看起來相當樂觀。
- Why: 例句語意與主要義「前景」不符，且中文翻譯與原句不一致。

### Core 1 · here

- Before: Please park here while I unlock the gate. 我開門時，請先把車停這裡。
- After: Please park here while I unlock the gate. 我開門時，請先把車停在這裡。
- Why: exampleZh「停這裡」不夠自然，應為「停在這裡」。

### Core 1 · still

- Before: The lake was still after the evening wind stopped. 晚風停後湖面平靜無波。
- After: The lake is still calm after the evening wind stopped. 晚風停後，湖面仍然很平靜。
- Why: still 最常見的意思是副詞「仍然」，不是「平靜的」，例句也需配合。

### Core 9 · alike

- Before: The twins dressed alike for their grandmother's birthday. 雙胞胎為祖母生日穿得一模一樣。
- After: The twins dressed alike for their grandmother's birthday. 雙胞胎為祖母的生日穿得一模一樣。
- Why: 主要詞義應為形容詞「相似的」，副詞「一樣地」應放在補充義。

### Core 9 · haul

- Before: Fishermen haul heavy nets onto the pier before noon. 漁民中午前把沉重漁網拖上碼頭。
- After: Fishermen haul heavy nets onto the pier before noon. 漁民中午前把沉重的漁網拖上碼頭。
- Why: meaningZh 過於簡略，應強調用力拖拉。

### Core 9 · electronics

- Before: My brother studies electronics because he loves building small robots. 我哥哥學電子學，因為他喜歡做小型機器人。
- After: She repairs electronics such as phones and old cameras after school. 她放學後修理手機和老相機等電子產品。
- Why: 最常用義為電子產品，例句也應符合該義。

### Core 9 · patrol

- Before: A night patrol checked the harbor after the theft. 竊案後夜間巡邏隊檢查港口。
- After: A night patrol checked the harbor after the theft. 竊案發生後，夜間巡邏隊檢查了港口。
- Why: 最常見義為巡邏，巡邏隊應放補充義。

### Core 9 · slap

- Before: The wet newspaper landed with a slap against the door. 濕報紙啪一聲拍在門上。
- After: The wet newspaper landed with a slap against the door. 濕報紙啪的一聲打在門上。
- Why: 主要詞義應為掌摑或巴掌，拍擊聲較不常用。

### Core 4 · gone

- Before: The mangoes were gone before the cousins reached the kitchen. 表親們到廚房前，芒果已經吃完了。
- After: The mangoes were gone before the cousins reached the kitchen. 表親們到廚房前，芒果已經被吃光了。
- Why: gone 作形容詞最常見為「離開的」，「沒有了」過於籠統，補充義重組。

### Core 4 · mind

- Before: Her mind stayed calm while the power flickered. 電燈閃爍時，她的頭腦仍很冷靜。
- After: Her mind stayed calm while the power flickered. 電燈閃爍時，她的心情仍很平靜。
- Why: mind 主要義為「心智」，例句譯為頭腦不自然。

### Core 8 · identical

- Before: The twins wore identical raincoats at the crowded station. 雙胞胎在擁擠車站穿著一模一樣雨衣。
- After: The twins wore identical raincoats at the crowded station. 雙胞胎在擁擠的車站穿著一模一樣的雨衣。
- Why: exampleZh 不通順，缺少「的」；補充詞義為空，可補上常見義。

### Core 8 · subtle

- Before: A subtle ginger note lingered after the tea cooled. 茶涼後，仍留著一絲微妙薑味。
- After: A subtle ginger note lingered after the tea cooled. 茶涼後，仍留著一絲淡淡的薑味。
- Why: exampleZh 用詞不自然，「微妙薑味」不通順。

### Core 8 · prediction

- Before: His prediction of heavy fog saved the morning ferry. 他預報濃霧，保住了早班渡輪安全。
- After: His prediction of heavy fog saved the morning ferry. 他預測會有濃霧，保住了早班渡輪的安全。
- Why: prediction 最常見意思是預測，預報不是主要意思；例句翻譯也不自然

### Core 14 · pumpkin

- Before: A pumpkin sat on the windowsill until winter. 一顆南瓜在窗台上放到了冬天。
- After: A pumpkin sat on the windowsill until winter. 一顆南瓜在窗臺上放到了冬天。
- Why: 「窗台」宜用臺灣慣用的「窗臺」，其餘無誤。

### Core 10 · defy

- Before: The swimmer tried to defy the warning and enter the rough water. 游泳者想無視警告進入洶湧海水。
- After: The swimmer tried to defy the warning and enter the rough water. 游泳者想無視警告，進入洶湧的海水。
- Why: 詞性應只有動詞；例句翻譯不通順，已修正。

### Core 10 · goose

- Before: A goose chased the postman across the muddy farmyard. 一隻鵝追著郵差穿過泥濘農院。
- After: A goose chased the postman across the muddy farmyard. 一隻鵝追著郵差穿過泥濘的農場院子。
- Why: 譯文「農院」不自然，已修正。

### Core 10 · eternal

- Before: Grandpa called the sea his eternal source of patience. 祖父說大海是他永恆的耐心來源。
- After: Grandpa called the sea his eternal source of patience. 祖父稱大海是他永不枯竭的耐心來源。
- Why: 中文譯文與英文句意不符，漏譯並添加內容。

### Core 12 · knitting

- Before: Her knitting slipped from the basket during the bus ride. 她的針織物在公車上從籃子滑出。
- After: Her knitting slipped from the basket during the bus ride. 她的編織物在公車上從籃子滑了出來。
- Why: knitting 作名詞最常見意思是「編織」，「針織物」不是最常見的主要意義。

### Core 12 · congratulations

- Before: Warm congratulations poured in after Mei passed the bar exam. 美通過律師考試後，熱烈祝賀紛紛湧來。
- After: Congratulations poured in after Mei passed the bar exam. 美通過律師考試後，祝賀的訊息紛紛湧來。
- Why: congratulations 最常見的意思是「恭喜」，主要詞義應調整。

### Core 9 · clarify

- Before: Please clarify the address before the driver leaves. 司機離開前請先釐清地址。
- After: Please clarify the address before the driver leaves. 司機離開前請先澄清地址。
- Why: clarify 最常見意思為「澄清、闡明」，「釐清」與補充義重複；例句譯文改為較自然的說法

### Core 9 · jungle

- Before: Monsoon rain soaked the trail through dense jungle before noon. 季風雨在中午前濕透叢林小徑。
- After: Monsoon rain soaked the trail through dense jungle before noon. 中午前，季風雨把穿越茂密叢林的小徑淋得濕透。
- Why: 補充義過長且不精確，譯文漏掉 dense 與 trail 的關係

### Core 9 · patron

- Before: A loyal patron bought breakfast there every morning. 一名老顧客每天早上都在那裡買早餐。
- After: The museum thanked each patron who donated money this year. 博物館感謝今年每一位捐款的贊助人。
- Why: patron 最常見義為贊助人，顧客為次要義，例句也需對應主要詞義

### Core 9 · foster

- Before: Neighbors foster abandoned puppies until homes are found. 鄰居暫時養育棄犬直到找到新家。
- After: Good teachers foster curiosity in their students every day. 好老師每天都在培養學生的好奇心。
- Why: 詞性標示含動詞，主要義應為培養；原例句 foster 用法與主義不符

### Core 9 · shield

- Before: A clear plastic shield protected the cashier from sudden splashes. 透明塑膠防護板保護收銀員免受突然濺來的液體。
- After: The knight lifted his shield to block the sword. 騎士舉起盾牌擋住那把劍。
- Why: shield 最常見義為盾牌，防護板為次要義

### Core 10 · compulsory

- Before: Helmet use is compulsory on every ride through the quarry. 每次騎車穿越採石場都必須戴安全帽。
- After: Helmet use is compulsory on every ride through the quarry. 騎車穿越採石場時，每次都必須戴安全帽。
- Why: 補充義加入常見的「義務的」，並使例句翻譯更通順。

### Core 6 · monthly

- Before: We pay the monthly phone bill at the convenience store near school. 我們在學校附近的便利商店繳每個月的電話費。
- After: The monthly magazine featured abandoned cinemas this issue. 這期月刊專題介紹廢棄電影院。
- Why: 詞性為形容詞，最常見意思應為「每月的」，「月刊」是名詞用法，應放在補充意思。

### Core 6 · revolutionary

- Before: The revolutionary pamphlet was hidden inside a cookbook. 革命小冊子藏在一本食譜裡。
- After: The revolutionary pamphlet was hidden inside a cookbook. 那本革命小冊子被藏在一本食譜裡。
- Why: 原entry尚可，但補充意思重複性低；譯文略欠自然。

### Core 6 · execute

- Before: Our class will execute the cleanup plan as soon as the rain stops. 雨一停，我們班就會執行打掃計畫。
- After: The chef can execute the knife cut in seconds. 主廚能在幾秒內完成那種刀工。
- Why: execute 最常見意思為「執行」，「完成」並非主要意思，應調整。

### Core 4 · pick

- Before: Pick the smaller mangoes if you need them ripe tomorrow. 如果明天要熟，挑小一點的芒果。
- After: Pick the smaller mangoes if you need them ripe tomorrow. 如果明天就要吃熟的，請挑小一點的芒果。
- Why: exampleZh 漏譯 ripe 的語意，且 need 譯得不自然。

### Core 12 · used

- Before: A used scooter stood outside the repair shop. 一輛二手機車停在修車行外。
- After: My brother is used to riding his scooter to school every morning. 我哥哥習慣每天早上騎機車去上學。
- Why: used 作形容詞最常見意義為「習慣於」，「二手的」應列為補充。

### Core 12 · planning

- Before: Careful planning kept the hikers off the dangerous ridge. 仔細規劃讓登山客避開危險山脊。
- After: Careful planning kept the hikers off the dangerous ridge. 周詳的規劃讓登山客避開了危險的山脊。
- Why: 譯文「仔細規劃讓」略不自然，需修正語句。

### Core 12 · unemployment

- Before: Local unemployment rose after the cannery closed in March. 罐頭廠三月關閉後，當地失業人數上升。
- After: Local unemployment rose after the cannery closed in March. 罐頭廠三月關閉後，當地失業率上升。
- Why: 主義應為最常見的「失業」，並修正譯文用詞。

### Core 12 · walking

- Before: Gentle walking to the harbor cleared her head after lunch. 午餐後輕鬆走到港口，讓她的思緒清醒許多。
- After: Gentle walking to the harbor cleared her head after lunch. 午餐後輕鬆步行到港口，讓她的思緒清醒許多。
- Why: 補充義「走路方式」不正確。

### Core 12 · driving

- Before: The driving rain blurred the windshield on the mountain road. 強勁大雨模糊了山路上的擋風玻璃。
- After: Driving at night can be tiring for new drivers. 夜間駕駛對新手來說可能很累人。
- Why: driving 作名詞最常見義為「駕駛」，例句也需配合主義。

### Core 12 · builder

- Before: The builder of the charity school returned for its anniversary. 那所慈善學校的建造者，在校慶時回到學校。
- After: The builder repaired the broken roof before the typhoon arrived. 建築工人在颱風來臨前修好了破損的屋頂。
- Why: builder 最常見的意思是建築工人，「建立者」不是主要詞義，例句也應配合。

### Core 12 · constituency

- Before: Her constituency demanded safer buses after the mountain crash. 她的選民在山區事故後要求更安全公車。
- After: Her constituency demanded safer buses after the mountain crash. 她的選區居民在山區事故後要求更安全的公車。
- Why: constituency 最常見意思是選區，「選民」應放在補充；例句翻譯也不自然。

### Core 12 · sergeant

- Before: A sergeant guided the lost hikers through the flooded checkpoint. 一名軍士帶迷路登山客穿過淹水檢查站。
- After: A sergeant guided the lost hikers through the flooded checkpoint. 一名軍士帶領迷路的登山客穿過淹水的檢查站。
- Why: 譯文語句不夠通順，已修正。

### Core 8 · verse

- Before: She copied one verse onto the back of a receipt. 她把一行詩抄在收據背面。
- After: She copied one verse onto the back of a receipt. 她把一段詩句抄在收據背面。
- Why: 例句 verse 譯成「一行詩」不精確，應為詩句或詩節；補充詞義「詩節」。

### Core 8 · avenue

- Before: Trees along the avenue shaded the bakery until late afternoon. 林蔭道旁的樹遮住麵包店直到午後。
- After: Trees along the avenue shaded the bakery until late afternoon. 大道旁的樹一直遮蔽著麵包店到傍晚。
- Why: avenue 最常見意思是大道，而非林蔭道；翻譯也應配合修正。

### Core 8 · genius

- Before: His genius for repairs saved the flooded theater. 他的修理天賦救了淹水的戲院。
- After: His genius for repairs saved the flooded theater. 他在修理方面的天賦拯救了淹水的戲院。
- Why: genius 最常見意思是天才，天賦應列為補充。

### Core 8 · manipulate

- Before: Do not manipulate the evidence before police arrive. 警方抵達前，不要操弄證據。
- After: Do not manipulate the evidence before police arrive. 警方抵達前，不要竄改證據。
- Why: 「操弄」不是最常見的解釋，主要義應為操縱；證據的翻譯應為竄改。

### Core 8 · mutter

- Before: He began to mutter when the vending machine jammed. 販賣機卡住時，他開始喃喃抱怨。
- After: He began to mutter when the vending machine jammed. 販賣機卡住時，他開始低聲嘀咕。
- Why: mutter 的主要意思是低聲嘀咕或喃喃自語，「喃喃抱怨」過於偏向抱怨，順序應調整。

### Core 8 · hostile

- Before: A hostile crowd blocked the clinic entrance after the rumor. 謠言傳出後，敵對人群堵住診所入口。
- After: A hostile crowd blocked the clinic entrance after the rumor. 謠言傳出後，一群懷有敵意的人堵住診所入口。
- Why: hostile crowd 指懷有敵意的人群，「敵對人群」不自然；補充義也有重複。

### Core 8 · lawsuit

- Before: The lawsuit began after sewage flooded three kitchens. 污水淹進三間廚房後，訴訟開始了。
- After: The lawsuit began after sewage flooded three kitchens. 汙水淹進三間廚房後，這場訴訟就展開了。
- Why: 「污水」在台灣慣用「汙水」，且譯文「訴訟開始了」不夠自然。

### Core 7 · blank

- Before: His blank stare worried the nurse on duty. 他空洞的眼神讓值班護理師擔心。
- After: Please leave the last page of your notebook blank. 請把你筆記本的最後一頁留白。
- Why: 最常見詞義是「空白的」；空洞的為次要詞義，例句也應對應主要詞義。

### Core 8 · rhetoric

- Before: His rhetoric sounded noble but avoided every hard question. 他的說辭聽來高尚，卻避開所有難題。
- After: His rhetoric sounded noble but avoided every hard question. 他的言辭聽來高尚，卻避開所有難題。
- Why: 最常見詞義應為「修辭」；例句用「修辭」翻譯不自然，應改為「言辭」。

### Core 3 · dismiss

- Before: The principal will dismiss students early before the typhoon. 校長會在颱風前提早放學生離校。
- After: The principal will dismiss students early before the typhoon. 校長會在颱風前提早讓學生放學。
- Why: 主要意義應為「解散；遣散」，「准予離開」過於狹窄，應移到補充義。

### Core 3 · august

- Before: August afternoons drive cats under cars for shade. 八月午後酷熱難耐，貓都躲到車底下乘涼。
- After: The august professor walked slowly into the quiet hall. 那位威嚴的教授慢慢走進安靜的大廳。
- Why: august 小寫為形容詞「威嚴的」，八月是 August 大寫；例句與詞形不符。

### Core 3 · saturday

- Before: Saturday vendors arrive before sunrise at the harbor market. 星期六港邊市場攤販日出前就到。
- After: Saturday vendors arrive before sunrise at the harbor market. 星期六港邊市場的攤販日出前就到了。
- Why: 例句缺少完整性，中文翻譯過於省略，讀來不自然。

### Core 14 · swan

- Before: A swan drifted past the reeds near the palace. 一隻天鵝漂過宮殿附近蘆葦叢。
- After: A swan drifted past the reeds near the palace. 一隻天鵝漂過宮殿附近的蘆葦叢。
- Why: exampleZh 語句稍不順，缺少「的」；詞性 vi 也不常見，但內容無誤。

### Core 14 · refrain

- Before: Please refrain from feeding birds near the platform. 請避免在月台附近餵鳥。
- After: Please refrain from feeding birds near the platform. 請勿在月台附近餵鳥。
- Why: exampleZh 的「請避免」較不自然，改為「請勿」更符合告示用語。

### Core 14 · intellect

- Before: Her sharp intellect solved the puzzle before dinner. 她敏銳的智力在晚餐前解開謎題。
- After: Her sharp intellect solved the puzzle before dinner. 她敏銳的智力讓她在晚餐前解開了謎題。
- Why: 「知識分子」雖可用，但此義較罕見；exampleZh 句式生硬，已修正。

### Core 1 · night

- Before: The harbor lights look beautiful at night after rain. 雨後夜裡，港口燈火很美。
- After: The harbor lights look beautiful at night after rain. 雨後的夜晚，港口的燈火看起來很美。
- Why: night 名詞主要詞義應為「夜晚」，「夜裡」不夠典型。

### Core 9 · midst

- Before: In the midst of chaos, she found the baby. 混亂中她找到了那名嬰兒。
- After: In the midst of the noisy crowd, she found her little brother. 在嘈雜的人群中，她找到了自己的弟弟。
- Why: 例句與譯文過於突兀（找到嬰兒）且譯文漏譯 In the midst of 的語氣；釋義「當中」不夠貼切。

### Core 9 · abolish

- Before: The council voted to abolish the old curfew. 議會投票廢止舊宵禁。
- After: The council voted to abolish the old curfew. 議會投票廢除舊宵禁。
- Why: 「廢止」較少用，常用為「廢除」；「消除」非常見補充義。

### Core 9 · outbreak

- Before: An outbreak of fever closed the dormitory quickly. 一波發燒疫情爆發後，宿舍很快就關閉了。
- After: An outbreak of fever closed the dormitory quickly. 一場發燒疫情使宿舍很快就關閉了。
- Why: 補充義「疫情爆發」與主義重複，且譯文「爆發後」有誤增。

### Core 9 · lightning

- Before: Bright lightning split the palm tree beside the road. 明亮閃電劈開路旁的棕櫚樹。
- After: Bright lightning split the palm tree beside the road. 明亮的閃電劈開了路旁的棕櫚樹。
- Why: 補充義「閃電般的」為形容詞用法，不常見；譯文不夠自然。

### Core 9 · retrieve

- Before: Divers retrieve lost phones from the shallow harbor. 潛水員從淺港取回遺失手機。
- After: Divers retrieve lost phones from the shallow harbor. 潛水員從淺水港灣取回遺失的手機。
- Why: 譯文「淺港」不自然，應為淺水港灣。

### Core 8 · descend

- Before: Mist began to descend over the fields before sunset. 日落前，霧氣開始緩緩降到田野上。
- After: Mist began to descend over the fields before sunset. 日落前，霧開始降落在田野上。
- Why: descend 最常見的意思是「下降」，而非「降落」。

### Core 8 · breach

- Before: Leaving the gate unlocked was a breach of security. 大門沒有上鎖，造成了安全漏洞。
- After: Leaving the gate unlocked was a breach of security. 沒有鎖上大門，是違反安全規定的行為。
- Why: 例句中文把 breach 譯成「漏洞」，與 meaningZh「違反」不一致。

### Core 1 · run

- Before: I run beside the canal after sunrise every Saturday. 每個星期六日出後，我都沿著運河跑步。
- After: I run beside the canal after sunrise every Saturday. 每個週六日出後，我都沿著運河跑步。
- Why: 「週六日出」容易被誤讀成週六與週日，翻譯不自然。

### Core 14 · clasp

- Before: Her frozen fingers could not close the necklace clasp. 她凍僵的手指扣不上項鍊釦子。
- After: Her frozen fingers could not close the necklace clasp. 她凍僵的手指扣不上項鍊的釦環。
- Why: clasp 最常見的意思是扣環或緊握，「釦子」較偏button，應改為「扣環」。

### Core 14 · clone

- Before: The lab grew a clone of the rare orchid. 實驗室培育出那株稀有蘭花的複製株。
- After: The lab grew a clone of the rare orchid. 實驗室培育出那株稀有蘭花的複製體。
- Why: 「複製株」過於罕用，clone 較常見的譯法為「複製體」。

### Core 5 · strict

- Before: The strict lifeguard closed the beach before sunset. 嚴格救生員在日落前關閉海灘。
- After: The strict lifeguard closed the beach before sunset. 嚴格的救生員在日落前關閉了海灘。
- Why: 例句中譯漏掉「的」，讀起來不自然

### Core 5 · countryside

- Before: The countryside smelled of wet grass after harvest. 收割後鄉間有濕草氣味。
- After: The countryside smelled of wet grass after harvest. 收割後，鄉間瀰漫著濕草的氣味。
- Why: 補充義與主要義重複；中譯不自然

### Core 4 · negative

- Before: The test result was negative, so he returned to work. 檢測結果呈陰性後，他便回去工作了。
- After: He tried not to say anything negative about the school trip. 他盡量不說任何關於校外教學的負面話。
- Why: 最常見詞義應為「負面的」，「陰性的」較專門；例句已改配合主要詞義。

### Core 4 · panel

- Before: A wooden panel hid the old fuse box. 一片木板遮住舊保險絲盒。
- After: The control panel beside the door shows the water level. 門旁的控制面板顯示水位。
- Why: 「板」過於籠統，最常見詞義為「面板」；例句也配合修正。

### Core 9 · gown

- Before: She wore a long blue gown to her sister's wedding. 她穿著一件藍色長禮服參加姊姊的婚禮。
- After: She wore a long silk gown to the school dance. 她穿著一件絲質長禮服去參加學校舞會。
- Why: gown 最常見的意思是禮服，睡衣不是主要字義，例句也與之不符。

### Core 9 · landmark

- Before: Taipei 101 is the most famous landmark in the city. 台北101是這座城市最有名的地標。
- After: The old temple is a famous landmark in our town. 那座古老的廟是我們鎮上著名的地標。
- Why: landmark 最常見的意思是地標，地界標很少用，不適合作為主要字義。

### Core 9 · recorder

- Before: The recorder clearly captured whispers behind the curtain. 錄音機清楚錄下簾後的低語。
- After: Our music teacher asked us to play the recorder. 音樂老師要我們吹直笛。
- Why: 對台灣高中生而言，recorder 最常見的意思是直笛，應調整主要字義與例句。

### Core 9 · intake

- Before: Doctors advise people to reduce their daily sugar intake. 醫師建議大家減少每天的糖分攝取量。
- After: Doctors say a high sugar intake is bad for your teeth. 醫生說糖的攝取量過高對牙齒不好。
- Why: intake 最常見的意思是攝取量，進氣口是較專門的字義。

### Core 10 · overhead

- Before: An overhead sign warned trucks about the low tunnel. 高架標誌提醒卡車駕駛，前方隧道高度不足。
- After: An overhead sign warned trucks about the low tunnel. 頭頂上的標誌提醒卡車駕駛，前方隧道高度不足。
- Why: overhead 並非「高架」，譯文應為「頭頂上方」；補充副詞義。

### Core 10 · fabulous

- Before: We had a fabulous time at the night market with our cousins. 我們和表兄弟姊妹在夜市度過了一段極好的時光。
- After: We had a fabulous time at the night market with our cousins. 我們和表兄弟姊妹在夜市玩得非常開心。
- Why: 最常見詞義是「極好的」，「傳說中的」為罕見義，應調整。

### Core 10 · plantation

- Before: Workers harvested tea leaves on the mountain plantation at dawn. 工人清晨在山坡茶園採摘茶葉。
- After: Workers harvested tea leaves on the mountain plantation at dawn. 工人清晨在山上的茶園種植園採摘茶葉。
- Why: 原譯文漏譯 plantation 且與例句不一致，需補足翻譯。

### Core 6 · jail

- Before: He spent two nights in jail after the protest. 抗議後他在拘留所待了兩晚。
- After: He spent two nights in jail after the protest. 抗議之後，他在牢裡待了兩個晚上。
- Why: jail 最常見的意思是監獄，拘留所應放在補充義。

### Core 6 · unity

- Before: The lantern parade gave the neighborhood a rare unity. 燈籠遊行讓整個社區難得團結在一起。
- After: The lantern parade gave the neighborhood a rare unity. 燈籠遊行難得凝聚了整個社區。
- Why: 「一致感」不是 unity 最常見的詞義，主要意思應為團結。

### Core 11 · zoom

- Before: Scooters zoom past the school gate every morning before eight. 每天早上八點前，機車都會從校門前呼嘯而過。
- After: The motorbikes zoom past the school gate every morning. 每天早上機車都呼嘯駛過校門。
- Why: zoom 最常見的意思是急速移動，猛漲不是主要義，例句也需配合。

### Core 11 · accomplished

- Before: An accomplished dancer taught tango in the old warehouse. 一位熟練舞者在舊倉庫教探戈。
- After: An accomplished dancer taught tango in the old warehouse. 一位技藝精湛的舞者在舊倉庫教探戈。
- Why: accomplished 主要指技藝精湛、有造詣，熟練的不夠貼切，譯文也應修正。

### Core 7 · shiny

- Before: The shiny coins covered the fortune teller's table. 發亮硬幣鋪滿算命師桌面。
- After: The shiny coins covered the fortune teller's table. 閃亮的硬幣鋪滿了算命師的桌面。
- Why: 中文翻譯過於簡略生硬，缺少「的」與「了」，已改為自然的說法。

### Core 7 · surroundings

- Before: The quiet surroundings made the hill temple feel remote. 安靜環境讓山寺顯得偏遠。
- After: The quiet surroundings made the hill temple feel remote. 安靜的周遭環境讓這座山寺顯得很偏遠。
- Why: 中文翻譯過於簡略生硬，已改為自然通順的說法。

### Core 7 · goodbye

- Before: She whispered goodbye before boarding the midnight train. 她登上午夜列車前輕聲道別。
- After: She whispered goodbye before boarding the midnight train. 她在登上午夜列車前輕聲說了再見。
- Why: 譯文漏譯 goodbye 的「再見」，並把它譯成動詞「道別」，已修正。

### Core 7 · rubbish

- Before: Wind blew rubbish across the empty parking lot. 風把垃圾吹過空停車場。
- After: Wind blew rubbish across the empty parking lot. 風把垃圾吹過了空蕩蕩的停車場。
- Why: 補充釋義「胡說」不夠自然，改為「廢話；胡說」，譯文也改得更通順。

### Core 7 · suitcase

- Before: Her suitcase rolled down the ferry ramp alone. 她的手提箱自己滾下渡輪斜坡。
- After: Her suitcase rolled down the ferry ramp alone. 她的行李箱自己滾下了渡輪的斜坡。
- Why: suitcase 最常見的意思是行李箱，手提箱應為補充釋義。

### Core 7 · jealous

- Before: He felt jealous when his sister won the kite race. 妹妹贏風箏賽時他很嫉妒。
- After: He felt jealous when his sister won the kite race. 妹妹贏得風箏比賽時，他覺得很嫉妒。
- Why: 「羨慕的」語氣較弱，不是 jealous 的常見義，改為「吃醋的」。

### Core 4 · gay

- Before: Our school club welcomes all students, whether they are gay or straight. 我們學校的社團歡迎所有學生，不論是同性戀或異性戀。
- After: Their gay friends joined the parade and celebrated together downtown. 他們的同性戀朋友加入遊行，一起在市中心慶祝。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「同性戀的」，「鮮豔的」為罕見古義，例句也不符常用義

### Core 4 · journal

- Before: Mei keeps a travel journal during every cycling trip. 小美每次騎車旅行都寫旅行日記。
- After: The student read a science journal article about sea turtles. 那位學生讀了一篇關於海龜的科學期刊文章。
- Why: journal 最常見義為期刊，日記為次要義，主要義順序不當

### Core 6 · earnings

- Before: Her weekend earnings paid for new violin strings. 她用週末賺來的錢買了新的小提琴弦。
- After: Her weekend earnings paid for new violin strings. 她週末賺的錢，付了新小提琴弦的費用。
- Why: exampleZh「賺來的收入」語意重複且「支付了…費用」不自然，已改寫。

### Core 7 · heating

- Before: The heating failed during the coldest night in January. 一月最冷那晚暖氣故障。
- After: The heating failed during the coldest night in January. 一月最冷的那個晚上，暖氣設備故障了。
- Why: 「加熱」作補充義不精確，詞義應為暖氣設備或供暖；譯文也略顯不完整。

### Core 9 · prescribe

- Before: The doctor will prescribe medicine for your cough after the checkup. 檢查完後，醫師會開治療咳嗽的藥給你。
- After: Doctors prescribe rest when the fever returns at night. 夜裡發燒復發時，醫師會囑咐要多休息。
- Why: 最常見義應為「開處方」，「囑咐」不精確。

### Core 9 · weed

- Before: A stubborn weed grew through the cracked sidewalk. 一株頑強雜草從裂開人行道長出。
- After: A stubborn weed grew through the cracked sidewalk. 一株頑強的雜草從裂開的人行道長了出來。
- Why: 原中文翻譯不通順，缺少「的」而不自然。

### Core 9 · clip

- Before: A silver clip held her hair during the dance. 跳舞時，一枚銀色髮夾固定著她的頭髮。
- After: A silver clip held her hair during the dance. 跳舞時，一個銀色髮夾固定著她的頭髮。
- Why: 「迴紋針」與夾子重複，改為「剪輯」。

### Core 9 · soar

- Before: Kites soar above the riverbank every autumn afternoon. 每年秋天下午風箏都在河岸上空高飛。
- After: Kites soar above the riverbank every autumn afternoon. 每年秋天的午後，風箏都在河岸上空高飛。
- Why: 詞性 n 不當，且譯文略不自然。

### Core 9 · passive

- Before: His passive response frustrated the rescue volunteers nearby. 他消極的反應，讓附近的救援志工很挫折。
- After: His passive response frustrated the rescue volunteers nearby. 他被動的反應，讓附近的救援志工很挫折。
- Why: 例句應對應主要義「被動的」，譯文與詞義不符。

### Core 9 · cater

- Before: Small vendors cater to hikers near the trailhead. 小攤販在登山口服務健行者。
- After: Small vendors cater to hikers near the trailhead. 小攤販在登山口為健行者提供餐飲。
- Why: cater 最常見義為提供餐飲，「服務」過於籠統。

### Core 2 · quality

- Before: The quality of the tea depends on the mountain weather. 茶葉的品質取決於山上的天氣。
- After: This tea shop is known for quality oolong. 這家茶行以優質烏龍茶聞名。
- Why: 主要意義應為名詞「品質」，「優質的」為形容詞用法，應放在補充意義。

### Core 2 · population

- Before: The population of our town grew after the new station opened. 新車站啟用後，我們鎮上的人口增加了。
- After: The deer population increased after hunting was banned. 禁獵後鹿群數量增加。
- Why: 主要意義應為人口，族群數量為補充意義。

### Core 12 · sponsorship

- Before: The forum ran under university sponsorship for three years. 這個論壇在大學的贊助下舉辦了三年。
- After: The forum ran under university sponsorship for three years. 這場論壇在大學的贊助下舉辦了三年。
- Why: sponsorship 最常見的意思是贊助，不是主辦；例句翻譯也應配合。

### Core 12 · comeback

- Before: Nina's comeback made the rude waiter stop laughing. 妮娜尖銳地回嘴，讓無禮的服務生笑不出來。
- After: Nina's sharp comeback made the rude waiter stop laughing. 妮娜尖銳地回嘴，讓無禮的服務生笑不出來。
- Why: 例句缺少 sharp，與中文翻譯不符，已補上。

### Core 12 · bureaucrat

- Before: A bureaucrat misplaced the permit inside a green folder. 一名官員把許可證錯放進綠色資料夾裡。
- After: A bureaucrat misplaced the permit inside a green folder. 一名官僚把許可證錯放進綠色資料夾裡。
- Why: bureaucrat 主要指官僚，帶貶義，不等於一般官員。

### Core 13 · stagger

- Before: He began to stagger after carrying rice up three flights. 扛米上三層樓後，他開始蹣跚。
- After: He began to stagger after carrying rice up three flights. 扛米上了三層樓後，他開始步履蹣跚。
- Why: 「蹣跚」為形容詞化用法，動詞義宜寫「蹣跚而行」；譯文也不自然。

### Core 13 · stocking

- Before: One red stocking hung from the hostel fireplace. 一隻紅長襪掛在旅社壁爐上。
- After: One red stocking hung from the hostel fireplace. 一隻紅色長襪掛在旅社的壁爐上。
- Why: 譯文「紅長襪」讀來不自然，應改為「紅色長襪」。

### Core 13 · excursion

- Before: Our boat excursion ended at a quiet coral beach. 我們的搭船短遊在安靜珊瑚海灘結束。
- After: Our boat excursion ended at a quiet coral beach. 我們的乘船短途旅行在一處安靜的珊瑚海灘結束。
- Why: 譯文「搭船短遊」不自然，應改為更通順的說法。

### Core 13 · magnet

- Before: Lily stuck the class schedule on the fridge with a magnet. 莉莉用磁鐵把課表貼在冰箱上。
- After: The lighthouse became a magnet for storm photographers. 燈塔成了吸引風暴攝影師的地方。
- Why: magnet 最常見義為「磁鐵」；例句為比喻義，宜改為磁鐵的例句。

### Core 3 · below

- Before: The spare key hangs below the kitchen shelf downstairs. 備用鑰匙掛在樓下廚房的架子底下。
- After: The spare key hangs on a hook below the kitchen shelf. 備用鑰匙掛在廚房架子下方的掛鉤上。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「在下方」，「樓下」不是最常見義；例句與例句翻譯也與詞義不完全相符。

### Core 3 · shot

- Before: One lucky shot won the basketball game at dusk. 黃昏時一記幸運投籃贏得比賽。
- After: The hunter fired one shot into the air to scare the birds. 獵人朝空中開了一槍來驚嚇鳥群。
- Why: shot 最常見的意思是射擊或一槍，投籃應放在補充義。

### Core 3 · critical

- Before: Clean water became critical after the hillside road collapsed. 山路坍方後，乾淨的飲用水成了最迫切的需求。
- After: Clean water became critical after the hillside road collapsed. 山路坍方後，乾淨的水變得非常緊要。
- Why: critical 最常見義為危急的；例句翻譯「最迫切的需求」與原句不完全對應。

### Core 3 · mountain

- Before: A blue mountain rose behind the morning mist today. 今天晨霧後方矗立著一座藍色山峰。
- After: A blue mountain rose behind the morning mist today. 今天晨霧後方矗立著一座藍色的山。
- Why: mountain 的主要詞義是「山」，「山峰」較不常用；補充義「山脈」不準確。

### Core 3 · recall

- Before: I recall the smell of rain on hot pavement. 我想起熱路面上的雨味。
- After: I recall the smell of rain on hot pavement. 我想起了雨水落在炙熱路面上的氣味。
- Why: 例句翻譯「熱路面上的雨味」不自然，應修正。

### Core 3 · settle

- Before: Dust began to settle on the unused piano upstairs. 灰塵開始落在樓上閒置鋼琴上。
- After: Dust began to settle on the unused piano upstairs. 灰塵開始落在樓上閒置的鋼琴上。
- Why: settle 較常見義為解決或安頓，「落下」非主要義；例句用法應為補充義。

### Core 3 · wood

- Before: Dry wood cracked loudly in the beach fire tonight. 今晚乾木柴在海邊火堆中劈啪作響。
- After: Dry wood cracked loudly in the beach fire tonight. 今晚乾木柴在海邊的火堆中劈啪作響。
- Why: wood 最常見義為木材；詞性 vt / vi 也不正確。

### Core 9 · preach

- Before: He would preach kindness while refusing to help neighbors. 他嘴上宣講要待人善良，自己卻不肯幫助鄰居。
- After: He would preach kindness while refusing to help neighbors. 他嘴上說教要人善良，自己卻拒絕幫助鄰居。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「講道」；例句中文「宣講善良卻拒絕幫助」不自然，且語意不完整。

### Core 3 · rich

- Before: The soup had a rich mushroom aroma after two hours. 湯熬兩小時後有濃郁菇香。
- After: The soup had a rich mushroom aroma after two hours. 湯熬了兩小時後，散發出濃郁的菇香。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「富有的」，「濃郁的」為次要義；例句中譯也略不完整。

### Core 3 · affair

- Before: Neighbors still whispered about the affair at the corner bakery. 街坊仍低聲談那間轉角麵包店的事件。
- After: Neighbors still whispered about the affair at the corner bakery. 街坊鄰居仍低聲談論轉角麵包店的那件事。
- Why: affair 最常見義為「事務」，且例句以「事件」較不自然，中譯也漏譯 Neighbors。

### Core 3 · key

- Before: Patience is the key to teaching a nervous puppy. 耐心是教容易緊張的小狗的關鍵。
- After: Patience is the key to teaching a nervous puppy. 耐心是訓練膽小小狗的關鍵。
- Why: key 作名詞最常見義為「鑰匙」，且例句中譯不自然。

### Core 3 · safety

- Before: Bike lights improve safety on rainy evening rides. 雨天傍晚騎車時車燈能提高安全。
- After: Bike lights improve safety on rainy evening rides. 雨天傍晚騎車時，車燈能提升安全性。
- Why: safety 不是動詞，詞性標示有誤；中譯「提高安全」不自然。

### Core 10 · bald

- Before: A bald patch appeared where the dog scratched constantly. 狗一直抓的地方出現一塊禿斑。
- After: The bald man wore a hat to protect his head from the sun. 那位禿頭的男子戴著帽子保護頭部免於曬傷。
- Why: 例句的禿斑與狗抓痕語意不自然；補充光禿義，並改用更自然的例句。

### Core 10 · olive

- Before: An olive rolled from the plate under the counter. 一顆橄欖從盤子滾到櫃台下。
- After: An olive rolled from the plate under the counter. 一顆橄欖從盤子滾到櫃檯下。
- Why: 「櫃台」應為台灣用字「櫃檯」。

### Core 10 · junk

- Before: Old junk filled the garage until rats moved in. 舊垃圾塞滿車庫直到老鼠搬進來。
- After: Old junk filled the garage until rats moved in. 舊雜物塞滿了車庫，後來連老鼠都搬進來了。
- Why: 例句中譯「舊垃圾」不自然，且漏譯「直到」的語氣，已改為通順的說法。

### Core 10 · denounce

- Before: Relatives denounce the fraud outside the bank every Friday. 親屬每週五在銀行外譴責詐欺。
- After: Protesters denounce the fraud outside the bank every Friday. 抗議民眾每週五在銀行外譴責這項詐欺行為。
- Why: 例句主詞 Relatives 與情境不自然，且中文「親屬」不貼切，已修正。

### Core 11 · acting

- Before: The acting manager approved refunds while the owner traveled. 老闆旅行時，代理經理批准退款。
- After: The acting manager approved refunds while the owner traveled. 老闆出外旅行期間，代理經理核准了退款。
- Why: 詞性含名詞與形容詞，但主要意義宜與例句一致；補充義「演戲」不夠自然，改為「演戲；表演」，譯文更通順。

### Core 11 · envy

- Before: I envy her calm voice during difficult phone calls. 我很羨慕她講棘手電話時，聲音仍能保持冷靜。
- After: I envy her calm voice during difficult phone calls. 我羨慕她在棘手的電話中仍保持冷靜的聲音。
- Why: 原中文「艱難電話中的冷靜聲音」不自然，應改寫得更通順。

### Core 4 · cycle

- Before: The life cycle of a butterfly has four stages. 蝴蝶的生命週期有四個階段。
- After: The washing cycle ended while we prepared dinner. 我們準備晚餐時，洗衣程序結束了。
- Why: cycle 最常見的意思是「週期」，「行程」不是常用義；例句譯文也不自然。

### Core 6 · penny

- Before: A penny rolled under the vending machine and disappeared. 一枚便士滾進販賣機底下不見了。
- After: A penny rolled under the vending machine and disappeared. 一枚一分錢硬幣滾進販賣機底下不見了。
- Why: penny 最常指美國一分錢硬幣，「便士」為英國用法，應作補充。

### Core 1 · cover

- Before: Put the cover back on the rice pot. 把蓋子蓋回飯鍋上。
- After: Please cover the rice pot with a lid. 請用蓋子把飯鍋蓋起來。
- Why: cover 作為動詞最常見的意思是「覆蓋」，應作為主要詞義，「蓋子」改列於補充。

### Core 6 · carrier

- Before: A mail carrier rescued letters from the flooded porch. 郵差從淹水的門廊搶救出信件。
- After: A mail carrier rescued letters from the flooded porch. 郵遞員從淹水的門廊搶救出信件。
- Why: carrier 最常見意思是運送者，郵遞員屬於特定用法，不宜作為主要詞義。

### Core 6 · segment

- Before: The first segment of the class video shows how to fold paper cranes. 課堂影片的第一部分示範如何摺紙鶴。
- After: The orange segment slipped from the child's chopsticks. 那瓣橘子從孩子的筷子間滑落。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「部分」，「一瓣」過於特殊。

### Core 3 · quote

- Before: The plumber gave a quote for fixing the leak. 水電工提供修漏水的報價。
- After: The teacher asked us to quote one line from the poem. 老師要我們從這首詩裡引用一行。
- Why: 最常見的詞義是「引用」，「報價」應列為補充義，例句也需配合主要詞義。

### Core 3 · shock

- Before: The sudden shock knocked cups from the shelf. 一陣突如其來的震動，把杯子從架上震落。
- After: The sad news came as a shock to the whole class. 這個壞消息讓全班同學都很震驚。
- Why: 最常見的詞義是「震驚」而非「震動」，例句也需配合主要詞義。

### Core 8 · desirable

- Before: Shade makes that corner desirable during summer lunch hours. 夏季午餐時段，那個有遮蔭的角落特別搶手。
- After: Shade makes that corner desirable during summer lunch hours. 夏季午餐時段，那個有遮蔭的角落特別令人嚮往。
- Why: 「搶手的」非最常見詞義，應為「令人嚮往的」；補充詞義也應調整。

### Core 8 · deadly

- Before: The deadly currents form near the rocks after heavy rain. 大雨後，岩石附近會形成致命急流。
- After: The deadly currents form near the rocks after heavy rain. 大雨後，岩石附近會形成致命的急流。
- Why: 副詞義「非常」過於口語且不精確，應改為「極度的」等較常見義。

### Core 8 · burning

- Before: A burning smell drifted from the closed bakery. 一股燃燒味從關門的麵包店飄出。
- After: A burning smell drifted from the closed bakery. 一股燃燒的味道從關門的麵包店飄出。
- Why: 「火辣的」不精確，應為「灼熱的」；譯文「燃燒味」稍不自然。

### Core 1 · cut

- Before: The cut on his finger needed a bandage. 他手指上的傷口需要包紮。
- After: She will cut the cake into eight pieces. 她會把蛋糕切成八塊。
- Why: cut 最常見詞義為動詞「切」，不是名詞傷口；例句也需對應。

### Core 1 · general

- Before: The general rule is simple for visitors here. 這裡對訪客的一般規定很簡單。
- After: The general rule is simple for visitors here. 這裡給訪客的一般規則很簡單。
- Why: general 最常見詞義為「一般的」，「基本的」不精確。

### Core 12 · facilitate

- Before: Clear signs facilitate boarding at the busy ferry terminal. 清楚的標示，方便旅客在繁忙的渡輪站登船。
- After: Clear signs facilitate boarding at the busy ferry terminal. 清楚的標示使旅客在繁忙的渡輪站更容易登船。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「促進；使便利」，「有助於」不準確；例句翻譯也不夠貼近原意。

### Core 12 · resume

- Before: Classes resume after workers clean the flooded rooms. 工人清理淹水教室後，課程恢復。
- After: Classes resume after workers clean the flooded rooms. 工人清理完淹水的教室後，課程就會重新開始。
- Why: 動詞最常見詞義為「重新開始」；「履歷」「摘要」屬名詞且拼寫不同，不適合列入。

### Core 12 · orientation

- Before: The map showed the correct orientation of the old temple. 地圖顯示舊寺廟的正確方向。
- After: The map showed the correct orientation of the old temple. 地圖顯示了這座舊寺廟的正確方位。
- Why: orientation 在例句中指方位，譯為「方向」不夠精準，補充義也重複。

### Core 12 · functional

- Before: The chart showed functional differences between the two clinics. 圖表顯示兩間診所的功能差異。
- After: The chart showed functional differences between the two clinics. 圖表顯示這兩間診所在功能上的差異。
- Why: 譯文略顯生硬，宜改為較自然的說法。

### Core 12 · harry

- Before: Creditors began to harry him with calls before breakfast. 債主早餐前就開始打電話騷擾他。
- After: Creditors began to harry him with calls before breakfast. 債主在早餐前就開始打電話騷擾他。
- Why: harry 為罕用字，且例句情境不貼近高中生日常。

### Core 12 · pant

- Before: After the sprint, I could only pant beside the fence. 衝刺後，我只能在圍欄旁喘氣。
- After: After the sprint, I could only pant beside the fence. 衝刺之後，我只能在圍欄旁喘氣。
- Why: 補充詞義「喘息」與主要詞義重複。

### Core 9 · commodity

- Before: Coffee is a valuable commodity for many farming families in the mountains. 對許多山區農家來說，咖啡是很有價值的商品。
- After: Rice became the village commodity most affected by drought. 稻米成了村裡最受乾旱影響的農產品。
- Why: 主要義應為「商品」，「農產品」過窄；例句中譯也應對應。

### Core 9 · sentiment

- Before: Public sentiment shifted after the mayor apologized on television. 市長上電視道歉後民意轉變。
- After: Public sentiment shifted after the mayor apologized on television. 市長上電視道歉後，民意轉變了。
- Why: 主要義應為「情感」；「看法」較次要，補充義「情緒」不佳。

### Core 10 · indictment

- Before: The indictment named three bankers and one city official. 起訴書列名三名銀行員和一名市府官員。
- After: The indictment named three bankers and one city official. 起訴書上列名三名銀行員和一名市府官員。
- Why: 常見意義是「起訴」，補充「控告」重複；中文句子需更通順。

### Core 9 · bureaucracy

- Before: The bureaucracy resisted the mayor's reforms for months. 官僚體制數月來一直抗拒市長的改革。
- After: The bureaucracy resisted the mayor's reforms for months. 官僚體制數月來抗拒市長的改革。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「官僚體制」，「官僚人員」較少用，需對調。

### Core 10 · terminate

- Before: The landlord may terminate the lease after two missed payments. 房客漏繳兩次後房東可終止租約。
- After: The landlord may terminate the lease after two missed payments. 房客漏繳兩次後，房東可以終止租約。
- Why: 例句中文漏譯「房客」與語氣不自然，已補全並改順。

### Core 8 · undertake

- Before: Few climbers undertake that climb without checking the weather first. 很少登山者會不先查天氣就進行那趟攀登。
- After: Few climbers undertake that climb without checking the weather first. 很少登山者不先查天氣就嘗試那趟攀登。
- Why: undertake 主要意思是「承擔、著手做」，而非「嘗試」；例句用法也不自然。

### Core 14 · succumb

- Before: Weak seedlings may succumb to frost before spring arrives. 弱小幼苗可能在春天前凍死。
- After: Weak seedlings may succumb to frost before spring arrives. 弱小的幼苗可能在春天來臨前就被凍死。
- Why: succumb 最常見義為屈服，「死於」應列為補充；例句譯文也不夠自然完整。

### Core 14 · prominence

- Before: The young singer rose to prominence after her first album. 那位年輕歌手發行第一張專輯後就聲名大噪。
- After: The singer rose to prominence after her first album. 那位歌手在第一張專輯後聲名大噪。
- Why: prominence 最常見義為顯著或重要性，「突出物」較少見，例句也應配合主要詞義。

### Core 3 · sentence

- Before: The judge will sentence the burglar after lunch today. 法官將在今天午餐後對竊賊宣判。
- After: Please read the first sentence of the story aloud. 請大聲朗讀這個故事的第一句。
- Why: 最常見的意義是名詞「句子」，例句也應配合此義。

### Core 3 · separate

- Before: Keep separate towels for the baby and guests upstairs. 樓上要替嬰兒和客人備不同毛巾。
- After: Please separate the paper from the plastic before recycling. 回收前請把紙類和塑膠分開。
- Why: 最常見用法為動詞「分開」，原例句亦不自然。

### Core 5 · nerve

- Before: It took nerve to cross the swaying bridge. 走過搖晃吊橋需要勇氣。
- After: The doctor said the injury had damaged a nerve in his arm. 醫生說這個傷勢損傷了他手臂上的一條神經。
- Why: nerve 最常見的意思是「神經」，「勇氣」較次要，故主要詞義應調整。

### Core 5 · genuine

- Before: Her genuine concern showed when she packed extra medicine. 她多帶藥時顯出真誠關心。
- After: She bought a genuine leather wallet at the night market. 她在夜市買了一個真正的皮革皮夾。
- Why: genuine 最常見的意思是「真正的」，「真誠的」較次要，故主要詞義應調整。

### Core 5 · entertain

- Before: The puppet troupe will entertain children near the temple. 布袋戲團會在廟旁逗孩子開心。
- After: The puppet troupe will entertain children near the temple. 布袋戲團會在廟旁娛樂孩子。
- Why: 主要義「逗開心」不是最常見詞義；應為「娛樂」。

### Core 5 · dirty

- Before: The dirty rainwater splashed onto Mina's white sneakers. 髒雨水濺到米娜白球鞋上。
- After: The dirty rainwater splashed onto Mina's white sneakers. 髒兮兮的雨水濺到了米娜的白球鞋上。
- Why: exampleZh 漏掉所有格且語句生硬，已改得更自然。

### Core 5 · pitch

- Before: Coach Lin taught me how to pitch a baseball without hurting my arm. 林教練教我怎麼投棒球才不會傷到手臂。
- After: The pitcher tried to pitch the ball faster than before. 投手試著把球投得比以前更快。
- Why: 主要詞義應為投擲而非攤位，例句也需改用 pitch 的常見詞義。

### Core 5 · craft

- Before: Local artists craft bowls from driftwood after typhoons. 在地藝術家用颱風後漂流木做碗。
- After: She learned a traditional craft from her grandmother in Tainan. 她在台南向祖母學了一項傳統手工藝。
- Why: 詞性 n 的主要詞義應為手工藝，原例句動詞用法與詞義不符。

### Core 5 · strip

- Before: Cut a strip of cloth to tie the splint. 剪一條布來綁固定板。
- After: Cut a strip of cloth to tie the splint. 剪一條布來綁住固定夾板。
- Why: exampleZh 的固定板不自然，應為固定夾板。

### Core 12 · standing

- Before: Her standing in the class rose after she won the speech contest. 她贏得演講比賽後，在班上的地位提升了。
- After: Her standing in the class improved after she won the speech contest. 她贏得演講比賽後，在班上的地位提升了。
- Why: 主要意義應為「地位」，「起立」不是最常見義，且例句語意與原義不符。

### Core 12 · domain

- Before: Marine biology became Lin's domain after college graduation. 大學畢業後，海洋生物學成了林的領域。
- After: Marine biology became Lin's domain after he finished college. 大學畢業後，海洋生物學成了阿林的專門領域。
- Why: 原例句 Lin's 人物指涉不清且 exampleZh 略不自然，已修正。

### Core 2 · entire

- Before: The entire family searched the beach for her ring. 全家人在海灘找她的戒指。
- After: The entire family searched the beach for her ring. 全家人一起在海灘上找她的戒指。
- Why: 譯文漏譯 entire 與 searched for her ring 的部分語意，應補足。

### Core 2 · positive

- Before: Her positive attitude helped the team finish the repair. 她積極的態度幫團隊完成修理。
- After: Her positive attitude helped the team finish the repair. 她積極的態度幫助團隊完成了修理工作。
- Why: 譯文「幫團隊完成修理」不夠自然，應改為較通順的說法。

### Core 9 · erect

- Before: Workers erect a temporary stage beside the temple. 工人在廟旁搭起臨時舞台。
- After: The soldiers stood erect while the flag was raised. 升旗時士兵們筆直地站著。
- Why: erect 最常見為形容詞「直立的」，例句詞義也應與之相符。

### Core 9 · strive

- Before: Athletes strive for balance on the narrow beam. 運動員努力在窄木樑上保持平衡。
- After: Students strive to improve their English every day. 學生們每天努力提升自己的英文。
- Why: 原例句 strive for balance 搭配不自然，且不符常見用法。

### Core 6 · liberty

- Before: Living alone gave my cousin the liberty to decorate her room however she liked. 自己一個人住，讓表姊可以自由地照喜好布置房間。
- After: The new rules gave sailors liberty after six weeks at sea. 新規定讓水手出海六週後獲准上岸休假。
- Why: liberty 最常見的意思是「自由」，「休假」是罕見的特殊用法，不應作為主要字義。

### Core 6 · compromise

- Before: Their compromise allowed music until ten at night. 他們的妥協允許音樂播放到晚上十點。
- After: Their compromise allowed music until ten at night. 他們的折衷方案允許音樂播放到晚上十點。
- Why: 例句中 compromise 為「折衷方案」，與主要字義「妥協」不完全相符，譯文應調整。

### Core 9 · monkey

- Before: A monkey stole peanuts from an open backpack. 一隻猴子從打開的背包偷花生。
- After: A monkey stole peanuts from an open backpack. 一隻猴子從敞開的背包裡偷走了花生。
- Why: 例句譯文漏掉「偷走」的完整語意且不自然；補充義「胡鬧」不完整

### Core 9 · exceptional

- Before: Her exceptional hearing caught the faint alarm outside. 她出色的聽力聽見了外面微弱的警報聲。
- After: Her exceptional hearing caught the faint alarm outside. 她出色的聽力聽到了外面微弱的警報聲。
- Why: 最常見詞義是「傑出的」，而非「異常的」；譯文不自然

### Core 9 · contradiction

- Before: His smile was a contradiction to the harsh words. 他的笑容和刺耳言語形成矛盾。
- After: His warm smile was a contradiction of his harsh words. 他溫暖的笑容與刺耳的話語互相矛盾。
- Why: 例句搭配 contradiction to 不自然；譯文也不通順

### Core 9 · downtown

- Before: They moved downtown to live above the bakery. 他們搬到市中心住在麵包店樓上。
- After: They moved downtown to live above a bakery. 他們搬到市中心，住在一間麵包店樓上。
- Why: 補充義「往市中心」詞性不符且不自然；譯文缺少停頓略不自然

### Core 9 · certainty

- Before: She spoke with certainty about the ferry schedule. 她很有把握地談起渡輪的時刻表。
- After: She spoke with certainty about the ferry schedule. 她很有把握地談論渡輪的時刻表。
- Why: 名詞 certainty 主要指確定性或確定的事；譯文「談渡船時刻」不自然

### Core 1 · its

- Before: The cafe changed its menu after the chef retired. 主廚退休後，那間咖啡館換了自己的菜單。
- After: The cafe changed its menu after the chef retired. 主廚退休後，那間咖啡館換了它的菜單。
- Why: exampleZh 把 its 譯成「自己的」，與「它的」不符，應直接譯出。

### Core 2 · trouble

- Before: Sorry for all the trouble I caused you yesterday. 抱歉昨天給你添了這麼多麻煩。
- After: Engine trouble delayed the fishing boat until sunrise. 引擎故障，讓那艘漁船一直耽擱到日出。
- Why: trouble 最常見的意思是「麻煩」，「故障」應放在補充意思。

### Core 2 · customer

- Before: The customer returned because the soup tasted sour. 顧客因湯有酸味而回來。
- After: The customer returned because the soup tasted sour. 顧客因為湯有酸味而回來了。
- Why: 原中文翻譯過於簡略且不自然，需補足語意。

### Core 6 · counterpart

- Before: Her counterpart in Japan sent heirloom seeds from Hokkaido. 她在日本職位相當的同行寄來北海道的傳家種子。
- After: Her counterpart in Japan sent heirloom seeds from Hokkaido. 她在日本的職務相當者寄來北海道的傳家種子。
- Why: 「對應窗口」不是最常見詞義，應為「職務相當的人」。

### Core 6 · presidency

- Before: Her presidency began with a promise to improve public schools across the country. 她就任總統之初，就承諾要改善全國的公立學校。
- After: Her presidency began with a promise to improve public schools. 她的總統任期從承諾改善公立學校開始。
- Why: 最常見詞義是總統職位，原釋義為總裁職位，例句也不符。

### Core 7 · faint

- Before: A faint smell of smoke drifted from the kitchen. 淡淡煙味從廚房飄出。
- After: A faint smell of smoke drifted from the kitchen. 一股淡淡的煙味從廚房飄了出來。
- Why: meaningZh 應為形容詞最常見義「微弱的」，「淡淡的」較不常用；補充義調整。

### Core 8 · gravity

- Before: Earth's gravity sent the spilled marbles rolling down the sloped hallway. 灑出的彈珠受地心引力影響，沿著傾斜的走廊滾下去。
- After: Gravity pulled the spilled marbles down the sloped hallway. 重力使灑出的彈珠沿著傾斜的走廊滾下去。
- Why: 例句過長且中文「地心引力影響」與英文不對應，已簡化並對應翻譯。

### Core 3 · collect

- Before: Grandpa likes to collect train tickets from old journeys. 爺爺喜歡收集舊旅行的火車票。
- After: Grandpa likes to collect train tickets from old journeys. 爺爺喜歡收集舊旅程留下的火車票。
- Why: 「集中」不恰當，改為「收取」；例句翻譯語意不順。

### Core 14 · earring

- Before: One silver earring slipped through the bathroom drain. 一只銀耳環滑進浴室的排水孔。
- After: One silver earring slipped through the bathroom drain. 一隻銀耳環滑進了浴室的排水孔。
- Why: 「一只」為不當量詞用法，應為「一隻」，且語句不夠自然。

### Core 14 · ram

- Before: A ram with curled horns blocked the narrow farm path. 一頭長著捲角的公羊擋住了狹窄的農場小路。
- After: A metal ram cracked the locked warehouse door. 金屬撞槌撞裂了上鎖的倉庫門。
- Why: ram 作名詞最常見意思為公羊；譯文漏掉「的」，讀來不自然。

### Core 14 · plainly

- Before: She plainly refused the bribe and left the office. 她明白拒絕賄賂後離開辦公室。
- After: She plainly refused the bribe and left the office. 她明白拒絕了賄賂，然後離開辦公室。
- Why: 主要意思應為明白地；譯文漏掉「然後」，銜接不自然。

### Core 14 · provocative

- Before: A provocative poster drew complaints from nearby parents. 一張挑釁海報引來附近家長投訴。
- After: A provocative poster drew complaints from nearby parents. 一張挑釁的海報引來附近家長投訴。
- Why: 詞性 a / n 的 n 有誤，且譯文缺少「的」不自然。

### Core 14 · depot

- Before: Buses slept overnight at the depot beside the river. 公車整夜停在河邊車庫裡。
- After: Buses slept overnight at the depot beside the river. 公車整夜停在河邊的車庫裡。
- Why: depot 較常見意思為車站或倉庫，車庫非主要意思；譯文不自然。

### Core 6 · crew

- Before: The film crew waited for sunlight beside the temple pond. 攝影團隊在廟池旁等待陽光。
- After: The film crew waited for sunlight beside the temple pond. 劇組人員在廟池旁等待陽光。
- Why: crew 最常見的意思是全體工作人員，「團隊」不精確。

### Core 6 · welfare

- Before: The shelter checks each rescued dog's welfare before adoption. 收容所在送養前會確認每隻救援犬的健康與福祉。
- After: The shelter checks each rescued dog's welfare before adoption. 收容所在送養前會確認每隻救援犬的福祉。
- Why: welfare 最常見的意思是福利或福祉，「照護狀況」不是常見主要詞義。

### Core 6 · catholic

- Before: My aunt goes to a small Catholic church near the night market every Sunday. 我阿姨每個星期天都會去夜市附近的一間小天主教堂。
- After: My neighbor goes to a catholic church every Sunday morning. 我的鄰居每個週日早上都去天主教堂。
- Why: catholic 最常見的意思是天主教的，「廣泛的」較罕見。

### Core 10 · banner

- Before: A torn banner still hung above the bakery after the storm. 暴風雨過後，那面破損的布條仍掛在麵包店上方。
- After: A torn banner still hung above the bakery after the storm. 暴風雨過後，那面破損的橫幅仍掛在麵包店上方。
- Why: banner 最常見意思為橫幅；「傑出的」為罕見用法，且詞性不符。

### Core 10 · pal

- Before: Rina saved a seat for her pal at the crowded cinema. 莉娜在擁擠戲院替好友留了一個位子。
- After: Rina saved a seat for her pal at the crowded cinema. 莉娜在擁擠的戲院替好友留了一個位子。
- Why: exampleZh 讀來不夠自然，補上「的」較通順。

### Core 5 · slight

- Before: A slight fever kept Jonah away from the baby. 喬納只是微微發燒，仍不敢靠近嬰兒。
- After: A slight fever kept Jonah away from the baby. 輕微發燒讓喬納不敢靠近嬰兒。
- Why: 中文翻譯加入「只是」「仍」等原文沒有的意思，應忠實翻譯。

### Core 13 · oath

- Before: The witness repeated the oath in a steady voice. 證人用穩定聲音重複誓言。
- After: The witness repeated the oath in a steady voice. 證人用平穩的聲音複誦誓言。
- Why: 「咒罵」用詞不完整，且中文翻譯「用穩定聲音重複」不自然。

### Core 13 · devotion

- Before: Her devotion to the injured child moved the nurses. 她對受傷孩子的投入感動了護理師。
- After: Her devotion to the injured child moved the nurses. 她對受傷孩子的奉獻精神感動了護理師。
- Why: devotion 最常見意思是奉獻或摯愛，「投入」不夠貼切。

### Core 13 · realism

- Before: Realism in the painting made the wet street glisten. 畫作逼真的筆觸，讓濕漉漉的街道閃著光。
- After: The painter's realism made the wet street seem to glisten. 畫家的寫實手法讓濕漉漉的街道彷彿閃著光。
- Why: 補充義重複，例句語意與翻譯也不夠一致。

### Core 13 · touchdown

- Before: The plane's touchdown was rough enough to wake sleeping passengers. 飛機著地時很顛簸，吵醒了熟睡乘客。
- After: The plane's touchdown was rough enough to wake sleeping passengers. 飛機著陸時很顛簸，吵醒了熟睡的乘客。
- Why: touchdown 最常見義為橄欖球觸地得分，且「著地」不如「著陸」自然。

### Core 13 · synthetic

- Before: Synthetic leather covered the cafe's bright yellow chairs. 人造皮革包著咖啡館亮黃色椅子。
- After: Synthetic leather covered the cafe's bright yellow chairs. 人造皮革包覆著咖啡館亮黃色的椅子。
- Why: 補充義「合成物」較罕用，且翻譯漏了自然的「包覆」語氣。

### Core 7 · farming

- Before: Organic farming saved the hillside from chemical runoff. 有機耕作讓山坡免受化學逕流污染。
- After: Organic farming saved the hillside from chemical runoff. 有機農業讓山坡免受化學逕流污染。
- Why: farming 最常見意思為農業，耕作為補充；「污染」與例句略不符，已調整。

### Core 11 · offshore

- Before: Offshore wind turbines now supply electricity to many homes in central Taiwan. 離岸風力發電機現在為台灣中部許多家庭供電。
- After: Offshore wind turbines now power many towns along the western coast. 離岸風力發電機如今為西部沿海許多城鎮供電。
- Why: 最常見意義為「近海的、離岸的」，「吹向海面的」是較罕見義，應調整順序與例句。

### Core 14 · lunar

- Before: Astronauts brought lunar rocks back to Earth for scientists to study. 太空人把月球岩石帶回地球，供科學家研究。
- After: Traffic during lunar New Year filled every mountain road. 農曆新年期間，車潮塞滿每條山路。
- Why: lunar最常見意義是「月亮的」，「農曆的」應列為補充；例句也不夠自然，需一併修正。

### Core 5 · entitle

- Before: This ticket will entitle you to one free drink. 憑這張票可以兌換一杯免費飲料。
- After: This ticket will entitle you to one free drink. 憑這張票你可以享有一杯免費飲料。
- Why: 例句中譯「兌換」不精確，應為「享有」；補充義「給命名」不完整。

### Core 5 · curious

- Before: A curious child opened every drawer in the guesthouse. 好奇孩子打開民宿每個抽屜。
- After: A curious child opened every drawer in the guesthouse. 好奇的孩子打開了民宿的每個抽屜。
- Why: exampleZh 不通順，缺少「的」與「了」，讀起來不自然。

### Core 5 · strain

- Before: Too much lifting put strain on his injured shoulder. 搬太多重物加重他受傷肩膀負擔。
- After: Too much lifting put strain on his injured shoulder. 搬太多重物，使他受傷的肩膀負擔更重。
- Why: 動詞用法以「拉傷」較常見；exampleZh 不通順，缺少「的」。

### Core 5 · conscious

- Before: She stayed conscious while waiting for the ambulance. 她等救護車時保持清醒。
- After: She stayed conscious while waiting for the ambulance. 她在等救護車時一直保持清醒。
- Why: conscious 最常見義為「有意識的」，應列為主要釋義。

### Core 9 · couch

- Before: Dad fell asleep on the couch before the movie ended. 電影結束前爸爸就在長沙發上睡著。
- After: Dad fell asleep on the couch before the movie ended. 電影結束前爸爸就在長沙發上睡著了。
- Why: 「表達」過於簡略且易誤解，動詞義應為「措辭表達」。

### Core 9 · bull

- Before: A bull blocked the farm road until the gate opened. 一頭公牛擋住農路直到大門打開。
- After: A bull blocked the farm road until the gate opened. 一頭公牛擋在農路上，直到大門打開。
- Why: 中文翻譯讀來不夠自然，漏掉「擋在路上」的語意。

### Core 3 · attract

- Before: Night blossoms attract moths to the garden wall. 夜間開花植物吸引蛾停在花園牆上。
- After: Night blossoms attract moths to the garden wall. 夜間開花的植物會把蛾吸引到花園牆邊。
- Why: 原中文誤譯為蛾停在牆上，與原句意義不符。

### Core 3 · plus

- Before: Two bowls of noodles plus tea cost two hundred. 兩碗麵加茶要兩百元。
- After: Two bowls of noodles plus tea cost two hundred dollars. 兩碗麵加茶要兩百元。
- Why: 原句沒有貨幣單位，中文補了「元」，英文宜補dollars使兩者相符。

### Core 9 · tolerance

- Before: Years abroad increased his tolerance for spicy food. 多年在外提高了他對辣食的耐受度。
- After: Teachers should show tolerance toward students who think differently. 老師應該對想法不同的學生表現出寬容。
- Why: 最常見的意思是「寬容」，「耐受度」較不常用，例句也一併配合。

### Core 9 · revenge

- Before: After losing the game, Tom planned his revenge for the next match. 輸了比賽後，湯姆計劃在下一場比賽復仇。
- After: He dreamed of revenge long after the trial had ended. 審判結束很久以後，他仍夢想著復仇。
- Why: 原例句 His revenge kept him awake 不自然，中文也與英文不一致。

### Core 9 · chorus

- Before: A chorus of children echoed through the alley. 孩子們的齊聲歌唱在巷弄迴盪。
- After: The school chorus practices every Tuesday after class. 學校合唱團每週二下課後練習。
- Why: 名詞最常見的意思是「合唱團」，而非「齊聲歌唱」。

### Core 9 · cult

- Before: The cult met secretly in the abandoned theater. 那個邪教團體在廢棄劇院裡祕密聚會。
- After: The cult met secretly in the abandoned theater. 那個邪教在廢棄的劇院祕密聚會。
- Why: cult 最常見的意思是「邪教」，「信徒團體」不準確，譯文也需對應。

### Core 5 · explode

- Before: Firecrackers explode loudly outside the temple every Lunar New Year's Eve. 每年除夕夜，廟外的鞭炮都會爆炸作響。
- After: Firecrackers explode outside the temple at midnight tonight. 今晚午夜時鞭炮在廟外爆響。
- Why: explode 最常見義為爆炸，爆響屬較少用義，應作補充；例句也不自然。

### Core 4 · signal

- Before: The red signal warned boats to stay outside the harbor. 紅色訊號警告船隻留在港外。
- After: The red signal warned boats to stay outside the harbor. 紅色信號警告船隻留在港外。
- Why: 「信號」為大陸用語，台灣慣用「訊號」。

### Core 4 · emphasis

- Before: The coach put emphasis on rest after hard practice. 教練強調，高強度練習後一定要休息。
- After: The coach put emphasis on rest after hard practice. 教練強調高強度練習後要充分休息。
- Why: 名詞 emphasis 的主要意思應為名詞「強調；重視」，譯句需對應原句。

### Core 4 · capture

- Before: The camera can capture fireflies without using flash. 這台相機不用閃光燈也能拍下螢火蟲。
- After: The camera can capture fireflies without using flash. 這台相機不用閃光燈也能捕捉螢火蟲的身影。
- Why: capture 最常見的意思是「捕捉」，「拍下」應列為補充。

### Core 4 · foundation

- Before: The foundation of the house cracked after repeated flooding. 房子地基在多次淹水後裂開。
- After: The foundation of the house cracked after repeated flooding. 房子的地基在多次淹水後裂開了。
- Why: 最常見意義應為「基礎」；補充中「建立」詞性不符，應為「創立」。

### Core 3 · lord

- Before: The villagers once called the landowner their lord. 村民曾稱地主為他們的統治者。
- After: The villagers once called the landowner their lord. 村民曾稱地主為他們的領主。
- Why: lord 最常見義為「上帝；主」，例句 lord 應譯為領主，原譯統治者不自然。

### Core 3 · hurt

- Before: My wrist began to hurt after carrying groceries upstairs. 提雜貨上樓後我的手腕開始痛。
- After: My wrist began to hurt after carrying groceries upstairs. 提雜貨上樓後，我的手腕開始疼痛。
- Why: hurt 主要詞義應為動詞「使受傷；疼痛」，「痛」過於簡略且詞性不符。

### Core 3 · progress

- Before: Her walking progress surprised the therapist this month. 這個月，她走路能力的進步讓治療師十分驚訝。
- After: Her walking progress surprised the therapist this month. 這個月，她走路方面的進步讓治療師十分驚訝。
- Why: 補充義「前進」略少用，應補上較常見的「進展」；例句語意可更自然。

### Core 3 · screen

- Before: The outdoor screen showed cartoons beside the temple tonight. 今晚戶外銀幕在廟旁播放卡通。
- After: The outdoor screen showed cartoons beside the temple tonight. 今晚戶外螢幕在廟旁播放卡通。
- Why: screen 最常見義為螢幕，銀幕較次要，應調整順序與譯文。

### Core 3 · spot

- Before: This quiet spot overlooks the whole fishing port below. 從這個安靜的地方，可以俯瞰整座漁港。
- After: There was a small spot on my white shirt after lunch. 午餐後，我的白襯衫上有一個小汙點。
- Why: 名詞 spot 最常見義為斑點，地點為次要義，應調整主要義與例句。

### Core 7 · rub

- Before: Please rub sesame oil gently onto the bruised shoulder. 把芝麻油輕輕擦在瘀青肩膀上。
- After: Please rub sesame oil gently onto the bruised shoulder. 請把芝麻油輕輕擦在瘀青的肩膀上。
- Why: 補充義「難處」為名詞罕用義，與主要詞性不符，改為較常見的「搓揉」。

### Core 7 · concerning

- Before: Passengers asked questions concerning the missing luggage at the service counter. 旅客在服務櫃台詢問遺失行李問題。
- After: Passengers asked questions concerning the missing luggage at the service counter. 旅客在服務櫃台詢問有關遺失行李的問題。
- Why: 中文翻譯漏掉 questions 與 concerning 的語意，讀來不自然。

### Core 7 · mouse

- Before: A wireless mouse disappeared beneath the hotel bed. 一隻無線滑鼠掉到旅館床底下。
- After: A mouse ran under the hotel bed last night. 昨晚有一隻老鼠跑到旅館床底下。
- Why: mouse 最常見的意思是老鼠，滑鼠應為補充義；例句也需配合主要義。

### Core 7 · hunting

- Before: Mushroom hunting kept them in the cedar forest until dusk. 他們為了採菇，在杉林裡一直待到黃昏。
- After: Hunting is not allowed in this national park. 這座國家公園內禁止狩獵。
- Why: hunting 最常見的意思是狩獵，主要詞義「尋找」不當，例句也需配合。

### Core 7 · adventure

- Before: Their canoe adventure ended at a quiet sandbar. 他們划著獨木舟冒險，最後抵達一處安靜的沙洲。
- After: Their canoe adventure ended at a quiet sandbar. 他們的獨木舟冒險最後在一處安靜的沙洲結束。
- Why: 名詞不應補充動詞義「冒險」，翻譯也多加了原句沒有的內容。

### Core 7 · flesh

- Before: Mango flesh stuck to the knife in golden strips. 芒果果肉一條條黏在刀上。
- After: The knife cut deep into the flesh of his arm. 刀子深深割進他手臂的肉裡。
- Why: flesh 最常見義為肉，果肉應為補充義；例句也需配合。

### Core 7 · weakness

- Before: The fever left a weakness in his legs for days. 退燒後的好幾天，他的雙腿仍然虛弱無力。
- After: The fever left him with weakness in his legs for days. 發燒讓他的雙腿虛弱無力好幾天。
- Why: 原句語意與翻譯不夠吻合，翻譯也加入原句沒有的內容。

### Core 8 · cabin

- Before: Their cabin overlooked pines and a narrow winter creek. 他們的小屋俯瞰松林和狹窄冬溪。
- After: Their cabin overlooked pines and a narrow winter creek. 他們的小木屋俯瞰著松林和一條狹窄的冬季溪流。
- Why: 詞性誤標含vt；例句中文「狹窄冬溪」不自然，已改為通順說法。

### Core 11 · stalk

- Before: Detectives watched the thief stalk the jeweler through the market. 偵探看著小偷在市場裡跟蹤珠寶商。
- After: The farmer cut each corn stalk close to the ground. 農夫把每根玉米莖貼近地面割下。
- Why: stalk 名詞「莖」較常見，且詞性列 n 在先，主要詞義應為莖

### Core 11 · conserve

- Before: The chef tried to conserve water by rinsing vegetables together. 主廚把蔬菜一起沖洗來節省用水。
- After: The chef tried to conserve water by rinsing vegetables together. 主廚把蔬菜一起沖洗來節約用水。
- Why: 詞性 n 不適合主義，conserve 動詞宜用「節約」較自然

### Core 1 · police

- Before: The police directed traffic after the concert ended. 音樂會結束後警方指揮交通。
- After: The police directed traffic after the concert ended. 音樂會結束後警察指揮交通。
- Why: 名詞最常見義應為「警察」，「警方」為補充義。

### Core 13 · feminism

- Before: Feminism shaped the magazine's bold coverage of factory women. 女權主義影響雜誌大膽報導女工。
- After: Feminism shaped the magazine's bold coverage of factory women. 女性主義影響了這本雜誌對女工的大膽報導。
- Why: 臺灣慣用「女性主義」而非「女權主義」；例句翻譯也漏掉「的」並不通順。

### Core 13 · lever

- Before: Pull the red lever to release the loading gate. 拉紅色槓桿即可放下裝貨門。
- After: Pull the red lever to release the loading gate. 拉下紅色控制桿就能打開裝貨門。
- Why: lever 最常見的意思是槓桿；例句中的「紅色槓桿」不自然，應譯為控制桿。

### Core 13 · probation

- Before: The offender met his counselor weekly during probation. 緩刑期間，那名違規者每週見輔導員。
- After: The new employee met her manager weekly during probation. 試用期間，這位新員工每週都和主管見面。
- Why: probation 最常用義為試用期，緩刑較少見；原例句 offender 稱為違規者也不當。

### Core 14 · recess

- Before: Children traded stickers during recess behind the gym. 下課時間，孩子們在體育館後方交換貼紙。
- After: Children traded stickers during recess behind the gym. 孩子們在體育館後方的下課時間交換貼紙。
- Why: 例句中譯「在…休息時間交換」不自然，應改為「在…的下課時間」。

### Core 14 · multitude

- Before: A multitude of lanterns floated above the river. 無數燈籠飄過河面。
- After: A multitude of lanterns floated above the river. 無數燈籠飄浮在河面上。
- Why: multitude 為名詞，「無數」是形容詞性，主要意義應為「大量」；例句中譯「飄過河面」與 above 不符。

### Core 10 · mighty

- Before: The mighty river swallowed the old ferry dock overnight. 洶湧河水一夜間吞沒舊渡船碼頭。
- After: The mighty river swallowed the old ferry dock overnight. 洶湧的河水一夜之間吞沒了舊渡船碼頭。
- Why: exampleZh 漏掉「的」且語句不通順，補充義也過於簡略。

### Core 10 · pesticide

- Before: The farmer stopped using pesticide near the beehives last spring. 農夫去年春天停止在蜂箱附近用殺蟲劑。
- After: The farmer stopped using pesticide near the beehives last spring. 農夫去年春天停止在蜂箱附近使用殺蟲劑。
- Why: 譯文「用」口語不自然，改為「使用」較通順。

### Core 7 · toilet

- Before: The public toilet behind the station had no paper. 車站後方公廁沒有衛生紙。
- After: The public toilet behind the station had no paper. 車站後方的公廁沒有衛生紙。
- Why: exampleZh 缺少「的」，讀來不自然；其餘無誤，僅微調。

### Core 7 · delicate

- Before: Two delicate tea cups rattled when the train passed. 兩只細緻茶杯在火車經過時輕響。
- After: Two delicate tea cups rattled when the train passed. 兩個精緻的茶杯在火車經過時輕輕作響。
- Why: delicate 最常見意思為易碎脆弱，「細緻」較次要；「兩只」用詞不自然。

### Core 7 · envelope

- Before: The envelope contained two tickets and a pressed fern. 信封裡有兩張票和壓乾蕨葉。
- After: The envelope contained two tickets and a pressed fern. 信封裡有兩張票和一片壓乾的蕨葉。
- Why: exampleZh 「壓乾蕨葉」不自然，且漏掉量詞。

### Core 7 · suck

- Before: The pump can suck water from the flooded basement. 幫浦能從淹水地下室吸水。
- After: The pump can suck water from the flooded basement. 幫浦能從淹水的地下室把水吸出來。
- Why: 「很糟」為俚語不適合作補充義；exampleZh 稍不自然。

### Core 7 · drunk

- Before: A drunk tourist sang softly outside the hostel. 醉酒旅客在旅舍外輕聲唱歌。
- After: A drunk tourist sang softly outside the hostel. 一名醉酒的旅客在旅舍外輕聲唱歌。
- Why: 「喝醉的人」是名詞用法，與詞性 a 不符。

### Core 9 · viable

- Before: Taking the MRT is a viable way to avoid traffic downtown. 搭捷運是避開市區塞車的可行方法。
- After: Only three viable seeds sprouted after the long drought. 長期乾旱後只有三顆能生長的種子發芽。
- Why: viable 最常見詞義是「可行的」，「能生長的」應列為補充。

### Core 9 · nick

- Before: A tiny nick appeared on the chef's knife. 主廚刀上出現一個小缺口。
- After: A tiny nick appeared on the chef's knife. 主廚的刀上出現了一個小缺口。
- Why: 例句中文漏掉所有格「的」，讀來不自然。

### Core 9 · compact

- Before: Her compact umbrella fit inside a jacket pocket. 她的小巧雨傘可放進外套口袋。
- After: Her compact umbrella fit inside a jacket pocket. 她的小巧雨傘可以放進外套口袋。
- Why: 補充詞義「壓縮」詞性不符，且例句中文略不自然。

### Core 9 · albeit

- Before: The soup was tasty, albeit far too salty. 這湯雖然實在太鹹但很好喝。
- After: The soup was tasty, albeit far too salty. 這湯很好喝，雖然實在太鹹了。
- Why: 例句中文語序錯誤，意思被扭曲。

### Core 9 · skip

- Before: You can skip the first chapter if you already know the basics. 如果你已經懂基本概念，可以跳過第一章。
- After: Do not skip breakfast before the long ride. 長途騎乘前不要不吃早餐。
- Why: skip 最常見詞義是「跳過」，「不吃」應列為補充；例句中文也很拗口。

### Core 9 · vocal

- Before: Vocal training strengthened her voice before the audition. 聲樂訓練在試唱前強化她的嗓音。
- After: Vocal training strengthened her voice before the audition. 試唱前，聲樂訓練強化了她的嗓音。
- Why: vocal 的主要詞義應為嗓音的，且「聲樂的」與例句重複，中文語序也需調整。

### Core 9 · transit

- Before: City transit workers reopened the station before rush hour. 大眾運輸員工在尖峰前重開車站。
- After: City transit workers reopened the station before rush hour. 市區大眾運輸員工在尖峰時段前重新開放了車站。
- Why: 例句中文把 City 漏譯，且語氣不自然。

### Core 11 · relentless

- Before: The relentless guard refused water to the exhausted prisoners. 冷酷守衛拒絕給疲憊囚犯水喝。
- After: The relentless rain forced us to cancel the school picnic. 持續不斷的大雨使我們取消了學校野餐。
- Why: 最常見義為「持續不斷的」，原例句用法偏離，已改為常見用法。

### Core 6 · mixed

- Before: The soup had mixed mushrooms from three mountain farms. 那鍋湯用了三座山區農場的混合菇類。
- After: The chef served a mixed salad with tomatoes and cucumbers. 主廚端上一份有番茄和小黃瓜的綜合沙拉。
- Why: 原例句 mixed mushrooms 不自然，且句意與中文翻譯不夠貼切，已改寫。

### Core 12 · jumper

- Before: The jumper cleared the puddle and landed beside the gate. 那名跳躍者越過水坑，落在門邊。
- After: She wore a warm wool jumper to school on the cold morning. 寒冷的早晨，她穿了一件保暖的羊毛衣去上學。
- Why: jumper 最常見義為毛衣，主要義與例句需調整。

### Core 4 · motion

- Before: The boat's motion made several tourists feel sick. 船身晃動讓幾位遊客覺得噁心。
- After: The boat's motion made several tourists feel sick. 船身的晃動讓幾位遊客覺得噁心。
- Why: motion 最常見的意義是「動作；運動」，「晃動」不是主要意義。

### Core 4 · tooth

- Before: My tooth hurt whenever I drank iced tea. 我每次喝冰茶牙齒就痛。
- After: My tooth hurt whenever I drank iced tea. 我每次喝冰茶，牙齒就會痛。
- Why: exampleZh 語氣略不自然，已調整為較通順的說法。

### Core 4 · selection

- Before: The bakery's selection was best before seven in the morning. 麵包店早上七點前選擇最多。
- After: The bakery's selection of bread was best before seven in the morning. 麵包店的麵包種類在早上七點前最齊全。
- Why: 例句中 selection 指可選的種類，與「選擇」不符；譯文也誤譯為選擇最多。

### Core 7 · presentation

- Before: Her presentation on jade necklaces made every buyer lean closer. 她介紹翡翠項鍊時每位買家都湊近看。
- After: Her presentation on jade necklaces made every buyer lean closer. 她關於翡翠項鍊的簡報讓每位買家都湊近看。
- Why: 最常見詞義為簡報或報告，介紹不是主要義；例句譯文也應對應簡報。

### Core 7 · custom

- Before: The tailor made a custom jacket for the violinist. 裁縫替那位小提琴手做了訂製外套。
- After: It is a local custom to bring fruit when visiting a neighbor. 拜訪鄰居時帶水果是當地的習俗。
- Why: 名詞最常見意思是習俗，原主要義訂製的是形容詞，與詞性及例句不符。

### Core 11 · elevate

- Before: A wooden crate can elevate the speaker above the crowded platform. 木箱可以把講者墊高，讓他高出擁擠月台上的人群。
- After: A wooden crate can elevate the speaker above the crowded platform. 木箱可以把講者抬高到擁擠的月台人群之上。
- Why: exampleZh 未忠實翻譯 elevate 的句意，改為與原句相符的譯文。

### Core 11 · foul

- Before: Foul water leaked from the pipe beneath the seafood stall. 海鮮攤底下的水管漏出惡臭污水。
- After: Foul water leaked from the pipe beneath the seafood stall. 海鮮攤底下的水管漏出惡臭的污水。
- Why: 原內容大致可用，但補充義應包含常見的「惡劣的」，譯文更自然。

### Core 8 · random

- Before: A random stranger paid for her tea at the station. 一位偶然遇見的陌生人在車站替她付茶錢。
- After: A random stranger paid for her tea at the station. 一位素不相識的陌生人在車站替她付茶錢。
- Why: random最常見的意思是「隨機的」，不是「偶然的」；例句翻譯也不夠自然

### Core 7 · honestly

- Before: She honestly thought the fried oysters tasted better yesterday. 她真心覺得炸蚵昨天比較好吃。
- After: She honestly told her teacher that she had forgotten the homework. 她誠實地告訴老師自己忘了寫作業。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「誠實地」；原例句未突顯此義，補充義也重複。

### Core 7 · broadly

- Before: The new rule applies broadly to coastal rentals. 新規定廣泛適用海岸出租屋。
- After: The new rule applies broadly to all students in our school. 新規定廣泛適用於我們學校的所有學生。
- Why: 例句情境不貼近學生，中文漏譯「to」後的對象且不自然。

### Core 7 · alternatively

- Before: You can alternatively take the coastal bus after lunch. 或者你午餐後可以搭海岸公車。
- After: We could take the bus; alternatively, we could ride our bikes. 我們可以搭公車，或者也可以騎腳踏車。
- Why: 例句 alternatively 位置不自然，中文翻譯語序也不自然。

### Core 7 · attempted

- Before: The attempted robbery failed when the alarm rang. 警報響起後那起搶劫沒有得逞。
- After: The attempted robbery failed when the alarm rang. 警報一響，那起搶劫未遂。
- Why: 補充義「企圖的」不恰當，應移除。

### Core 5 · sail

- Before: We sail only when the captain trusts the weather. 船長相信天氣時我們才航行。
- After: We sail only when the captain trusts the weather. 船長信任天氣狀況時，我們才會出航。
- Why: exampleZh「船長相信天氣」不自然，trusts the weather 應譯為信任天氣狀況。

### Core 5 · cooking

- Before: Grandma's cooking filled the stairwell with the aroma of soy sauce. 奶奶煮飯時，醬油香瀰漫整座樓梯間。
- After: Grandma's cooking filled the stairwell with the aroma of soy sauce. 奶奶煮的菜讓醬油香瀰漫整座樓梯間。
- Why: cooking 最常見意思為烹飪，「飯菜」應列為補充義；譯文也略有不精確。

### Core 5 · mate

- Before: Kai and his best mate shared fried chicken after the basketball game. 籃球賽結束後，阿凱和他最好的夥伴分著吃炸雞。
- After: The plumber's mate carried extra washers for the sink repair. 水電工的助手帶了修水槽用的備用墊圈。
- Why: mate 最常見意思是夥伴或同伴，「助手」較少見，不宜作為主要詞義。

### Core 5 · invitation

- Before: The wedding invitation arrived with a pressed jasmine flower. 婚禮請帖夾著壓乾茉莉花寄到。
- After: The wedding invitation arrived with a pressed jasmine flower. 婚禮請帖附著一朵壓乾的茉莉花寄到了。
- Why: invitation 最常見意思是邀請，「請帖」是次要義，且補充義重複。

### Core 5 · generous

- Before: A generous neighbor shared soup after our stove broke. 爐子壞掉後慷慨的鄰居分我們湯。
- After: A generous neighbor shared soup after our stove broke. 爐子壞掉後，慷慨的鄰居分了湯給我們。
- Why: 補充義「大量的」不自然，應為大方的或豐盛的；譯文也欠通順。

### Core 1 · security

- Before: The hotel added cameras to improve security at night. 飯店加裝了監視器來加強夜間的安全。
- After: Airport security checked every bag before boarding today. 今天登機前機場安檢檢查了每件行李。
- Why: 主要詞義應為「安全」，「安檢人員」不是最常見的意思。

### Core 7 · lemon

- Before: A bright lemon rolled under the restaurant refrigerator. 一顆亮黃檸檬滾到餐廳冰箱下。
- After: A bright yellow lemon rolled under the restaurant refrigerator. 一顆亮黃色檸檬滾到餐廳冰箱下。
- Why: 原例句 bright lemon 易誤解，且中文多出「黃」，兩者不一致，已修正。

### Core 13 · imitation

- Before: The imitation jade bracelet turned green in the rain. 仿玉手環在雨中變綠。
- After: The imitation jade bracelet turned green in the rain. 那條仿玉手環淋雨後變綠了。
- Why: meaningZh「仿製的」是形容詞，與名詞詞性不符，應為「仿製品」；補充義重複

### Core 5 · friendly

- Before: The friendly driver waited while Grandpa climbed aboard slowly. 友善司機等爺爺慢慢上車。
- After: The friendly driver waited while Grandpa climbed aboard slowly. 那位友善的司機等爺爺慢慢上車。
- Why: 中文翻譯漏掉 The 與 while 的語氣，讀起來不自然，已改為通順句子。

### Core 5 · extreme

- Before: The extreme heat kept fruit sellers under wet towels. 天氣酷熱難耐，水果攤販只好一直披著濕毛巾降溫。
- After: The extreme heat kept fruit sellers under wet towels. 酷熱讓水果攤販只好披著濕毛巾降溫。
- Why: 補充釋義「極限」不精確，且原譯文添加了原句沒有的內容。

### Core 14 · daytime

- Before: Owls sleep during daytime beneath the abandoned temple roof. 貓頭鷹白天睡在廢棄寺廟屋頂下。
- After: Owls usually sleep during the daytime inside old trees. 貓頭鷹通常白天在老樹裡睡覺。
- Why: 例句 during daytime 缺少冠詞不自然，且內容不符合日常情境，已改寫。

### Core 14 · orient

- Before: Migrating birds orient by the stars on clear nights. 候鳥在晴朗夜晚靠星星辨別方向。
- After: New students attend a meeting to orient themselves to school life. 新生參加說明會，以適應學校生活。
- Why: 最常見詞義應為「使適應；確定方位」，原主要詞義過於偏僻，例句也不貼近。

### Core 14 · gem

- Before: The tiny cafe is a hidden gem near the harbor. 那間小咖啡館是港口旁隱藏的珍寶。
- After: She wore a small blue gem on her silver ring. 她銀戒指上鑲著一顆藍色小寶石。
- Why: gem 最常見詞義是寶石，主要與補充詞義顛倒；詞性也不應含 vt。

### Core 10 · competing

- Before: Two competing teams practiced on the same field after school. 兩支相互競爭的隊伍放學後在同一個球場練習。
- After: Two competing explanations divided the family after the accident. 事故發生後，兩種互相衝突的說法讓家人各執一詞。
- Why: competing 最常見的意思是「競爭的」，「矛盾的」較次要，主副義應對調。

### Core 1 · fight

- Before: The fight ended before anyone was badly hurt. 在有人嚴重受傷前衝突就結束了。
- After: The fight ended before anyone was badly hurt. 在有人嚴重受傷前，這場打架就結束了。
- Why: fight 最常見的意思是「打架」，「衝突」不是主要義，例句翻譯也應配合。

### Core 1 · concern

- Before: Her main concern is safety on the stairs. 她主要擔心樓梯安全。
- After: Her main concern is safety on the stairs. 她最擔心的是樓梯上的安全。
- Why: 原中文翻譯漏掉 main 的語氣且不夠自然。

### Core 4 · capable

- Before: She is capable of repairing radios without instructions. 她有能力不用說明書修收音機。
- After: She is capable of repairing radios without instructions. 她有能力不看說明書就修好收音機。
- Why: exampleZh 譯文生硬，「不用說明書修收音機」不自然，也未譯出完成之意。

### Core 4 · invest

- Before: They invest in solar panels for the family farm. 他們出資為自家農場裝設太陽能板。
- After: They invest in solar panels for the family farm. 他們投資太陽能板，為自家農場所用。
- Why: 例句中譯「出資為…裝設」與原文 invest in 不符，改為更貼近原意的翻譯。

### Core 4 · lovely

- Before: Your grandmother has a lovely smile in old photographs. 舊照片裡，你奶奶的笑容很可愛。
- After: Your grandmother has a lovely smile in old photographs. 舊照片裡，你奶奶的笑容很迷人。
- Why: exampleZh 缺少「的」，語句不通順，不自然。

### Core 3 · broad

- Before: A broad river runs through the middle of our city. 一條寬闊的河流穿過我們城市的中心。
- After: A broad smile crossed her face after the call. 接完電話後她露出大大的笑容。
- Why: broad 作形容詞最常見的意思是「寬廣的」，「大大的」應放在補充義。

### Core 3 · block

- Before: Fallen branches block the shortcut behind the school today. 今天倒下的樹枝堵住了學校後方的捷徑。
- After: Fallen branches block the shortcut behind the school today. 今天倒下的樹枝擋住了學校後方的捷徑。
- Why: block 最常見的意思是名詞「街區」，且例句中譯漏掉「的」讀來不順。

### Core 3 · youth

- Before: Youth gave him courage to cross the ocean alone. 正因為年輕，他才有勇氣獨自橫渡大海。
- After: Youth gave him courage to cross the ocean alone. 青春賦予他獨自橫渡大海的勇氣。
- Why: youth 名詞主要義為「青春」，「年輕」是形容詞語感；補充義也需修正。

### Core 3 · spread

- Before: The flu spread quickly through the crowded dormitory overnight. 流感一夜間在擁擠宿舍裡傳開。
- After: The flu spread quickly through the crowded dormitory overnight. 流感一夜之間在擁擠的宿舍裡傳開。
- Why: 「傳開」較口語，主要義應為「傳播」；譯文也略作修順。

### Core 12 · disapproval

- Before: Her disapproval showed when she pushed away the contract. 她推開合約時，反對之意很明顯。
- After: Her disapproval showed when she pushed away the contract. 她把合約推開時，明顯流露出不贊成的神情。
- Why: 例句中譯將 disapproval 譯成「反對之意」，語氣稍重，宜與詞義「不贊成」一致。

### Core 4 · instrument

- Before: The doctor cleaned each instrument before the small operation. 小手術前，醫師清潔每件器械。
- After: My sister plays an instrument in the school orchestra every Friday. 我姊姊每週五都在學校管弦樂團演奏一種樂器。
- Why: 最常見的意思是樂器，器械不是首要意思，例句也應配合。

### Core 6 · auction

- Before: The auction began with a cracked porcelain bowl. 拍賣從一只裂紋瓷碗開始。
- After: The auction began with a cracked porcelain bowl. 拍賣從一只裂紋陶瓷碗開始。
- Why: 「瓷」為簡體用字，台灣用「陶瓷」，已改為繁體並更自然。

### Core 6 · plunge

- Before: Tourists watched salmon plunge over the low waterfall. 遊客看著鮭魚躍下低矮瀑布。
- After: Tourists watched salmon plunge over the low waterfall. 遊客看著鮭魚衝下低矮的瀑布。
- Why: plunge 最常見義為「跳入；猛然落下」，「躍下」不是最常見義，例句也不自然。

### Core 11 · shattered

- Before: She looked shattered after the overnight bus ride. 搭完夜車後，她看起來極度疲憊。
- After: The shattered window glass covered the classroom floor. 破碎的窗玻璃撒滿了教室地板。
- Why: 最常見的意思是「破碎的」，「極度疲憊」應放在補充義。

### Core 11 · bog

- Before: Wheels bog down when trucks cross the wet field. 卡車穿越濕田時，車輪會陷入泥沼。
- After: The hikers got stuck in a muddy bog near the lake. 登山客困在湖邊一處泥濘的沼澤裡。
- Why: 詞性以名詞為首，最常用意思是「沼澤」，例句也應配合。

### Core 11 · brew

- Before: Trouble began to brew after the ferry tickets sold out. 渡輪票售完後，麻煩開始醞釀。
- After: My father likes to brew tea every morning before work. 我爸爸每天早上上班前都喜歡泡茶。
- Why: 最常見的意思是「釀造／沖泡」，「醞釀」較少用，應調整主要詞義與例句。

### Core 2 · civil

- Before: Students learned about civil rights in history class today. 學生們今天在歷史課上認識了公民權利。
- After: Civil rights protect every citizen under the law. 公民權利在法律下保障每一位公民。
- Why: civil 最常見的意思是「公民的」，「有禮貌的」較少見，應調整主要詞義與例句。

### Core 8 · wealthy

- Before: A wealthy collector bought the cracked vase anyway. 一位富有收藏家仍買下那只裂花瓶。
- After: A wealthy collector bought the cracked vase anyway. 一位富有的收藏家還是買下了那只裂花瓶。
- Why: 補充義「豐富的」不常見且易誤導；中文例句漏「的」且不自然。

### Core 2 · will

- Before: Her will kept the family shop open through hard winters. 她的意志讓家裡店鋪撐過艱難冬季。
- After: I will call you after the baseball game tonight. 今晚棒球比賽結束後我會打電話給你。
- Why: will 最常見用法為助動詞「將會」，主要詞義應調整，並改用相符例句。

### Core 2 · lot

- Before: A lot of steam rose from the bamboo baskets. 許多蒸氣從竹籠裡冒出來。
- After: We saw a lot of stars on the mountain last night. 昨晚我們在山上看見了很多星星。
- Why: 補充詞義「一堆」重複且「份額」不恰當；詞性標示也不宜含動詞。

### Core 9 · constituent

- Before: One constituent complained about broken streetlights near the park. 一位選民抱怨公園旁路燈壞了。
- After: Salt is a key constituent of seawater and many snacks. 鹽是海水和許多零食的重要成分。
- Why: constituent 最常見意思是「成分」，「選民」不是最常用義，應調整主副義。

### Core 9 · wagon

- Before: The old wagon carried pumpkins from the field. 舊農用車把南瓜從田裡載出來。
- After: The old wagon carried pumpkins from the field. 舊馬車把南瓜從田裡載出來。
- Why: wagon 主要意思是馬車，「四輪車」太籠統；例句翻譯也應與詞義一致。

### Core 9 · uncover

- Before: Workers uncover old tiles by lifting the station floor. 工人掀開車站地板露出舊瓷磚。
- After: Workers uncover old tiles by lifting the station floor. 工人掀開車站地板，露出了舊瓷磚。
- Why: uncover 最常見意思是揭露或發現，主義應調整。

### Core 6 · gesture

- Before: Her small gesture of pouring tea softened his anger. 她倒茶的小舉動緩和了他的怒氣。
- After: He made a quiet gesture to tell us to stop talking. 他比了個安靜的手勢，要我們別再講話。
- Why: gesture 最常見的意思是手勢，「舉動」較不常見，例句也需配合。

### Core 6 · dominant

- Before: The dominant scent in the shop was roasted sesame. 店裡壓過其他味道的，是烘烤芝麻的香氣。
- After: Pop music is the dominant style among students at our school. 流行音樂是我們學校學生之間最盛行的風格。
- Why: dominant 最常見的意思是占優勢或主導的，「最明顯的」不是主要詞義。

### Core 6 · treasury

- Before: After years of war, the kingdom's treasury had almost no gold left. 經過多年戰爭，王國的國庫幾乎沒有黃金了。
- After: The treasury ran short of money after the long war. 長期戰爭過後，國庫的資金不足了。
- Why: treasury 最常用的意思是國庫，寶庫較少見，故調整主要詞義與例句。

### Core 6 · beaten

- Before: The beaten path led behind the tea warehouse. 被踩平的小路通到茶倉後方。
- After: Our team felt beaten after losing the final game by ten points. 我們隊伍以十分之差輸掉決賽，大家覺得被打敗了。
- Why: beaten 作形容詞最常見的意思是被打敗的，被踩平的屬於特定搭配。

### Core 10 · startle

- Before: The temple bell can startle pigeons from the roof. 廟鐘一響，屋頂上的鴿子就會嚇得飛走。
- After: The loud temple bell can startle pigeons on the roof. 響亮的廟鐘聲可能會嚇到屋頂上的鴿子。
- Why: 「驚起」不是最常見的詞義，應為「使受驚嚇」，例句中文也需改得自然。

### Core 10 · sandy

- Before: His sandy shoes left prints across the clean floor. 他沾沙的鞋在乾淨地板上留下腳印。
- After: His sandy shoes left prints across the clean floor. 他沾滿沙子的鞋在乾淨的地板上留下腳印。
- Why: 「沾沙的」不是常見詞義，主要詞義應為「沙質的」，補充詞義也需調整。

### Core 10 · minus

- Before: Dinner cost forty dollars minus the coupon discount. 晚餐四十美元再扣掉折價券優惠。
- After: Dinner cost forty dollars minus the coupon discount. 晚餐花了四十美元，扣掉折價券優惠後的金額。
- Why: 「扣掉」不夠標準，常見詞義為「減去」，例句中文也有不通順之處。

### Core 11 · obituary

- Before: The obituary described her years rescuing injured workers. 訃告提到她多年來救助受傷工人的事蹟。
- After: The obituary described her years rescuing injured workers. 訃聞提到她多年來救助受傷工人的事蹟。
- Why: 詞性應以名詞為主，「訃告」不如台灣常用的「訃聞」自然

### Core 11 · armour

- Before: Rusty armour lined the castle wall near the stairs. 生鏽甲冑排列在樓梯旁的城堡牆邊。
- After: Rusty armour lined the castle wall near the stairs. 生鏽的盔甲排列在樓梯旁的城堡牆邊。
- Why: 「甲冑」過於文言，應用「盔甲」，例句翻譯也不通順

### Core 11 · psychiatry

- Before: Psychiatry helped him name the fear he had carried. 精神病學幫他說出一直承受的恐懼。
- After: Psychiatry helped him name the fear he had carried. 精神醫學幫他說出一直承受的恐懼。
- Why: 譯文用「精神病學」與詞條「精神醫學」不一致

### Core 9 · directory

- Before: The school directory lists every teacher's office and phone number. 學校名錄列出每位老師的辦公室和電話。
- After: The hotel directory listed a nearby dentist in the downtown area. 飯店的名錄列出了市中心附近的一間牙醫診所。
- Why: directory 最常見意思是名錄或目錄，「指南」不是最常見義，宜調整。

### Core 9 · mansion

- Before: The old mansion overlooked the tea fields below. 從那棟老宅可以俯瞰整片茶園。
- After: The old mansion overlooked the tea fields below. 那棟老豪宅俯瞰著下方的茶園。
- Why: mansion 最常見意思為豪宅；原例句翻譯漏了 mansion 的「豪華」意涵且語序不自然。

### Core 9 · enact

- Before: Lawmakers enact stricter rules for factory waste after protests. 抗議後立法者制定更嚴格的工廠廢棄物規則。
- After: Lawmakers voted to enact stricter rules for factory waste after the protests. 抗議之後，立法者投票通過制定更嚴格的工廠廢棄物規定。
- Why: 原例句時態不自然（抗議後應用過去式），且補充義缺少常見的「頒布」。

### Core 2 · accord

- Before: The fishermen signed an accord about sharing the small pier. 漁民簽署協議共同使用小碼頭。
- After: The fishermen signed an accord to share the small pier. 漁民簽署了協議，同意共用這座小碼頭。
- Why: accord 最常見意思為「一致」；例句 about 搭配稍不自然，中文也宜更通順。

### Core 13 · scarf

- Before: Mina wrapped a red scarf around the cracked statue. 米娜把紅圍巾繞在裂開的雕像上。
- After: Mina wrapped a red scarf around her neck before leaving the house. 米娜出門前把一條紅圍巾圍在脖子上。
- Why: 例句用在雕像上不符日常情境；補充義缺少常見的動詞義「狼吞虎嚥」。

### Core 13 · compartment

- Before: She hid the passport in a secret suitcase compartment. 她把護照藏在行李箱的暗格裡。
- After: She hid the passport in a secret compartment of her suitcase. 她把護照藏在行李箱的一個暗格裡。
- Why: 補充義「車廂包廂」不通順；例句 suitcase compartment 稍不自然。

### Core 13 · ensemble

- Before: Her linen ensemble looked crisp at the courthouse ceremony. 她的亞麻全套服裝在法院典禮上很俐落。
